Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348)
*Yaxin Luo,Jiacheng Cui,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Liu,Xinyue Bi,Zhaoyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LLMSurgeon的框架，利用模型生成的文本作为输入，通过解决标签偏移逆问题，实现了在无需访问原始训练数据的情况下，对大语言模型预训练数据组成（数字DNA）进行高保真审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的预训练数据（数字DNA）是决定其能力的关键，但往往是未公开的封闭黑盒，导致模型行为审计和溯源极为困难。因此需要一种后验（post-hoc）的审计方法来分析数据组成。

Method: 提出“LLMSurgeon”框架，将数据混合重构建模为标签偏移（label-shift）下的反问题。通过估计校准的“软”混淆矩阵（soft confusion matrix），并解决约束优化问题，以克服模型在分类过程中产生的系统性偏差，从而还原预训练数据的潜在混合分布。

Result: 引入了LLMScan评估基准，实验证明LLMSurgeon能够在给定已知训练混合比例的模型上，高精度推断并恢复其对应的领域数据分布。

Conclusion: “Data Mixture Surgery”是一种有效的模型审计手段，能够在无法获取原始训练数据的情况下，通过分析模型生成的文本，高保真地重构其预训练数据的领域分布。

Abstract: The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [IORM: Hierarchical I/O Governance for Thousands of Consolidated Databases on Oracle Exadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29006)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Zakaria Alrmaih,Chenhao Guo,Anubhav Singh,Sue Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oracle Exadata的存储侧I/O资源管理器（IORM），通过引入语义标记、层级化配置和统一存储治理机制，有效解决了大规模数据库多租户场景下的资源竞争与性能隔离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle Exadata实现了高密度的多租户数据库整合，传统存储块级调度器缺乏对数据库语义和租户边界的感知，无法解决多租户环境下的“吵闹邻居”干扰问题，导致性能不可控。

Method: 1. I/O标记（I/O Tagging）：将数据库内核的语义上下文传递至存储调度器；2. 层级资源配置（Hierarchical Resource Profiles）：利用份额（shares）和限制（limits）表达跨租户层级的组合分配策略；3. 统一存储治理（Unified Storage Governance）：在持久内存、闪存和硬盘等全存储栈中执行策略，包括缓存放置决策。

Result: 在生产环境评估中，IORM显著提升了延迟一致性，消除长尾延迟异常，并在混合负载下将平均读取延迟提升了数倍，各层级资源限制能够精确执行，即使在需求高度偏态的情况下也能保持良好的比例分配。

Conclusion: IORM成功解决了Oracle Exadata多租户环境下复杂的I/O调度难题，通过在存储层实现细粒度的语义感知和层级化资源管控，确保了高密度部署下的性能隔离与QoS稳定性。

Abstract: Oracle Exadata consolidates thousands of tenant databases onto shared storage infrastructure deployed at hundreds of customer sites worldwide. Oracle Multitenant architecture enables this extreme density, with thousands of tenant databases sharing a single Exadata storage system -- but this creates a multi-level resource hierarchy (container databases, tenant databases, and workloads within tenants) that commodity block-layer schedulers cannot govern, as they lack visibility into database semantics and tenant boundaries. This paper presents the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a storage-side scheduler built on three mechanisms: I/O Tagging, which propagates semantic context from the database kernel to the storage scheduler; Hierarchical Resource Profiles, which express compositional allocation policies across consolidation tiers using shares and limits; and Unified Storage Governance, which applies these policies consistently across all tiers of the storage hierarchy -- persistent memory, flash, and hard disk -- including cache placement decisions. IORM enables successful cloud deployments where thousands of tenants coexist on shared storage: production OLTP workloads run alongside concurrent analytical workloads from the same or different databases without noisy-neighbor interference. Evaluation on production Exadata systems demonstrates that IORM dramatically improves latency consistency, virtually eliminating tail latency outliers and delivering several-fold improvements in average read latency under mixed workloads. Hierarchical limits compose correctly across all three levels, and proportional share allocation tracks configured ratios closely even under highly skewed demand.

</details>


### [114] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin 是一款新型框架，它通过提取并隐私化 Parquet 文件的物理元数据，在保护敏感数据的前提下，帮助开发者高精度地重现影响性能的查询数据分布与剪枝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云数据平台中，由于隐私限制无法获取用户原始数据，导致开发者难以复现特定租户数据集上的性能回归问题；现有的合成数据方案多关注统计分布，忽略了影响查询执行引擎（如行组剪枝 pruning）的关键物理属性。

Method: 提出 ScanTwin 框架：从 Parquet 文件的页脚（footer）提取每行组（row-group）的草图信息（Sketch），包括边界值（boundary values）和压缩大小（compressed sizes），并采用边界参数化的差分隐私（$\varepsilon$-differential privacy）机制进行发布。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和 SSB 数据集上，$\varepsilon=\infty$ 时 pruning 误差为 0%，字节误差小于 1%；在 $\varepsilon=5$ 的强隐私保护下，高选择性查询的剪枝误差低于 8.5%，性能追踪与原始数据高度一致。

Conclusion: ScanTwin 成功在保护数据隐私的前提下，通过对 Parquet 文件的物理元数据（行组边界与压缩大小）进行建模，解决了因数据敏感性导致的云数据平台性能回归复现难题。该方法在保证高精度的同时，提供了差分隐私保护。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [115] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对多核服务器下 Shuffle 操作扩展性差的问题，提出了一种 Ring-buffer 流式 Shuffle 设计，通过无锁原子操作实现高效的跨线程数据重分布，显著提升了现代多核处理器的查询引擎性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务器 CPU 核心数增加至数百个，现有的两种 Shuffle 设计（批量分区和通道流式传输）均存在局限性：批量分区存在全局同步瓶颈及缓存局部性差的问题；通道流式传输则遭受高额的通道间同步开销。这些问题阻碍了现代并行查询引擎在多核硬件上的高效扩展。

Method: 提出了一种基于环形缓冲区（Ring-buffer）的流式 Shuffle 设计，采用无锁原子槽位获取技术，针对固定大小的批处理组进行操作。实现了按摊销 O(1) 的同步开销和独立于输入内存大小的 O(M) 内存占用。

Result: 在 72 核 NVIDIA GraceHopper、192 核 AWS Graviton4 及 96 核 AMD EPYC 系统上进行了对比验证。在 192 核系统中，该方案比流式通道方案性能高出 100% 以上，比批量分区方案高出 300% 以上。真实工作负载（TPC-H 和 ClickBench）测试表明其性能优势具有场景依赖性。

Conclusion: Ring-buffer shuffle 设计在多数高核数场景下显著优于传统的批量分区和通道流式传输，但在具有多个 L3 缓存的小芯片（chiplet）架构中，由于共享原子计数器可能成为瓶颈，传统方法仍具有竞争力。其性能优势高度依赖于工作负载特性。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Wenxin Xu,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现有 Text-to-Vis 模型“单次生成”模式导致的认知负担和高错误率，本文提出了渐进式多轮交互范式 PMVis、配套数据集 PMVisBench 以及具备自我修复能力的智能体框架 PMVisAgent，显著提升了复杂可视化任务的生成准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Text-to-Vis 方法大多采用“单次生成（one-shot）”模式，要求用户在单一回合内列出所有可视化细节，这不仅造成用户认知负担，也容易导致生成错误。

Method: 1. 提出了 PMVis 渐进式多轮交互范式；2. 构建了首个支持渐进式查询的基准数据集 PMVisBench；3. 开发了基于智能体的框架 PMVisAgent，包含模拟用户、系统及具有验证与自我修复功能的 Validation Agent，利用 ReAct 模式进行循环验证与修复。

Result: 在 PMVisBench 上进行的大量实验表明，PMVisAgent 显著优于现有基准模型，在单表和多表设置下，执行准确率分别提升了 17.57% 和 23.21%。消融实验验证了渐进式交互与 clarify 机制结合的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过引入渐进式多轮交互与智能体验证机制，PMVis 有效解决了现有 Text-to-Vis 方法在复杂任务中因指令过载导致的错误，在单表和多表数据集上均取得了显著的性能提升，证明了该范式在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>


### [117] [Zero-Scan Data Quality: Leveraging Table Format Metadata for Continuous Observability at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30308)
*Mohit Verma,Shantanu Rawat,Christian Bush,Sumedh Sakdeo,Lokesh Amarnath Ravindranathan,Dwarak Bakshi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用Apache Iceberg元数据进行“元数据优先”的数据质量监控方法，通过在写入时记录统计信息和增量式草图（sketches），在显著降低计算成本的同时，实现了对生产环境中大规模数据的高效质量监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格式（如Iceberg）的现有元数据统计与数据质量（DQ）监控需求高度重合，但目前的利用率偏低，且传统的全量数据扫描进行监测成本昂贵。

Method: 利用Apache Iceberg的元数据（写入时统计信息），结合轻量级计数器和可增量合并的草图算法（Theta sketches, KLL sketches），在无需扫描原始数据的情况下实现数据质量监控。

Result: 在LinkedIn规模（20万+表/800+ PB）下的部署显示，该方法在零边际计算成本下满足了约60%的DQ规则，并将分析资源消耗降低了约50%；通过引入草图算法，DQ规则覆盖率可进一步提升至90%。

Conclusion: 通过在Puffin sidecar文件中存储基于草图（sketches）的元数据，可以在不增加数据扫描开销的情况下，实现更高覆盖率的自动化数据质量（DQ）监控。建议将此模式作为现代表格式的标准。

Abstract: Modern table formats such as Apache Iceberg compute and store metadata-commit timestamps, record counts, and column-level statistics such as null counts and value bounds at write time as part of file writing. These statistics serve query planning, yet they overlap substantially with data quality (DQ) monitoring needs. We describe a metadata-first approach that repurposes write-time statistics for continuous DQ observability: anomaly detection, drift monitoring, null-rate tracking; without scanning any data. Deployed at LinkedIn across 200,000+ Iceberg tables (800+ PB), this approach satisfies approximately 60% of user-defined DQ rules at zero marginal compute cost and reduces profiling resource consumption by around 50%. Extending manifest statistics with lightweight counters (sum, zero-value counts, boolean counts) and incrementally mergeable sketches; Theta sketches for distinct counts, KLL sketches for quantiles; can further raise metadata-satisfiable coverage to close to 90% of production DQ rules. We validate sketch accuracy, mergeability, and storage overhead on production data and propose that table formats should store per-file sketches in Puffin sidecar files, following the same store-then-aggregate pattern used for existing manifest statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Human-in-the-Loop Swarms: A Bionic Swarm Approach to Real-World Soil Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29091)
*Petras Swissler,Mohammadali Rashidioun,Nicholas Sahu,Raaid Kabir,Ayodeji Aderibigbe,Oladoyin Kolawole*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人硬件开发门槛高的问题，本文提出了“Bionic Swarm”系统，利用人类作为移动节点进行人机协同，实现在真实环境中高效验证群体搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体和野外机器人硬件部署成本高、开发周期长，限制了算法在真实环境下的验证。

Method: 提出一种名为“Bionic Swarm”的系统，通过将机器人难以实现的感知与环境交互任务交给人类（人工代操），利用智能手机Web App、蓝牙传感器及中心化服务器实现算法调度与协同。此外，作者提出并测试了一种名为“Score-Biased-Search”的坐标搜索算法。

Result: 实验证明Score-Biased-Search算法在仿真中具有超越线性的地图重构能力，并成功通过“Bionic Swarm”平台在真实户外场景中进行了有效性验证。

Conclusion: 通过引入“Bionic Swarm”系统，验证了人机协同方案能有效降低群体及野外机器人研究的现实验证门槛，为算法在复杂真实环境中的部署提供了低成本、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Swarm and field robotics face significant barriers to real-world validation due to the high cost and development time to deploy hardware. This paper introduces the ``Bionic Swarm,'' a novel system that lowers these barriers by abstracting away many of the tasks that are difficult to implement on robots but which do not contribute to the overall algorithm evaluation, giving these tasks to human users. These human users take directions from a smartphone web-app that takes measurements from Bluetooth-connected sensors and relays them to a centralized server. This server runs the swarm algorithm and directs actions to the human users. We evaluate this system through the experimental validation of a geotechnically-focused search algorithm named Score-Biased-Search, which functions by assigning a ``score'' to each location on a reconstructed map, then biases search patterns through areas of higher expected scores, and which exhibits superlinear map reconstruction relative to the number of search agents. After presenting simulation results for the algorithm, we then apply the algorithm on the Bionic Swarm platform to validate its function in a real-world, outdoor setting. This work demonstrates that this human-in-the-loop approach significantly lowers the barrier to entry for field and swarm robotics research.

</details>


### [119] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC方案，在保持高性能控制的同时，解决了可微MPC计算耗时过长的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AC-MPC方法中，可微MPC层需要在正向和反向传播过程中反复求解优化问题，导致巨大的训练和推理延迟，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于CUDA加速的AC-MPC（演员-评论家模型预测控制）变体，通过对可微MPC层进行计算优化，减少了前向和反向传播中的计算开销。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞速任务的仿真验证中，该方法达到了行业领先的单圈成绩，展现了近乎极限的动态控制能力，并大幅度降低了训练与推理时间。

Conclusion: 通过CUDA加速技术，有效解决了AC-MPC中可微MPC层运行效率低下的瓶颈，在保持原有控制性能的同时，实现了显著的加速效果。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [120] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“动态对称性”概念，认为通过优化机器人质心加速度的均匀性（动态各向同性），可显著提升机器人的敏捷性、健壮性与多功能性，并凭借Argus系列机器人验证了该设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人设计主要局限于几何对称性，忽视了动力学层面的对称设计。作者旨在探索能否通过提升运动能力的动态均匀性（即动力学对称性）来增强机器人的综合性能。

Method: 1. 提出了“动态对称性”概念，并将其形式化为“动态各向同性度”（dynamic isotropy）；2. 通过超过1000种模拟形态进行大规模实验验证；3. 开发了Argus系列球形机器人，采用径向线性驱动器直接控制质心动力学，并制造了20条腿的物理原型进行实地测试。

Result: 模拟结果显示，高动态对称性能显著提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性及能量效率。物理原型Argus实现了接近极限的动态各向同性，在复杂地形中展现了方位无关的运动能力、快速自稳定及抗故障性能。

Conclusion: 在设计机器人时，将对称性从几何形态扩展到动力学层面的“动态对称性”（通过动态各向同性度衡量）是提升机器人敏捷性、健壮性和多功能性的通用且有效的途径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [121] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo 是一种通过单臂机器人与人类协作收集数据，并利用增强技术合成双臂操作演示，从而实现双臂机器人策略高效训练和零样本部署的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人硬件与相关数据集匮乏限制了双臂操作任务的学习，而单臂机器人资源相对丰富，亟需一种高效的方法将单臂操作经验转移至双臂系统。

Method: 提出 MonoDuo 框架。通过单臂机器人与人类协作交替执行任务来收集数据，结合手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割与修复技术，将单臂交互数据增强为合成的双臂操作演示，最后用于训练双臂机器人策略。

Result: 在 5 项复杂操作任务中，MonoDuo 实现了针对未知双臂配置的零样本部署（最高 70% 成功率），且仅需少量（25 条）目标机器人数据微调即可将成功率显著提升 65%-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo 成功验证了利用单臂机器人演示数据训练双臂机器人策略的可行性与高效性，证明了通过人机协作和数据增强技术可以弥补双臂机器人数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [122] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 发布：在保持经典采样式运动规划算法优势的同时，新增硬件加速以支持实时规划，并深度优化了与 AI 研究工作流的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了适应近二十年来运动规划领域算法的快速演进（如渐进最优、懒惰计算、受限规划等），并应对当前实时规划需求及与 AI 技术深度结合的挑战。

Method: 在 OMPL 既有采样式运动规划算法架构的基础上，引入硬件加速技术，并对库的接口进行升级，以 seamless 地集成到现代 AI 研究工作流中。

Result: 推出了 OMPL 2.0，显著提升了库在实时规划场景下的性能，并优化了其兼容现代 AI 研究生态的能力。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0 代表了该库的一次重大演进，旨在通过硬件加速提升实时性能，并与现代 AI 工作流集成，展现了运动规划领域从传统算法向现代计算范式的发展趋势。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [123] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相位条件感知与力觉反馈相结合的机器人操作框架，通过FiLM调制解决策略中的状态混淆，并利用多模态感知触发自主故障恢复，显著提升了变形物体操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推断时假设马尔可夫性，导致在视觉相似但动作要求不同的场景下产生状态混淆（State Aliasing），且缺乏对操作故障的自主恢复能力。

Method: 提出了一种闭环分层架构，包含：1. FiLM（特征线性调制）调节的ACT编码器，用于相位特异性策略；2. 基于视觉、力和位姿的多模态相位预测器，用于实时状态监测与故障恢复；3. 混合阻抗控制器和触觉遥操作接口，用于柔顺执行与数据采集。

Result: 在双臂挂衣和脱衣任务中，挂衣成功率从56%提升至87%；消融实验证明FiLM调节优于未调节或Token级调节基准，t-SNE分析显示FiLM生成了区分度良好的相位特异性特征表示。

Conclusion: 通过引入相位条件调制和力觉感知反馈，该分层框架有效地解决了变形物体操作中的状态混淆问题，并显著提升了长周期复杂任务的成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [124] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于被动磁吸附的小型无人机空中对接平台，通过进度感知监督策略协调双机通信与控制，成功解决了小型无人机在有限载荷下进行可靠对接的难题，并提供了一个可量化的基准测试环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机进行空中对接对于实现模块化空中协作与操控至关重要，但受限于推力和载荷，难以实现精准的相对位姿控制和可重复的对接操作。

Method: 采用基于“领航者-跟随者”模式的双无人机系统，结合被动磁吸附对接结构，并设计了包含“接近、对齐、捕获、稳定”四个阶段的“进度感知任务监督器（progress-aware mission supervisor）”进行控制。实验基于ROS 2框架，在仿真与真实环境中对对接成功率、时间及误差等指标进行量化评估。

Result: 成功实现了轻量化模块化对接架构，平台在实验中展现了良好的对接性能，并提供了详细的量化指标（如对接成功率、时间及失败模式统计），验证了该监督策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提供一个集成化的软硬件测试平台，验证了小型无人机在中等载荷和推力约束下进行可靠空中对接的可行性，为模块化空中协作提供了可重复、可度量的实验工具。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [125] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA 是一个即插即用的框架，通过多视图几何约束、实例 Token 和掩码自监督机制，解决了 VLA 模型在 3D 空间理解、实例感知及遮挡处理上的不足，提升了机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型缺乏 3D 场景理解能力，导致空间定位弱、实例认知不足以及在遮挡情况下推理脆弱，且现有的 3D 感知方法难以融入 VLA。

Method: 提出了三个核心模块：(1) 基于多视图一致性约束和空间条件几何聚合的 3D 特征编码；(2) 用于 3D 实例感知的实例评估模块；(3) 利用掩码自监督 3D 分支进行视觉 Token 补全以应对遮挡挑战。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 基准测试中，与多个 VLA 基线对比，性能均获得一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA 作为一个即插即用的框架，能够有效地为预训练的 VLA 模型注入鲁棒的 3D 推理能力，无需额外的标注，且显著提升了机器人在复杂场景下的操纵性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [126] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA 是一个插件式动态调度框架，通过根据任务阶段调节计算资源的投入（重利用或全计算），在不改变基础模型的前提下，显著提升了 VLA 机器人的推理效率和控制频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型计算成本高、控制频率低，且现有优化方法多为静态，忽略了具身控制中不同步骤对计算需求的不均匀性。受人类运动控制启发，模型应根据任务阶段的敏感度动态调整计算资源。

Method: 提出一个插件式阶段自适应推理框架（ElegantVLA），包含一个轻量级调度器，根据时间表征相似度、机器人运动线索和任务进度，动态分配视觉编码器、LLM 和动作头的计算资源：1. 视觉-语言感知端采用五级计算模式，实现从全量重算到多步时间重用的调度；2. 动作生成端采用三级去噪模式，在稳定运动阶段重用中间去噪状态。

Result: 在 GR00T 和 CogACT 基准测试中分别实现了最高 2.55 倍和 3.77 倍的加速；在六项真实世界 GR00T 任务中，计算量减少 2.18 倍，控制频率从 13.8 Hz 提升至 26.3 Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA 证明了通过动态计算调度，无需修改或重新训练基础模型，即可在保持性能的同时显著提升 VLA 模型的实时推理速度和控制频率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [127] [VLA-Pro: Cross-Task Procedural Memory Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29562)
*Shengyu Si,Yuanzhuo Lu,Ruimeng Yang,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Pro是一个即插即用的VLA框架，通过在训练中构建LoRA程序性记忆库，并在推理时动态检索与融合这些记忆，从而显著增强了机器人模型在未知任务中的跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对未见过的机器人操作任务时，难以有效地在不同物体、场景和动作模式间迁移经验。

Method: 提出VLA-Pro框架，在训练阶段将特定任务的LoRA适配器作为“程序性记忆”存储；在推理阶段，根据多模态上下文检索相关记忆，并进行动态融合以生成动作序列。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RLBench及真实世界场景中表现优异；在仿真环境中实现高达207%的相对提升，真实世界操作成功率由5.8%显著提升至65.0%。

Conclusion: 通过程序性记忆检索和动态融合机制，VLA-Pro为VLA模型的跨任务泛化提供了一种高效、模块化且稳定的方案，有效解决了机器人模型在面对未见过任务时的迁移难题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet they still struggle to generalize to unseen tasks that necessitate transferring relevant experience across objects, scenes, and action patterns. This paper proposes VLA-Pro, a plug-and-play framework designed to enhance cross-task generalization by storing task-relevant procedural memories at training time and transferring these memories during inference. Specifically, VLA-Pro stores task-specific LoRA adapters as parameterized procedural memories during training. At inference time, VLA-Pro retrieves relevant procedural memories based on the current multi-modal context and dynamically fuses these memories for generating the current action chunk. Experiments on RoboTwin, RLBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show that VLA-Pro consistently improves cross-task generalization across multiple backbones, achieving up to a 207% relative improvement in simulation and increasing real-world success rate from 5.8% to 65.0%. These results suggest that procedural memory retrieval and adaptation provide an effective mechanism for transferring manipulation experience to novel tasks while preserving modularity and execution stability.

</details>


### [128] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种真实世界下的强化学习蒸馏框架，将视觉策略的知识通过“师生蒸馏”转移给感知策略，实现了无需仿真、高鲁棒性且具备泛化能力的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的机器人操作任务中，视觉信息虽能加速强化学习，但易导致模型过拟合，缺乏鲁棒性和迁移能力；现有的解决方案（如域随机化）往往依赖复杂的仿真环境。

Method: 提出了一种人机协作（human-in-the-loop）的强化学习框架，采用师生蒸馏（teacher-student distillation）技术：训练一个带视觉的“教师”策略，将其知识蒸馏至一个仅依赖本体感知（位姿、扭矩、受力状态）的“学生”策略中。全程在真实物理环境中进行训练，且不依赖域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在NIST组装基准测试中，经过约50分钟的训练，在3个代表性任务上达到95%的总体成功率；在8个未见过的场景中表现出强大的泛化能力；通过蒸馏微调解决了最高难度任务，性能优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过人机协作和师生蒸馏，可以有效地将视觉信息转化为鲁棒的视觉-感知策略，从而在无需复杂仿真或大量数据增强的情况下，实现真实世界机器人操作任务的快速学习与高泛化性。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [129] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的触觉感知计算框架，揭示了热感和顺应性在人类材料辨识中的核心作用，为提升机器人和人工触觉系统的感知能力提供了设计指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于人类触觉感知中低级触觉信号如何转化为高级感知表征的机制尚不明确，制约了触觉技术在数字环境中的应用及人型机器人触觉感知能力的发展。

Method: 提出了一种可解释的计算框架，包含三个互联模型：模型1（将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感觉属性）、模型2（基于感知表征进行材料分类）、模型3（直接从触觉特征进行材料分类）。该框架通过整合按压、静态接触和滑动交互的多传感器触觉数据进行分析。

Result: 研究表明，结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互的信息能显著提高预测准确性。此外，热感应信号在心理物理属性建模和材料识别任务中均表现出极高的信息价值。

Conclusion: 研究证实了热感和顺应性（compliance）对人类触觉感知的关键作用。由于现有机器人触觉传感器和触觉显示器对这些特征的利用不足，未来的系统设计应重点整合这些模态，以提升人工系统的人类感知逼真度及地面化界面设计。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [130] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对越野环境下语义分割面临的分布偏移问题，本文提出了ST-Seg框架。通过风格扩展（SE）增加域覆盖范围，并结合纹理正则化（TR）稳定特征表示，有效提升了复杂地形下的感知鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化（越野）环境下，源-目标域差异和粗糙地形导致的传感器噪声造成了显著的数据分布偏移，导致语义分割失效，影响自动导航安全。

Method: ST-Seg框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 风格扩展（SE）：通过生成多样化的真实风格来扩大源域分布；2. 纹理正则化（TR）：利用深度纹理流形来稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多个存在分布偏移的目标域上进行了实验，结果表明ST-Seg显著优于现有方法，提升了语义分割在越野导航中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg是一个有效的域泛化语义分割框架，通过风格扩展和纹理正则化显著提高了非结构化（越野）环境下感知模型的鲁棒性和真实场景适用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [131] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAConf 是一种针对视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型的高效置信度评估框架，通过单次前向推理即可对连续动作任务进行置信度估算，有效解决了传统方法计算开销大及无法处理连续动作空间的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度评估方法（如集成法或基于动作Token概率的方法）存在推理效率低下（需重复采样）以及难以推广到连续动作空间的局限性，限制了机器人在开放世界中进行可靠的决策。

Method: 提出了一种名为 VLAConf 的一类判别式置信度框架，通过轻量级置信度头直接对冻结的 VLA 内部特征进行单次前向推理来预测异常分数，并引入步长条件建模（step-conditioned modeling）以编码轨迹信息。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，VLAConf 显著提升了置信度信号的质量，在推理效率和预测准确性上均大幅优于现有基线，并在真实机器人实验中验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: VLAConf 通过利用冻结的 VLA 预训练表示，在保持高推理效率的同时，有效提升了 VLA 模型在连续动作空间中的置信度评估能力，且具备良好的跨架构通用性。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [132] [EXACT-MPPI: Exact Signed-Distance Navigation for Arbitrary-Footprint Robots from Point Clouds via Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29663)
*Chen Peng,Zhikang Ge,Wenwu Lu,Haiming Gao,Stavros Vougioukas,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EXACT-MPPI 是一种无需训练的局部导航框架，通过在 MPPI 控制器中直接使用精确的机器人足迹几何计算（而非简化模型），实现了高性能且通用的复杂形状机器人避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航规划器通常简化机器人足迹（将其处理为凸形或膨胀体），在处理复杂、非凸形状的负载时会导致可行运动被误判为不可行。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EXACT-MPPI 的导航框架，该框架跳过中间地图表示（如占用网格），直接将局部点云与稀疏引导映射为动作；其核心是将解析的、精确的符号距离评估器嵌入到模型预测路径积分 (MPPI) 控制器中，并利用 JAX 进行 GPU 并行计算。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在处理复杂足迹时的计算速度超过了基于学习的基线方法，能够保持在凸形足迹规划器会失败场景下的运动可行性，且对于多种差速、阿克曼等平台具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: EXACT-MPPI 证明了结合精确足迹几何与基于采样的预测控制，是一种实现多平台、高性能且无需训练的机器人局部导航的实用方案。

Abstract: Ground robots often carry payloads, implements, or other attachments that turn their effective footprint into complex, non-convex shapes. Navigating safely through clutter then requires reasoning about this true geometry, yet most local planners simplify it with convex or inflated proxies and rasterize sensor data into occupancy grids or distance fields. Both choices eliminate feasible motions when clearance is comparable to the footprint geometry. We present EXACT-MPPI, a training-free local navigation framework that maps local point-cloud observations and sparse guidance directly to motion commands, without any intermediate map representation. The framework embeds an analytic, exact signed-distance evaluator into a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The footprint is represented as a simple polygon for general convex or concave planar shapes, with a rectangle-cover specialization for faster evaluation of rectilinear footprints, enabling footprint-aware collision costs without convex decomposition, inflation, or learned encoders. During each MPPI rollout, observed obstacle points are transformed into the predicted body frame and evaluated against the footprint. All operations are batched in JAX, leveraging GPU parallelism for real-time receding-horizon control. Experiments show that EXACT-MPPI accelerates batched distance evaluation over a learned point-to-robot baseline, preserves feasible motion where convex-footprint planners fail, and remains robust under dense static and moving obstacles. The same framework deploys on differential-drive, Ackermann, omnidirectional, and hybrid-mode platforms by changing only the footprint description and motion model without per-platform training. Pairing exact footprint geometry with sampling-based predictive control thus offers a practical, training-free path to footprint-aware local navigation across diverse robots.

</details>


### [133] [FLIP: Real-Time and Resilient Formation Planning for Large-Scale DIstributed Swarms via Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29704)
*Yuan Zhou,Guangtong Xu,Zhenyu Hou,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空点云配准（PCR）的大规模无人机集群分布式队形规划方法，有效解决了大规模协作中的计算瓶颈与鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模队形规划方法要么过度简化队形表示导致性能下降，要么采用完全协作关系导致计算负载过大。

Method: 将最优队形位置序列（OFPS）计算转化为时空点云配准（PCR）问题；采用带离群值剔除的PCR算法实现大规模位置匹配；并基于OFPS进行协作式轨迹优化。

Result: 在120架无人机的仿真实验中表现优异，并在鲁棒性和计算效率上显著优于现有主流（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将队形规划问题转化为点云配准问题，成功解决了大规模集群形成规划带来的计算负载与性能折中难题，实现了鲁棒、高效且分布式的队形轨迹规划。

Abstract: Traditional large-scale formation planning either oversimplify the formation representation which leads to poor performance, or they employ complete collaborative relationships, which results in excessive computational load. To achieve high-performance and large-scale formation planning, we transform the Optimal Formation Position Sequence \cite{c1} (OFPS) calculation problem into a spatiotemporal Point Cloud Registration (PCR) problem. Each agent derives its OFPS by distributively computing the matching result between current positions and the desired formation positions of all other agents. Then each agent optimizes the cooperative formation trajectory by using OFPS. We leverage the PCR method with outlier rejection to rapidly perform large-scale formation position registration. This prevents suboptimal trajectories and failed agents from propagating through the cooperative network and affecting more agents. Consequently, we uniformly achieve resilient, efficient, and distributed trajectory planning for large-scale swarms. The effectiveness and the superiority of the proposed method are demonstrated through large-scale simulations of 120-drone formation, and rigorous benchmarking against state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods.

</details>


### [134] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型评估方法单一且统计显著性不足的问题，作者提出了PhAIL基准测试，通过基于CDF的统计方法提供更精确的性能对比和人类对标指标，揭示了当前模型与人类效率的客观差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前现实世界中视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略的评估过于依赖固定超时下的二元成功率，且样本量过小、缺乏置信区间统计，导致无法准确地比较模型间的微小性能差异。

Method: 引入PhAIL基准测试，采用“成功时间累积分布函数(CDF)”作为核心评估指标，并通过以下两种机制进行评估：1. 人类相对吞吐量(HRT)，提供置信区间；2. 基于Kolmogorov-Smirnov测试的显著性检验，用于量化对比模型性能。

Result: 通过对四种公开VLA模型测试发现，宏观平均KS检验在较小样本量（N≤30）下成功分辨了传统二元指标无法识别的模型差异（如GR00T vs. ACT），并量化出最佳VLA模型的运行速度仅为人类参考水平的1/7左右。

Conclusion: 作者认为现有的二元成功率指标不足以评估VLA模型，提出了基于CDF的统计学评估体系，该体系能更灵敏地识别模型性能差异，并指出当前VLA模型与人类操作效率仍存在显著差距（约7倍）。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [135] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人模仿学习中生成式策略效率低下和确定性策略多样性不足的问题，MARS提出了一种自适应采样策略，通过仅在必要时引入随机性，实现了高性能与高效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于生成式模型的机器人模仿学习虽然能处理多模态行为，但存在随机噪声依赖和迭代去噪过程，导致训练复杂、推理延迟高；且并非所有任务阶段都需要行为多样性，导致了计算资源的浪费。

Method: 提出了Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) 策略。该方法引入了一个自适应机制，能够根据任务阶段动态调整：在需要行为多样性的阶段注入随机噪声进行多模态生成，而在单一模态阶段切换为高效的确定性推断，从而优化训练和推理效率。

Result: 在8个模拟任务和4个真实机器人任务中表现出色，真实环境中成功率提升了16.67%，推理延迟大幅降低了83.20%，在训练效率上也优于传统的确定性策略。

Conclusion: MARS框架通过按需调用随机性，成功平衡了多模态生成策略的表达能力与确定性策略的推理效率，并能有效提升在近确定性任务中的建模表现，证明了选择性多模态机制在机器人操作任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [136] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于在线增量学习的腕关节角度估计系统，通过两阶段建模方法解决了软体传感器在复杂应用场景下的数据漂移问题，实现了高适应性的关节运动监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体可穿戴传感器应用较为简单，难以适应不同的佩戴环境、个体差异及传感器数据漂移问题，限制了其在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 采用两阶段在线增量学习方法：第一阶段利用IMU实时数据作为基准，通过在线学习更新模型以捕捉用户的动作特征；第二阶段利用更新后的模型，仅靠腕带传感器实现腕关节的角度估计。

Result: 该系统在不同测试配置（如左右手互换、佩戴位置偏差、不同受试者）下表现出良好的稳定性，腕关节轨迹估计的平均误差约为15度。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线增量学习方法有效解决了软体可穿戴传感器因佩戴位置偏差或个体差异导致的性能漂移问题，证明了该系统在不同场景下的鲁棒性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [137] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过“未来体验条件化”（FEC）利用生成式未来视频辅助机器人策略学习的方法，证明短时限的视频预测能作为有效的先验信息，显著提升复杂操纵任务的策略适应与强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步机器人操作任务中存在环境演变的不确定性，导致探索与策略适应困难，研究旨在探讨短时限、任务一致的未来视频预测能否为决策提供有效的结构化先验。

Method: 提出“未来体验条件化”（Future-Experience Conditioning, FEC）框架，通过包含三阶段的流程生成未来视频：1.利用LLM推理任务本体；2.数字孪生环境下的物体运动推演；3.无掩码视频扩散模型合成机器人动作一致的未来片段。该框架将这些未来视频作为潜空间输入，增强BC（行为克隆）和BC+RL策略的表现。

Result: 在RoboCasa和CALVIN基准测试中，FEC架构在BC+RL下取得了最佳性能。实验证明GenFuture（生成式未来）性能优于NoFuture，而WrongFuture（错误预测）会导致性能下降，验证了该方法的有效性与对未来信息偏差的敏感性。

Conclusion: 短时限的未来视频预测可作为稳健的结构化先验，有效地辅助机器人执行复杂任务，提升任务探索效率与策略自适应能力。即使预测存在偏差，只要具备一致性，生成式未来视频仍能优于无先验的方法。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


### [138] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Fisher Preserving Guidance 的训练无关推理方法，通过限制 Fisher 漂移并结合不确定性感知融合，显著提升了扩散模型在导航任务中的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉导航中虽有效，但标准的采样和推理引导容易导致轨迹偏离训练数据分布（Fisher 漂移），从而产生不可靠或低效的动作。

Method: 提出了 Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection，通过低秩雅可比矩阵分解计算保持 Fisher 的更新；引入 Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity 作为不确定性度量，用于多样本动作融合。

Result: 在 Maze2D、PushT 和真实视觉导航机器人平台上的实验表明，该方法在不增加额外训练开销的情况下，性能相较于基线扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）有显著提升。

Conclusion: Fisher Preserving Guidance 是一种训练无关的推理方法，能够有效解决扩散模型在导航任务中的轨迹漂移问题，提升了任务性能和轨迹可靠性，且无需额外训练即可应用于现有模型。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [139] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在机器人仿真测试过程中集成数据溯源（Provenance）和FAIR原则，解决测试复现性不足的问题，并提供了具体的实施框架与实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真测试的复现性极易受到测试配置、执行及后处理过程文档记录缺失的影响，缺乏端到端的透明度。

Method: 通过在现有的机器人仿真测试框架中嵌入溯源跟踪和元数据收集机制，对移动机器人导航数据集进行增强处理（增加结构化溯源信息和符合FAIR的元数据）。

Result: 成功在仿真测试框架中实现了溯源信息的自动化记录， enriquecido（丰富）了导航数据集，并总结了在此过程中遇到的词汇表对齐、属性选择及领域标准采纳等障碍及其实践建议。

Conclusion: 将数据溯源与FAIR原则集成到机器人验证工作流中是实现可重复性测试的关键，虽然存在术语对齐和标准采纳等技术挑战，但通过自动化元数据收集可显著提升实验的可重现性。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [140] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA 是一种针对机器人灵巧手操控的离线转在线 RL 后训练框架，通过动作条件价值评价和在线人工干预残差微调，克服了 VLA 模型在复杂物理环境中的执行误差，显著提升了操控成功率与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在处理高维灵巧手控制时面临执行误差大、时间不一致及在线样本效率低等挑战，需要一种能够平衡视觉语义理解与物理可靠执行的后训练框架。

Method: 1. 离线阶段：构建动作条件价值评价器（Action-conditioned Critic），利用 VLM 的认知 Token 和动作序列进行价值评估；2. 在线阶段：冻结 VLA 基础模型，引入“人在回路”（HiL）的块级残差适配机制，通过干预驱动的奖励机制在真实环境中修正执行误差。

Result: 在五个复杂的真实世界灵巧操控任务中，BORA 的平均成功率较纯模仿学习及传统离线转在线基线提升了 33%，在未见过物体的泛化任务中表现提升高达 43%。

Conclusion: BORA 框架通过结合离线价值引导与在线人工干预残差适配，有效解决了 VLA 模型在复杂灵巧手操控任务中的动作一致性与物理可靠性难题，显著提升了机器人的成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [141] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA是一个统一的具身基础模型，通过扩展视觉-语言模型并引入DiT动作解码器，实现了在不同机器人平台、任务和环境下的通用决策与连续动作控制，显著提升了泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能研究多局限于单一特定任务或机器人形态，导致系统碎片化，缺乏跨任务、跨环境和跨平台的通用泛化能力。作者旨在探索是否能通过单一视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型统一处理异构具身决策问题。

Method: 1. 模型架构：基于Qwen视觉-语言模型栈，引入了基于DiT（Diffusion Transformer）的动作解码器，实现连续动作与轨迹生成。2. 训练策略：采用跨领域大规模联合预训练，涵盖机械臂操作、人类第一视角演示、仿真数据及导航数据。3. 对齐机制：通过具身感知提示调节（Embodiment-aware prompt conditioning）处理多机器人平台差异；将操作、导航与轨迹预测纳入统一的任务框架。

Result: Qwen-VLA在多个权威基准测试中表现出色，在LIBERO (97.9%)、Simpler-WidowX (73.7%)、R2R (69.0% OSR) 及真实世界ALOHA实验中均取得优异成绩，并展现出强大的零样本（Zero-shot）和分布外（OOD）泛化能力。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA证明了通过统一的视觉-语言-动作架构和大规模多源数据训练，能够实现跨任务、跨环境、跨具身形态的通用具身决策能力，为构建通用具身智能底座提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [142] [Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30282)
*Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Boyu Ma,Shijia Han,Xinyu Zhou,Xichen Yuan,Chuhao Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gaze2Act 是一种新型 VLA 框架，通过引入人类视线信号，解决了机器人执行复杂操控任务时语言指令描述模糊的问题，实现了更精准的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型仅依赖语言指令难以精确传达人类意图，特别是在处理相似物体区分、具体操作位置指定以及动态意图调整等复杂任务时存在局限性。

Method: 提出 Gaze2Act 框架：1. 通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称视线映射到机器人视角；2. 生成目标掩码和视线点进行粗细结合的目标指定；3. 通过感知层提示（perception-level prompting）和动作层条件控制（action-level conditioning）将视线信号融入 VLA 模型。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上进行的 7 类 16 项实验任务证明，Gaze2Act 在意图准确度和任务成功率上达到了 SOTA 水平，显著优于现有基准模型。

Conclusion: 人类视线（Gaze）是用于人机协作的高效、低负担且具备高度表达能力的模态，能够有效弥补语言在复杂操控任务中的不足。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.

</details>


### [143] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab 是一种打破“GPU 模拟是高效强化学习唯一选择”定论的异构训练框架，通过解耦 CPU 物理模拟与 GPU 策略学习，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了系统的跨平台兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习领域存在一种误区，即认为高效训练必须依赖 GPU 驻留的物理模拟。本文旨在挑战这一假设，指出核心矛盾在于端到端流水线（模拟吞吐量、策略学习、同步）的效率，而非单一处理器的类型。

Method: 提出了 UniLab 架构：一个解耦 CPU 并行模拟与 GPU 策略更新的异构系统。该系统利用统一的运行时（Runtime）处理数据移动、缓冲和同步，并配套开发了 MuJoCoUni 和 MotrixSim CPU 批处理物理后端。

Result: 在同等硬件配置下，UniLab 将机器人控制任务的端到端训练效率提升了 3-10 倍；降低了对 NVIDIA CUDA 的依赖，实现了在 macOS、AMD ROCm 及 Intel XPU 等多个平台的跨设备运行。

Conclusion: GPU模拟并非实现高效强化学习训练的必要条件。UniLab 证明了通过优化的架构设计，异构的 CPU 模拟/GPU 学习流水线同样能实现卓越性能，并显著提升了机器人强化学习训练的硬件与软件兼容性。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [144] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWits 是一个旨在评估机器人认知推理、创造性工具使用及鲁棒性的新型双臂基准测试，通过多智能体框架自动生成复杂任务，研究发现当前 VLA 模型在复杂、多变的实际操作任务中表现脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试侧重于技能执行，缺乏对机器人应对现实世界意外挑战时的认知推理、创造性工具使用及鲁棒性的评估。

Method: 1. 提出了 RoboWits 双臂机器人基准测试；2. 设计了一个多智能体协作框架，用于自动生成种子任务、验证、指标设定、场景生成及任务变异。目前包含 30 个种子任务和 208 个涵盖几何、材料、组装推理的变异任务。

Result: 实验发现，尽管预训练 VLA 模型在微调后的基础任务（种子任务）上表现尚可，但在处理具有变异的任务时性能大幅下降，暴露了其在策略适应性和鲁棒性上的不足。

Conclusion: 目前的预训练 VLA 模型在应对需要推理、策略调整以及面对意外/受限环境的任务时表现出显著的脆弱性，现有的技能级基准测试无法充分评估机器人的认知推理能力。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>


### [145] [DynaFLIP: Rethinking Robotics Perception via Tri-Modal-Dynamics Guided Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30350)
*Jusuk Lee,Seungjae Lee,Jonghun Shin,Hoseong Jung,Sungha Kim,Daesol Cho,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaFLIP 是一种新型机器人预训练框架，通过将 3D 运动流引入视觉预训练，使机器人不仅能识别“场景内容”，还能掌握“物体如何随动作变化”，从而显著提升机器人操控任务的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视觉编码器多基于静态识别或视觉-语言对齐预训练，缺乏对运动和动力学的理解，导致下游策略对动态场景的适应性不足。

Method: 提出了 DynaFLIP 框架，利用图像-语言-3D流（image-language-3D flow）三元组进行预训练；通过最小化由三种模态组成的超球面空间体积（simplex volume）来强化对齐，并引入余弦正则化和对比学习目标以防止模型坍塌。

Result: DynaFLIP 学到的特征能够聚焦于控制相关的关键区域；在多种模拟及真实测试中表现优于基准方法，在非分布（OOD）场景下性能提升高达 22.5%。

Conclusion: 将动力学理解融入感知阶段，使其能够编码动作对世界的影响，是提升机器人泛化能力的关键，DynaFLIP 证明了这种“感知-动作”联合预训练方法的有效性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation critically depends on perception that preserves the action-relevant aspects of a scene. Yet most robot learning pipelines are built upon visual encoders pre-trained for static recognition or vision-language alignment, leaving motion understanding to downstream policies. We introduce DynaFLIP, a dynamics-aware multimodal pre-training framework that pushes motion understanding upstream into perception. We construct image-language-3D flow triplets from heterogeneous human and robot videos, and use these triplets as training-time supervision to shape an image-only encoder. Our key idea is to encourage the three modalities to span a small simplex volume in the shared hyperspherical space -- a smaller simplex volume indicating stronger alignment. To avoid the geometric ambiguity and trivial collapse of naive volume minimization, we combine simplex-volume minimization with a cosine regularizer and a contrastive objective. Our analyses show that DynaFLIP focuses on control-relevant regions critical for manipulation. The resulting dynamics-aware representations serve as reusable visual backbones and consistently outperform baselines across diverse downstream policies, including VLAs. We validate this across diverse simulation and real-world setups, with gains reaching +22.5% under out-of-distribution scenarios. Our results suggest that robot generalization improves when visual representations are trained to encode not just what is present, but how the world changes under action.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [From Roofline to Ruggedness: Decomposing and Smoothing the GEMM Performance Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29752)
*Aditya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 GPU 中普遍存在的“性能崎岖性”现象，并提出了一种分析框架。通过动态平铺选择和自动填充优化，有效消除了由硬件离散结构引起的性能波动，使平均吞吐量提升了 30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Roofline 模型和峰值 FLOPs 直觉无法解释 GEMM 在细微参数变化下表现出的巨大性能差异（性能崎岖性），导致非峰值工作负载性能低下且难以优化。

Method: 1. 引入“性能崎岖性分析”框架，将性能视为多维曲面而非标量边界，从机制上分解性能波动源。
2. 在 Intel Battlemage GPU 上对 BF16 GEMM 进行了 32,768 种配置的大规模扫描。
3. 实现两阶段软件栈：(i) 基于六种策略的动态平铺（Tile）选择；(ii) 基于动态规划的填充与拆分（Padding-and-splitting）优化器。

Result: 1. 成功将 16.8 TFLOPs 的原始性能波动（每 128 步）降低了 70%。
2. 将平均吞吐量提升了 30%。
3. 定量分析了剩余波动源，证明其主要由硬件结构（如 Wave 量化、GDDR6 通道哈希等）决定，且周期与软件平铺尺寸高度对齐。

Conclusion: 性能崎岖性（Performance Ruggedness）是 GPU 工作负载中普遍存在的现象，反映了底层离散硬件结构（片、子组、缓存行）与连续算术运算之间的相互作用。通过采用性能崎岖性分析框架并结合动态平铺选择和填充/拆分优化器，可以显著平滑性能表面并提升平均吞吐量。

Abstract: Adjacent GEMM problems that differ by a single 128-element step in N can show 30% different throughput on the same GPU. This pervasive performance ruggedness - invisible to roofline analysis and peak-FLOPs intuition, yet dominant for every non-peak workload - is the subject of this paper.
  We propose performance ruggedness analysis as an analytical framework complementary to roofline: rather than summarizing GPU performance with a scalar bound, treat the full multidimensional performance surface as the object of study, decompose its texture into mechanism-attributable components and separate software-removable contributions from hardware-bound ones. The framing is directly analogous to deep-learning loss landscapes - a continuous quantity (the idealized time 2MNK / compute_throughput_peak) made rugged by interaction with discrete hardware substrates (tiles, sub-groups, cache lines, DRAM channels).
  We apply the framework to BF16 NN (no transpose) GEMM on Intel Battlemage (Arc B580, sycl-tla) via a 32,768-configuration sweep (M, N, K) belongs to {128, ..., 4096}^3. The peak is 110.8 TFLOPs at the non-square shape M=3840, N=2048, K=4096 with the default tile size; the initial landscape roughness is 16.8 TFLOPs per 128-step against an ideal of 2.0.
