A two-stage software stack - (i) best-of-six dynamic tile selection and (ii) a novel dynamic-programming based padding-and-splitting optimizer with O(1) runtime lookup - reduces roughness by 70% and raises mean throughput by 30%. Cross-tile experiments establish that the residual sawtooth period scales exactly with software tile size, ruling out cache set conflicts and attributing the remaining variance to four hardware-bound sources (per-kernel base overhead, wave quantization, DPAS atom geometry and GDDR6 channel-hash interactions).

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了在稀疏更新环境下，动量动力的相位结构受“动量记忆时长”与“学习时长”比值的控制，并指出全局动量参数在处理不同频率稀疏数据时会产生频谱冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量理论假设梯度以恒定速率到达每个参数，但现实中重尾数据分布和现代架构违背了这一假设，导致原有理论适用性受限。

Method: 通过分析最小二乘法（稀疏输入）和逻辑回归（稀疏类别）两个可处理的模型，推导其二阶矩动力学的精确闭式解，并在稀疏度、批量大小和动量衰减的三个缩放指数下对其高维极限进行表征。

Result: 研究识别出了三种相位行为：当学习慢于记忆时系统表现类似于SGD；当学习快于记忆时系统不稳定；学习与记忆达成平衡时恢复经典重球（Heavy-ball）动力学。

Conclusion: 在大规模稀疏数据中，存在动量全局参数与特定稀疏度之间产生“频谱冲突”的问题；系统动力学受动量记忆时长与学习时长之比的相位结构控制。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [148] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Anytime-FC-RAG，一种在联邦生成式检索增强（RAG）中实现随时有效（anytime-valid）序列覆盖的框架，通过严格的统计建模支持自适应带宽管理和模型刷新，在降低通信成本的同时保持了理论上的统计安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 FC-RAG 仅限于固定时域，无法支持更动态的联邦场景（如任意停止时间、自适应带宽调整或模型刷新），且简单的组合方法在序列化下会失效。

Method: 提出 Anytime-FC-RAG 框架：通过将偏差预算分配给序列步骤，结合“可加的校准偏差预算”将边缘界转换为条件界，并使用“截断的博弈 e-过程（truncated betting e-process）”作为非负超鞅，实现了对所有停止时间的随时有效性保障。

Result: 在 MMLU、DBpedia 和 AG News 数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够实现预期的报警率和覆盖包络，且在保持统计安全性的前提下，实现了 14%-57% 的通信带宽节省。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG 成功实现了随时有效的序列覆盖，在保障严格统计有效性的同时，通过自适应带宽控制显著降低了通信成本，并证明了在复杂刷新机制下的安全性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [149] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了受限带宽下的联邦语言模型估计问题，证明了单轮联邦探针-Logit 蒸馏（FPLD）中带宽项的速率下界，提出了多轮残差量化机制以改进性能，并解决了异构节点带宽分配的最优性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦语言模型中节点间数据共享受限，现有研究（Dubey and Huo, 2026）给出了一定的 KL 散度速率上界，但该界中带宽项的紧致性尚未得到证明，且未能解决节点带宽异构情况下的最优资源分配问题。

Method: 1. 理论下界分析：利用非退化假设，证明了抖动 FPLD 构造的带宽项速率下界；2. 多轮精炼算法：提出利用嵌套/缩放残差量化器实现 $T$ 轮有序细化以优化带宽项；3. 异构带宽策略：推导出闭式最优分配方案 $B_i^*$，并设计了适应性变体以处理权重估计不确定性；4. 数值模拟：通过合成 n-gram 实验验证了 KL 散度与理论边界的一致性及分配策略的有效性。

Result: 1. 确定了单轮 FPLD 带宽项的紧致下界 $\Omega(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$；2. 证明了 $T$ 轮序贯精炼可将带宽项优化至 $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$；3. 给出了异构带宽下的最优分配闭式解，提出了一种基于预热阶段的适应性分配方案，并证实该策略在实际表现中优于均匀分配和逆权重基线。

Conclusion: 本文填补了联邦语言模型中带宽项速率紧致性的空白，证明了量化误差的下界为 $\Theta(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$，提出了多轮精炼机制以打破带宽瓶颈，并推导出了异构带宽分配下的“对数倾斜注水算法”（log-tilted water-filling rule），在理论与实验上均优于现有的基线方法。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


### [150] [Instance-dependent Stochastic Lipschitz bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29748)
*Marius Potfer,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对Lipschitz bandit问题，提出了一种基于局部水平集次优间隙积分的分析方法，实现了对算法遗憾的更精确刻画，并改进了特定情形下的收敛速率，使其能更好地捕捉函数局部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Lipschitz bandit遗憾界限要么是针对最差情况（Worst-case），要么仅依赖于Zooming维度（d_z）。Zooming分析过于依赖近最优水平集的渐近增长行为，无法体现函数本身细微的局部结构属性，导致在某些情形下的遗憾界不够紧致。

Method: 提出了一种新的分析框架和算法，通过对函数f在水平集上的次优间隙（suboptimality gap）进行积分来刻画遗憾，而非仅依赖Zooming维度。该方法能够捕捉函数f更细腻的结构属性。

Result: 推导出了能适应水平集局部增长规律的遗憾界；当最优解集维度为d* > 0时，获得了显著改进的自适应速率 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$；并将分析扩展至Lipschitz专家问题（全信息环境）。

Conclusion: 该研究为Lipschitz bandit问题提供了更精细的遗憾上界，克服了现有Zooming算法仅依赖渐近性质的局限性，实现了对局部增长性的自适应，并在特定条件下（如存在高维最优集）取得了优于经典Zooming的收敛速率。同时将理论框架推广到了全信息场景。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz bandit problem, where a learner sequentially maximizes an unknown Lipschitz function $f$ over a domain $\mathcal{X} \subset [0,1]^d$ using noisy pointwise evaluations. Existing regret bounds are either worst-case, scaling as $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d+1/d+2}\right )$, or adaptive via the zooming dimension $d_z$, yielding $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d_z+1/d_z+2}\right )$. However, such zooming-based guarantees are only partially instance-dependent, as they depend solely on the asymptotic growth of near-optimal level sets and fail to capture finer structural properties of $f$. We provide an analysis and an algorithm that characterizes the regret through integrals of the suboptimality gap of $f$ over its level sets. This yields regret bounds that adapt to the local growth of level sets, rather than only their asymptotic behavior. As a corollary, when the set of maximizers has dimension $d^\star>0$, we obtain improved adaptive rates of order $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$ strictly improving over classical zooming bounds in this regime. Finally, we extend our analysis to the full-information setting (Lipschitz experts) and show how some of the regularity assumptions can be relaxed.

</details>


### [151] [Joint Model and Data Sparsification via the Marginal Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29908)
*Alexander Timans,Thomas Möllenhoff,Christian A. Naesseth,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过联合学习特征与样本相关性实现稀疏化的贝叶斯学习新方法，解决了传统SBL对噪声敏感的问题，显著提升了模型的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL）依赖同方差噪声模型，对离群点和噪声误设非常敏感，导致模型拟合和预测性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种扩展的稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL）框架，在保留共轭性的前提下，将自动相关性确定（ARD）应用于特征权重，并引入样本稀疏性（类似于鲁棒回归），通过单一贝叶斯目标函数进行优化，实现封闭形式（closed-form）的更新。

Result: 实验结果表明，该联合ARD方法在多种回归任务中表现出优异的稳健性和稀疏性，能够有效抵御数据污染。

Conclusion: 联合学习特征和样本相关性的方法能够同时实现模型和数据的稀疏化，从而在回归任务中提供更稳健且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse recovery in linear systems underpins applications from signal processing to high-dimensional regression. Sparse Bayesian Learning, grounded in the principle of automatic relevance determination (ARD), offers a practical Bayesian mechanism for feature sparsity via marginal likelihood optimization. Yet, its reliance on a homoscedastic noise model renders it sensitive to data contaminations such as outliers or misspecified noise, harming model fit and predictions. Instead, we propose jointly learning individual feature and sample relevancies, enabling simultaneous model and data sparsification via a single Bayesian objective. This symmetric pruning of model and data offers a natural extension that preserves conjugacy, admits closed-form updates for standard optimization procedures, and aligns with perspectives from robust regression and influence functions. Empirical results across diverse regression tasks affirm that a joint ARD approach consistently yields both sparse and robust prediction models.

</details>


### [152] [Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319)
*Anay Mehrotra,Phuc Tran,Van H. Vu,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了面板数据下的异质性处理效应估计，通过将其转化为矩阵补全问题，提出了一个新的计算高效估计量，并在理论上首次确立了低秩近似的行级 $\ell_2$ 扰动界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵补全理论主要关注全局（平均处理效应）误差界，无法提供估计每个样本异质性处理效应所需的行级（per-row）误差保障。

Method: 将异质性处理效应估计问题建模为矩阵补全问题，利用低秩结构，提出了一种简单且计算高效的估计量；在理论分析上，通过建立新的行级 $\ell_2$ 扰动界来推导误差界。

Result: 在无需预知倾向评分、满足标准低秩性和正则性假设的前提下，该估计量达到了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$ 的行级 $\ell_2$ 误差界。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个针对低秩近似的尖锐行向量 $\ell_2$ 范数扰动界，证明了该估计量在处理异质性处理效应时具有统计一致性，解决了传统矩阵补全方法在行级估计上的局限。

Abstract: A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.

</details>


### [153] [Diffusion Models Are Statistically Optimal for Learning Low-Dimensional Multi-Modal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30153)
*Jingda Wu,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了扩散模型在学习低维子空间并集分布时，具有仅依赖于内在维度的样本复杂度，从而在无需强正则性假设的情况下，从理论上解释了其在高维学习任务中的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型理论通常依赖于过强的正则性假设（如一致有界密度或全局平滑得分函数），无法解释模型在处理具有低维和多模态特征的复杂分布时表现出的高统计效率。

Method: 研究了支持在低维子空间并集上的分布（假设子空间内分布为次高斯分布），通过分析扩散模型的样本复杂度，证明了其在 1-Wasserstein 距离下的收敛率。

Result: 证明了扩散模型达到 $\varepsilon$ 误差所需的样本复杂度至多为 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$（$k$ 为内在维度）。该结果实现了近优收敛率，仅依赖于内在维度，有效避免了维度灾难。无需平滑性、有界密度或对数凹性假设。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够自动自适应数据的内在低维结构，同时处理多模态数据，为扩散模型在复杂高维任务中的成功提供了有力的理论支撑。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable empirical success in learning high-dimensional distributions, particularly those exhibiting low-dimensional and multi-modal structures. However, theoretical understanding of their statistical efficiency remains limited. Existing theories typically rely on strong regularity assumptions, such as uniformly bounded densities or globally smooth score functions, which fail to capture such intrinsic structures. In this work, we study the sample complexity of diffusion models for learning distributions supported on a union of low-dimensional subspaces. Assuming that the data distribution within each subspace is subgaussian, we show that diffusion models require at most $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ samples to achieve $\varepsilon$ error in 1-Wasserstein distance, where $k$ is the intrinsic dimension. This near-optimal convergence rate depends only on the intrinsic dimension and significantly improves upon prior theoretical guarantees that suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Notably, our analysis applies to a broad collection of distributions without imposing smoothness, bounded-density, or log-concavity assumptions. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can statistically adapt to intrinsic low-dimensional structure while naturally accommodating multi-modal data, offering a rigorous theoretical justification for their success in complex high-dimensional learning tasks.

</details>


### [154] [Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30253)
*Rocco Caprio,Adrien Corenflos,Sam Power*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过传输-信息不等式和泛函平滑条件，从理论上证明了坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）在 Wasserstein 距离下的收缩性，并为多种贝叶斯统计模型的算法收敛性提供了严谨保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）在参数推断中被广泛应用，但其收敛性理论尚不完备。研究旨在通过 Wasserstein 距离这一更稳健的视角，为 CAVI 提供通用的收敛性保证。

Method: 利用传输-信息不等式（transport-information inequality）分析固定点附近的特性，结合泛函平滑条件（functional smoothness），在一般平滑流形及部分非平滑空间中分析 Wasserstein 距离下的收缩性。

Result: 证明了 CAVI 在满足特定不等式和光滑条件下表现出收缩性，并提供了局部收敛保证。实验验证了这些结论适用于高斯混合模型（GMM）、贝叶斯 Probit 回归及基于 Pólya-Gamma 变量的 Logistic 回归（Jaakkola-Jordan 算法）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）在 Wasserstein 距离下的收缩性质，证明了该算法在温和条件下（传输-信息不等式与泛函平滑性）具有局部收敛性，并将其理论框架成功应用于多种贝叶斯模型。

Abstract: We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).

</details>


### [155] [Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30292)
*Hanyang Jiang,Rina Foygel Barber,Ashwin Pananjady,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对时间序列数据中非可交换性导致传统共形预测方法失效或精度低的问题，本文提出了一种名为“留窗口法”（LWO）的改进策略，通过量化数据偏离循环可交换性的程度，实现了在保持有效覆盖率的同时提高预测区间的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的共形预测依赖于数据的可交换性假设，而时间序列的各种假设（如时间依赖性）常导致标准方法失效或预测效率降低；传统的分割共形方法（split conformal）虽稳健但因数据拆分导致预测精度损失。因此，寻找不依赖拆分且适用于时间序列的可靠置信区间构建方法成为研究重点。

Method: 提出了“留窗口法”（Leave-a-Window-Out, LWO），用以替代传统的留一法（Jackknife）。该方法通过在模型拟合中加入稳定性约束，量化了时间序列对“循环可交换性”的偏离度，从而能够在保证覆盖率的同时适应时间依赖性。

Result: 实验表明，LWO方法在标准留一法（Jackknife）因时间依赖性而失效的场景下，仍能维持有效的覆盖率，且构建的置信区间明显窄于传统的分割共形预测方法。

Conclusion: LWO方法通过引入窗口剔除机制，在时间序列预测中实现了比拆分共形预测更窄的置信区间，同时保持了有效的覆盖率。研究进一步量化了数据偏离循环可交换性的程度，为非独立同分布数据的预测推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting.
  In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Constant Depth Threshold Circuits For Exhaustive Epistasis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29719)
*André Ribeiro,Aleksandar Ilic,Leonel Sousa*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种利用神经形态硬件中阈值门电路加速生物信息学中表位检测（Epistasis Detection）问题的方案，通过创新的编码策略和高效的人口计数电路设计，在保持对数线性空间复杂度的同时，大幅提升了计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机在处理生物信息学中极其复杂的组合问题（如表位检测）时算力受限，而新兴的大规模神经形态硬件为利用阈值门电路解决此类问题提供了契机。

Method: 提出了一种基于阈值门（Threshold Gate）电路的流水线架构，利用漏积分发放（LIF）神经元进行数据编码和组合生成，并构建了基于对数深度、对数立方连接数的恒定深度人口计数电路（Population Count Circuit）。

Result: 提出的电路设计成功实现了以组合总数为界的执行时间上限，避免了传统方法的额外复杂度开销，并有效解决了在硬件有限扇入和可变精度限制下的电路可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用神经形态硬件的特性（如存内计算和可配置并行性）可以显著提高表位检测（Epistasis Detection）任务的效率，将算法复杂度降低至仅取决于待计算组合的数量，且空间复杂度为对数线性。

Abstract: The development of large-scale neuromorphic hardware has made practical implementations of threshold gate-based circuits a near-term possibility. The complexity advantages regarding traditional computing classes, as evidenced in the literature, have prompted us to tackle Epistasis Detection, one of the most computationally complex combinatorial problems in bioinformatics. We propose specially designed circuits that calculate the relative frequencies of all dataset combinations in an efficient pipelined fashion, taking advantage of co-located memory and configurable parallelism, obtaining complexity gains. Overall, we obtain the runtime to be bounded by the number of combinations to calculate, without any additional complexity overhead, contrary to classical approaches, using log-linear space. To accomplish this, we propose a data encoding and combination generation strategy using Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) neurons, that feeds a constant depth threshold gate population count circuit. Accounting for typical hardware characteristics, such as limited fan-in and variable precisions, we obtain logarithmic depth and log-cubic linear connections, for the population count circuit by composing developed unbounded fan-in constant depth threshold gate circuits to perform population count and binary array sum.

</details>


### [157] [Design-Oriented Modeling of TSV Substrate Noise Coupling to Ring VCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29867)
*Ilias Exouzidis,Alberto Garcia-Ortiz,George Floros,Georgios Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种量化3D-IC中TSV寄生耦合引起的衬底噪声的解析宏模型，并验证了其在RF电路中的噪声注入效应，发现耦合强度随频率升高而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSV的金属-氧化物-硅结构会在3D-IC和chiplet系统中引入显著的寄生耦合路径，威胁敏感RF模块的频谱纯度，亟需一种有效的评估方法。

Method: 1. 建立基于信号-地（Signal-Ground）TSV对的三端口RLGC闭式解析宏模型；2. 采用22nm FD-SOI技术设计三级环形压控振荡器（Ring VCO）；3. 利用Spectre RF进行多音谐波平衡（Harmonic Balance）仿真，并通过PDK提供的基板终端接口进行受控噪声注入。

Result: 在1 GHz、0.5 Vpp的噪声干扰下，观察到初级边带杂散（spur）为-35.2 dBc；噪声幅度与边带杂散呈单调增长关系；耦合效应具有低通特性，spur幅值随频率从500 MHz到2 GHz呈现下降趋势（-20.2 dBc降至-33.1 dBc）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种紧凑且面向设计的TSV衬底噪声评估方法，通过RLGC宏模型量化了TSV寄生耦合效应对RF电路（如振荡器）频谱纯度的影响，并验证了耦合响应呈现低通特性。

Abstract: Through-silicon vias (TSVs) enable dense vertical interconnects in 3D-IC and chiplet systems, but their metal-oxide-silicon structure introduces significant parasitic coupling paths that can degrade the spectral purity of sensitive RF blocks. This paper presents a compact, design-oriented methodology for assessing TSV-induced substrate noise in mixed-signal circuits. We derive a closed-form analytical three-port RLGC macromodel for a Signal-Ground TSV pair that explicitly exposes the substrate node. The methodology is validated using a three-stage Ring VCO designed in a 22 nm FD-SOI technology, where specific RF devices from the process design kit (PDK) provide direct access to the transistor substrate terminals for controlled noise injection. Multi-tone Harmonic Balance simulations in Spectre RF quantify the impact of TSV aggressors on the oscillator's output spectrum. The results indicate that an aggressor of 1 GHz, 0.5 V$_{pp}$ induces a primary sideband spur of -35.2 dBc. Sensitivity characterization reveals that the magnitude of these sideband spurs increases monotonically with the aggressor amplitude. Furthermore, frequency sweeps demonstrate a low-pass coupling response, where the induced spur magnitude decreases from -20.2 dBc at 500 MHz to -33.1 dBc at 2 GHz.

</details>


### [158] [Precomputed 1D-CNNs for Atrial Fibrillation Detection on Tiny Smart Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29994)
*Lukas Einhaus,Natalie Maman,Julian Hoever,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分组卷积的优化方法，提升了FPGA上基于LUT预计算的1D-CNN架构的扩展性，在极小资源消耗下实现了高效的生物信号分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对微型智能传感器系统中1D-CNN面临的资源限制与延迟要求，传统基于LUT的预计算方法存在扩展性差的问题，现有深度可分离卷积方案仍有局限。

Method: 提出了一种新型卷积模块，涵盖多种分组卷积形式，并配套开发了超参数选择算法，以优化基于查找表（LUT）的预计算架构。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心电数据集上达到95%的F1分数；硬件实现仅需2,844个LUT，且无需使用DSP或BRAM，成功部署于AMD Spartan 7 S15 FPGA。

Conclusion: 通过将分组卷积引入基于LUT的FPGA加速器设计，提出了一种兼顾资源效率与扩展性的方法，验证了其在极小规模FPGA上部署高性能医疗时间序列分析模型的可行性。

Abstract: 1D-CNNs play a crucial role for time-series analysis on tiny smart sensor systems, e.g. for biosignal analysis, predictive maintenance, or structural health monitoring. LUTbased precomputation has emerged as an interesting optimization technique to implement such neural networks on FPGAs. The core idea is to precompute all possible outputs of a neural network layer and store them directly in the lookup tables of the FPGAs. This enables highly resource-efficient networks with ultra-low latency but suffers from poor scalability. Previous work has explored using depthwise-separable convolutions to improve scalability. In this paper, we generalize this approach to consider additional forms of grouped convolutions. Based on this, we propose a novel type of convolutional block and an algorithm to guide the choice of hyper parameters for this block. We evaluate our approach on a medical time-series dataset for predicting atrial fibrillation using the MIT-BIH database (ECG recordings). The resulting hardware accelerators are small enough to be deployed on an AMD Spartan 7 S15. They achieve a F1-Score of up to 95% while only requiring 2,844 LUTs and no DSPs or BRAM.

</details>


### [159] [elasticAI.explorer: Towards a Unified End-to-End Framework for Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30019)
*Natalie Maman,Florian Hettstedt,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 elasticAI.explorer，这是一个高度可扩展的 Python NAS 框架，通过 YAML 驱动的动态搜索空间和自动化的硬件在环工作流，解决了传统 NAS 框架在硬件适配和部署方面的耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NAS 框架通常将搜索空间定义、模型实现和部署管线耦合过紧，导致难以扩展到新硬件平台或集成自定义算子，增加了嵌入式 AI 部署的工程难度。

Method: 1. 基于 Optuna 构建，引入 YAML 格式定义搜索空间，支持动态模型生成；2. 支持层级式、单元式及分层式搜索空间；3. 集成硬件相关的代码生成、Docker 交叉编译工具链及自动化性能基准测试二进制文件生成，支持硬件在环（Hardware-in-the-loop）工作流；4. 提供 FLOPs、参数量及延迟估计的可扩展评估器。

Result: 成功实现了一个统一的框架，实现了 NAS 过程从自动化搜索到硬件部署的全流程自动化，显著降低了跨硬件平台移植 NAS 的复杂性。

Conclusion: elasticAI.explorer 提供了一个灵活、可扩展且支持硬件在环的 NAS 框架，通过解耦搜索空间定义、模型实现与部署流程，有效降低了在嵌入式 AI 中部署硬件感知 NAS 的工程门槛，并加速了针对异构加速器的相关研究。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has become an important approach for automatically designing neural networks under task-specific and hardware-specific constraints. However, many existing NAS frameworks tightly couple search space definitions, model implementations, and deployment pipelines, making extension to new hardware platforms and custom operators difficult. In this paper, we present the elasticAI.explorer, an extensible Python framework for hardware-aware NAS built on top of Optuna. The framework introduces a YAML-based search space specification that dynamically translates into executable neural network models during sampling. The approach supports layer-wise, cell-based, and hierarchical search spaces while maintaining a unified interface for optimization and deployment. Beyond architecture generation, the framework integrates hardware-specific code generation, Docker-based cross-compilation toolchains, and automated creation of on-device benchmarking binaries, enabling hardware-in-the-loop NAS workflows. The system further provides extensible evaluators for FLOPs, parameter count, and latency estimation. The elasticAI.explorer aims to reduce the engineering overhead of embedded AI deployment and accelerate research on hardware-aware NAS for heterogeneous accelerator platforms

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [RTP-LLM: High-Performance Alibaba LLM Inference Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29639)
*Boyu Tan,Jiarui Guo,Zongwei Lv,Hanbo Sun,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Xinfei Shi,Zetao Hu,Yaxin Yu,Chi Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Xi Yang,Wei Zhang,Bo Cai,Silu Zhou,Xiyu Wang,Na He,Yinghao Yu,Wending Bao,Guiyang Huang,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Nan Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Lu Chen,Guoding Li,Tao Lan,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM是一款面向工业级场景的高性能LLM推理引擎，通过计算/访存解耦、高效缓存管理及系统级协同优化，在阿里巴巴的亿级用户生产环境中实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在大规模工业部署中面临模型加载慢、推理延迟高、内存容量瓶颈及多模态处理复杂等挑战，现有方案难以满足高性能及大规模并发需求。

Method: 提出了RTP-LLM推理引擎，核心技术包括：1. 基于文件顺序驱动的I/O与并行通信重叠优化模型加载；2. 预填充-解码解耦架构（Prefill-Decode Disaggregation）优化计算与访存压力；3. 分层多级KV Cache管理提高缓存复用；4. 模块化投机采样（Speculative Decoding）、自适应KV Cache量化及多模态处理支持。

Result: 相比vLLM和SGLang，RTP-LLM表现卓越：加载速度提升4.7-6.3倍；生产环境中TTFT P95延迟降低35-37%，缓存复用率提升215%；投机采样与多模态推理吞吐量分别提升1.12-2.48倍和1.86-2.52倍。

Conclusion: RTP-LLM凭借其生产验证的架构及多种优化技术，成为工业级大模型部署的领先解决方案，显著提升了大规模应用场景下的推理性能与效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI applications, but deploying them at scale presents significant challenges. We present RTP-LLM, a high-performance inference engine for industrial-scale LLM deployment, successfully deployed across Alibaba Group serving over 100 million users. RTP-LLM addresses fundamental bottlenecks through integrated design. It optimizes model loading via file-order-driven I/O and parallel I/O-communication overlapping. The Prefill-Decode Disaggregation architecture decouples compute-intensive prefill from memory-bound decode phases, combined with hierarchical multi-tiered KV cache management enabling efficient cache reuse. In addition, RTP-LLM incorporates modular speculative decoding supporting multiple algorithms, adaptive KV cache quantization, and decoupled multimodal processing, with support for multi-level parallelism.
  Comprehensive evaluations across diverse model architectures (8B-235B parameters) have been conducted, where both controlled benchmarks and real production workloads are used. The results demonstrate RTP-LLM's superior performance against vLLM and SGLang: 4.7x-6.3x model loading speedup, 35-37% TTFT P95 latency reduction with 215% cache reuse improvement in production traffic scheduling, 1.12x-2.48x and 1.86x-2.52x throughput improvements in speculative decoding and multimodal inference, respectively, and 35-40% batch latency reduction with 1.9x-3.0x TTFT improvement in quantized inference. RTP-LLM's production-proven architecture and open-source availability make it a comprehensive solution for industrial LLM deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Residual-Entropy Accounting for Routed Atom-Budgeted Learned Indexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29061)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了特定学习索引架构下的前驱与秩查询复杂度，通过引入“条件残余答案熵”构建了查询性能的理论模型，提出了影价格分配规则，并实验分析了该架构相对于传统方法在处理真实数据集时的开销与瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习索引缺乏在特定“路由-预测-修复”架构下的量化复杂度分析，旨在通过信息论视角量化此类架构在处理有序数据查找时的查询时间下界与性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一个包含有序目录路由、带计数本地预测器（提供认证秩窗口）及精确修复（比较搜索）的体系结构；引入了“条件残余答案熵”作为衡量查询时间的主参数；证明了双侧账务定理（Accounting Theorem）；并开发了非预言性的分段线性映射方法及影价格（Shadow-price）分配规则。

Result: 证明了在给定的架构约束下，查询时间与条件残余答案熵成比例；在SOSD和Zenodo数据集上完成了实证评估，对比了PGM-index、RadixSpline和二分查找，揭示了当前原型实现的性能瓶颈与开销。

Conclusion: 该研究定义了路由、原子预算和认证修复学习索引架构下的查询时间缩放规律。通过引入条件残余答案熵，建立了查询复杂度与模型信息量之间的理论桥梁，并揭示了影价格分配机制，指出了该原型系统在实际基准测试中存在的开销瓶颈。

Abstract: We study exact predecessor and rank search in a routed, atom-budgeted, certified-repair learned-index architecture. An ordered directory routes each query to a contiguous interval, a counted local predictor returns a certified rank window, and exact repair resolves the remaining uncertainty by comparisons. The result is scoped to this architecture and does not claim guarantees for arbitrary learned-index designs such as unconstrained RMI dispatch, hash routing, neural routing, or exact-payload indexes without additional accounting.
  The main parameter is conditional residual answer entropy: the entropy of the exact answer after the leaf, predictor output, certificate, and charged pre-repair information are observed. We prove a two-sided accounting theorem showing that this functional gives the query-time scale under the stated architecture and local predictor-atom budget. Directory space, sorted-array storage, and transcript-indexed repair-program space are treated as separate system costs, so the theorem is not a byte-level space lower bound or a compact implementation recipe.
  We also give a rank-spread specialization in which the radius term log(1 + Delta) is valid only when many residual ranks remain likely after the predictor transcript is known. For counted piecewise-linear segments, we make the profile term non-oracular, derive a shadow-price allocation rule, compute finite-instance RGapM and GapM values on real SOSD and Zenodo samples, and report benchmarks against PGM-index, RadixSpline, and binary search. The benchmarks expose overheads and bottlenecks rather than claiming speed for the shadow prototype.

</details>


### [162] [An Improved Greedy Approximation for (Metric) $k$-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29165)
*Moses Charikar,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Ruiquan Gao,Fabrizio Grandoni,Euiwoong Lee,Ernest van Wijland*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过克服将LMP Greedy算法应用于平方距离函数的理论障碍，针对一般度量空间下的 k-Means 问题提出了新的 $5.83$-近似算法，显著提升了该问题的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有k-Means问题的最优近似比仍有提升空间，且此前受限于难以将用于设施选址问题的Greedy LMP算法（具有2-近似比）适配至平方距离的代价函数中。

Method: 通过将基于平方度量连接成本的改进型LMP近似算法，与Cohen-Addad等人（STOC'25）提出的针对k-Median问题的通用框架相结合。

Result: 在一般度量空间中实现了 $3+2\sqrt{2}+\epsilon < 5.83$ 的多项式时间近似算法，显著优于先前的 $(9+\epsilon)$-近似，并略优于欧几里得空间下最好的 5.92-近似。

Conclusion: 研究成功通过对带有平方度量连接成本的Lagrangian Multiplier Preserving (LMP) 机制进行改进，突破了传统分析障碍，为一般度量空间下的 k-Means 问题提供了更优的近似比。

Abstract: Clustering is a basic task in data analysis and machine learning, and the optimization of clustering objectives are well-studied optimization problems; amongst these, the $k$-Means objective is arguably the most well known. Given a collection of points in a metric space, the goal is to partition them into $k$ clusters, each with an associated center, so as to minimize the sum of squared distances of points to their cluster centers. In this paper, we present a polynomial-time $3+2\sqrt{2}+ε<5.83$-approximation algorithm for $k$-Means in general metrics. This substantially improves on the current-best $(9+ε)$-approximation in [Ahmadian, Norouzi-Fard, Svensson, Ward - FOCS'17, SICOMP'20], and even slightly improves on the $5.92$-approximation in [Cohen-Addad, Esfandiari, Mirrokni, Narayanan - STOC'22] for the Euclidean special case.
  A natural approach for $k$-Means is to leverage Lagrangian Multiplier Preserving (LMP) approximations for the facility location problem. The previous best results for $k$-Means build upon an adaptation of an LMP $3$-approximation for facility location with metric connection costs in [Jain, Vazirani - J.ACM'01] based on a primal-dual method, rather than on the improved LMP greedy $2$-approximation for the same problem in [Jain, Mahdian, Markakis, Saberi, Vazirani - J.ACM'03]. The barrier to using the improved LMP algorithm was that no adaptation of this algorithm and its analysis to the case of squared metric connection costs was known (since squared distances violate triangle inequality). Our main contribution is overcoming this barrier by providing such an adaptation. This new LMP approximation algorithm is then combined with the framework recently introduced in [Cohen-Addad, Grandoni, Lee, Schwiegelshohn, Svensson - STOC'25] for the related (metric) $k$-Median problem.

</details>


### [163] [Distributed Gaussian Mean Testing under Communication Constraints: messages, samples, and coins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29426)
*Clément L. Canonne,Nimitt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文推广了分布式高斯均值检验问题，在更现实的异构通信、异构样本及有限共享随机性的设定下，给出了相应的理论证明与复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式高斯均值检验研究多基于同构样本（每人 $m$ 个样本）和同构通信（每人 $\ell$ 位）的假设。为了更贴近实际通信受限场景，本文旨在解决当系统资源异构（样本数不同、通信能力不同）以及共享随机源有限时，高斯均值测试的理论性能边界问题。

Method: 理论分析与信息论界限推导。通过扩展传统设定的参数（如共享随机数 $s$、异构样本数 $m_k$ 和异构通信约束 $\ell_k$），建立分布式高斯均值检验的 Minimax 风险下界和最优性分析框架。

Result: 明确了在该推广模型下，实现均值检验区分（$\|μ\|_2 = 0$ 与 $\|μ\|_2 \ge \varepsilon$）所需的信息论极限，并量化了在有限共享随机位 $s$、异构样本数 $m_k$ 和异构通信 $\ell_k$ 约束下，样本复杂度与通信复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 作者通过引入更一般的通信与样本分布模型，进一步完善了分布式高斯均值检验的理论边界，为资源受限环境下进行多方统计推断提供了更广泛的数学分析框架。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of Gaussian mean testing in a distributed, communication constrained setting, where each of $n$ users independently observes samples from an unknown $d$-dimensional spherical Gaussian distribution $\mathcal{G}(μ,\mathbb{I}_d)$, and can communicate up to $\ell$ bits to a central referee. The referee's goal is then to distinguish between cases (i) $\|μ\|_2 = 0$ versus (ii) $\|μ\|_2\ge \varepsilon$. This problem has been considered in the private- and public-coin settings, when each user holds exactly one sample, or more generally when each holds exactly $m$ samples. In this work, we significantly generalize the question in three directions: when the users only share a small number $s$ of random bits, when each user holds a different number of samples $m_k$, and when each user can send a different number of bits $\ell_k$ to the referee.

</details>


### [164] [Sampling Directed Eulerian Tours in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29566)
*Nima Anari*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间内近似均匀采样有向欧拉回路的随机算法，通过 flip--repair walk 和动态数据结构突破了传统采样算法在稀疏图上的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的欧拉回路采样算法在稀疏图中受到 $mn$ 复杂度界限的限制，作者旨在通过构造更高效的马尔可夫链与数据结构，在近乎线性的时间内实现均匀采样。

Method: 1. 核心为 2-in/2-out 图，设计了一种新的局部马尔可夫链“翻转-修复游走”（flip--repair walk），并通过动态弦数据结构（dynamic chord data structure）进行高效实现；2. 使用逐点度数缩减（pointwise degree-reduction）封装器将该方法扩展至任意度数的图。

Result: 在任意有向欧拉多重图中，实现了 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间复杂度的近似均匀采样，证明了 flip--repair walk 具有近线性的混合时间。

Conclusion: 该算法实现了在任意有向欧拉多重图中以 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间复杂度近似均匀采样欧拉回路，打破了稀疏图上受限于 $mn$ 的采样瓶颈。

Abstract: We give a randomized algorithm that samples a nearly uniform Eulerian tour of a directed Eulerian multigraph with $m$ arcs in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ time. The guarantee is worst-case, applies to arbitrary directed Eulerian multigraphs, and breaks the $mn$-type arborescence-sampling barrier on sparse graphs.
  The core case is a $2$-in/$2$-out graph. We introduce a new local Markov chain, the flip--repair walk: one step locally splits a tour into two circuits and then chooses uniformly among the local flips that repair the state to one tour. We prove that this walk mixes in nearly linear many steps and implement the walk using a dynamic chord data structure. A pointwise degree-reduction wrapper extends the sampler from this degree-two core to arbitrary degrees while preserving the $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ total running time.
  The high-level algorithmic plan, the switching-network reduction, and the dynamic data-structure argument were devised by the author. The author conjectured the mixing theorem underlying the analysis, and GPT 5.5 Pro Extended produced its linear-algebra proof. Codex assisted with manuscript assembly and typesetting.

</details>


### [165] [On Language Generation in the Limit with Bounded Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30324)
*Jon Kleinberg,Anay Mehrotra,Amin Saberi,Grigoris Velegkas*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了受限内存下的语言生成与识别问题，证明了有限内存虽会显著限制语言识别能力，但语言生成在特定策略下仍对可数语言集合可行，且自适应存储优于滑动窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言生成研究多假设可访问完整历史，这不符合现实中算法仅能保留有限信息的限制。本文旨在探讨在严格有界内存约束下，语言生成与语言识别任务的可行性及效率边界。

Method: 使用了计算学习理论框架，结合Sperner定理和对称链分解等组合数学方法，分析了无内存生成器、滑动窗口及自适应存储策略在不同约束下的表现，并对比了增量识别算法的局限性。

Result: 1.无内存生成器在特定枚举限制下可生成任意可数无限语言集合；2.推导了有限集合下记忆无内存生成器的最优极小化密度（minimax density）；3.证明了自适应选择性存储优于简单的滑动窗口；4.发现增量识别算法对包含三个以上语言的集合即失效，但近似识别在有限集合下可行。

Conclusion: 有限内存对语言生成和语言识别的影响存在显著差异：语言生成在可数集合下仍可实现（尽管效率受限），而语言识别在受限内存下仅限于有限集合，且性能随规模扩大而下降。此外，自适应存储比滑动窗口更能有效提升生成密度。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under bounded memory. In this task, a learner observes examples from an unknown target language one at a time and must eventually output only new valid examples. Prior work assumes access to the entire history, a strong assumption since realistic algorithms retain limited past information. Classical work in learning theory shows memory constraints dramatically alter learnability; we extend this to language generation.
  First, we study memoryless generators. Under a mild enumeration restriction, every countable collection of infinite languages remains generable without memory. Without this restriction, we exactly characterize when memoryless generation is possible. For finite collections, we characterize the optimal minimax density achievable by memoryless generators -- the best density guaranteed against any collection of a given size. This combinatorial bound relies on Sperner's theorem and symmetric chain decompositions.
  We further show that a sliding window of the last $W$ examples does not improve this worst-case density, whereas allowing it to store $b$ adaptively chosen past examples improves the achievable density for every $b \geq 1$.
  Finally, we revisit identification in the limit, where the learner must converge to a single correct hypothesis for the target language. We focus on its incremental variant, where the learner remembers only its previous guess. Here, although exact identification fails on a collection of just three languages, a mild relaxation requiring convergence to an ``approximate'' version of the target is achievable for every finite collection.
  These results show bounded memory affects these tasks differently: generation remains achievable for every countable collection, while density and identification are confined to finite collections, with guarantees weakening as the collection grows.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [HPC-vQPU: A Service-Export Architecture for Virtual QPUs on Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28845)
*Shusen Liu,Pascal Jahan Elahi,Ugo Varetto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HPC-vQPU 是一种新型服务架构，通过将设备语义快照与 HPC 批处理作业解耦并原子绑定，成功在受限的 HPC 环境中安全、高效地导出了交互式虚拟量子处理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子模拟需要 HPC 算力，但 HPC 系统严格的批处理调度机制与量子软件预期的交互式、后端驱动接口不兼容，且安全规范限制了直接调用，导致难以在 HPC 环境中提供具备完整拓扑、原生门和校准语义的虚拟量子处理器（vQPU）。

Method: 提出了 HPC-vQPU 架构，将系统划分为“云端控制平面”（管理设备身份、任务生命周期和快照）和“HPC 常驻执行平面”（通过作业调度程序运行 GPU 任务）。引入了基于拓扑和校准感知的“设备快照”机制，在任务领取时进行原子绑定，作为不可变契约随作业执行，确保语义的一致性和 hermetic（密封）执行。

Result: 在 Pawsey 超算中心的 Setonix GPU 上实现了原型系统。实验表明：系统服务开销可控；校准快照显著提升了输出精度；原子绑定机制有效解决了设备变动带来的陈旧数据问题；实现了任务精确一次执行（exactly-once）以及故障后的状态自动恢复。

Conclusion: HPC-vQPU 成功实现了在受限的批处理 HPC 环境中导出高保真虚拟 QPU 服务，证明了通过架构解耦（控制平面与执行平面）和快照绑定机制，可以在不破坏 HPC 安全策略的前提下，提供互动式、语义一致的量子模拟体验。

Abstract: Device-aware quantum simulation increasingly requires HPC-scale accelerators, yet secure supercomputers expose batch-scheduled execution environments rather than the interactive, backend-oriented interfaces expected by quantum software. The key obstacle is not only remote job submission: an HPC-hosted virtual QPU must preserve topology, native-gate, and calibration semantics across queue delay, scheduler allocation, compute-node isolation, and partial execution-side failures, without opening inbound paths into the cluster.
  We present HPC-vQPU, a service-export architecture for virtual QPUs on batch-scheduled HPC systems. HPC-vQPU separates a cloud-facing control plane, which owns device identity, task lifecycle, snapshot binding, and event projection, from an HPC-resident execution plane, which claims work and realises it through scheduler-backed GPU jobs. Coordination is exclusively outbound and agent initiated. The central abstraction is a topology- and calibration-aware device snapshot bound atomically at claim time and carried into execution as an immutable contract, making each scheduled job hermetic while preserving fresh device semantics.
  We implement HPC-vQPU at the Pawsey Supercomputing Research Centre using Setonix GPUs, Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum, and IBM Fez calibration data. Production experiments show that service overhead is bounded and additive, while workload scaling remains confined to the simulator; calibration-bearing snapshots produce measurable output shifts; claim-time binding prevents stale execution after pre-claim device mutation; concurrent agents complete 50/50 tasks exactly once; and explicit recovery restores stale running tasks after agent failure. These results show that secure, scheduler-mediated HPC infrastructure can export device-faithful quantum simulation as an interactive virtual-QPU service.

</details>


### [167] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZEROGNN 是一款消除宿主机编排瓶颈的系统，通过将元数据驱动的控制逻辑下沉至 GPU，实现了动态 GNN 训练的全 GPU 驻留执行，显著提升了端到端性能与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GNN 采样训练中，元数据驱动的控制流导致 CPU 成为关键瓶颈，CPU-GPU 频繁同步和 host-device 的编排开销严重制约了计算吞吐量，传统技术（如 CUDA Graphs）难以处理这种具备动态特性的执行结构。

Method: 提出了将运行时元数据保留在 GPU 设备端的方法；设计了一种“固定启动结构”（fixed launch structure）以支持动态执行流的 CUDA Graph 复用；并使用“保守而紧凑的执行包络”（conservative yet tight execution envelope）来解决内存分配与核函数启动的动态性问题，从而实现完全驻留在 GPU 端的执行。

Result: 在 GNN 采样任务中，端到端性能提升高达 5.28 倍，GPU 利用率接近 100%，内存效率与理想的元数据预分配相当，且有效消除了多 GPU 扩展时的宿主机瓶颈。

Conclusion: ZEROGNN 成功将基于元数据的控制流卸载至 GPU，消除了 CPU 的瓶颈，通过固定启动结构与紧凑的执行包络设计，实现了动态 GNN 训练的近 100% GPU 利用率，并显著提升了多 GPU 扩展性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [168] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对异步多任务（AMT）环境下嵌套Fork-join程序的SDC高效检测与局部恢复方案，通过任务树追溯和选择性重计算，实现了极低的错误恢复开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超级计算机规模扩大，无声数据损坏（SDC）风险增加；而在异步多任务（AMT）运行时环境中，由于动态任务生成和工作窃取特性，传统的任务复制策略难以实施。

Method: 提出一种针对嵌套Fork-join程序的复制机制：通过记录任务树，在检测到最终结果不一致时，执行自上而下的树遍历以定位损坏任务，并仅对受损任务进行重计算，同时复用已验证的正确子任务结果。

Result: 在Itoyori集群AMT运行时中的实验表明，识别和重新处理受损任务所需的时间开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该机制成功地以极小的开销实现了嵌套Fork-join程序中SDC的检测与恢复，并具备通过Futures机制扩展至更复杂协作任务的潜力。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [169] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一种基于Kubernetes、Knative和Apache Kafka的实时物流数据处理框架。该框架将MapReduce模型与FaaS架构相结合，实现了高效的分布式计算和负载自适应扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统产生海量的实时数据流（如GPS、IoT数据），传统处理方式难以满足实时监控、运营优化和快速决策的需求，因此需要一种能够高效处理此类数据并支持弹性伸缩的框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于事件驱动的MapReduce框架。该系统架构基于Kubernetes和Knative，利用Apache Kafka作为组件间通信主干；采用五个松耦合服务进行数据接收、处理和聚合；使用Redis存储工作流元数据，AWS S3进行持久化存储；融合了函数即服务（FaaS）理念与分布式处理技术。

Result: 实验评估表明，该系统随着输入数据量的增加能够实现有效的水平扩展，且支持按需进行“零规模”（scale-to-zero）的自动化部署，证明了其处理大规模、波动性物流数据的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的基于事件驱动的MapReduce框架成功实现了物流数据的实时流式处理，并在Kubernetes/Knative环境下展示了优异的可扩展性，特别是在按需计算（scale-to-zero）和负载适应性方面表现出色。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [170] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TC-MIS，一种通过将 MIS 计算重构为稀疏矩阵运算并利用 GPU Tensor Cores 进行加速的算法，在大规模图数据处理上实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现代 GPU 中 Tensor Cores (TCs) 在图算法领域应用不足的问题；利用 TC 比传统 CUDA Cores 高出 8-16 倍的吞吐量，突破图算法中因不规则访存和工作负载不平衡导致的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种 TC-MIS 算法，将最大独立集（MIS）计算的关键阶段转化为稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV）；通过对图邻接矩阵进行分块（Tiling），并结合 Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) 指令集，将不规则的图遍历转化为规则的矩阵计算。

Result: 在多种现代 GPU 架构（Ampere、Ada Lovelace、Hopper、Blackwell）上评估显示，TC-MIS 相比现有最先进方法平均加速 2.84x 至 18.80x，在 H200 上峰值加速达 44.38x，同时保持了高质量的解。

Conclusion: TC-MIS 成功展示了利用 Tensor Cores 加速非矩阵类图算法（MIS）的可行性，证明了通过将图算法重构为稀疏矩阵运算（SpMV），可显著提升 GPU 处理大规模非结构化数据的性能。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [171] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AMDP 通过限制流水线阶段内参数更新的时间差，并结合梯度累积与多流水线并发机制，解决了传统异步流水线并行中的收敛性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步流水线并行方法在训练大规模模型时，由于前向和后向传递之间的参数不匹配（Parameter Mismatch）问题，会导致模型收敛性能下降。

Method: 提出异步多向流水线并行（AMDP）：1. 限制第一阶段在反向传播前最多处理两个小批次以控制失配；2. 启动多个并发流水线并根据深度自动调整；3. 采用累积梯度策略，确保参数失配被严格限制在单个优化步骤内。

Result: 在 GPT 和 BERT 模型上的实验表明，AMDP 在保证收敛性的同时，显著加速了模型训练过程。

Conclusion: AMDP 通过限制参数失配范围和并发流水线调度，在解决异步流水线并行训练中收敛性下降问题的同时，有效维持了高硬件利用率。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [172] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套跨架构的自动化CARM模型构建框架，支持x86、ARM和RISC-V，通过定制化汇编微基准测试，实现了极高精度的性能建模，解决了当前CARM分析工具在非Intel架构上的缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能计算（HPC）系统日益复杂，但针对Cache-aware Roofline Model（CARM）的支持工具存在严重局限：Intel有专属工具，ARM支持不足，而AMD及RISC-V等架构则完全缺乏自动化工具，这阻碍了跨架构的应用性能优化。

Method: 开发了一套针对现代CPU架构（x86、ARM、RISC-V）的通用汇编微基准测试框架，覆盖了从标量到各种向量扩展（ISA extensions）以及所有内存层级的性能特性；同时结合性能计数器（Performance Counters）和动态二进制插桩（Dynamic Binary Instrumentation）技术实现应用特征分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，该自动化框架构建的CARM上限值（roofs）在各种测试架构下均表现出不到1%的偏差，验证了其高可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥补了当前CARM工具在非Intel架构上的空白，证明了通过通用微基准测试方法可以高精度地为不同CPU架构与ISA构建Roofline模型，且偏差极小。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [173] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于$d$维环面拓扑的高效MPI全对全通信算法，通过通信域分解与MPI派生数据类型实现了“零拷贝”的数据重排，提升了高性能计算环境下的通信性能与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用MPI_Alltoall实现可能无法在特定$d$维环面拓扑结构中达到最优性能，作者旨在通过算法定制和拓扑适配来提升通信效率。

Method: 1. 利用笛卡尔通信域（Cartesian communicator）将$p$个进程分解为$d$个维度特定的通信域；2. 将All-to-all操作分解为一系列维度上的MPI_Alltoall；3. 使用MPI派生数据类型（Derived Datatypes）实现隐含的数据重排，达成“零拷贝”；4. 引入双缓冲机制（double-buffering）管理内存。

Result: 实验结果表明，该实现与原生MPI_Alltoall表现相当，且在特定场景下能暴露并克服原生实现可能存在的问题。

Conclusion: 该算法在$d$维环面上实现了性能竞争力的全对全（all-to-all）通信，且通过零拷贝（zero-copy）技术有效提升了效率，为高性能计算下特定拓扑的优化提供了参考。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [174] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RaFI是一个基于CUDA和MPI的框架，旨在简化跨GPU的工作项迁移开发，自动处理复杂的底层通信与数据同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在构建需要跨多个GPU进行光线追踪或类似任务迁移的分布式数据并行应用时，直接使用CUDA和MPI进行底层开发过于复杂且繁琐。

Method: 基于CUDA和MPI构建的软件框架，通过提供简化的编程接口，在底层自动管理跨GPU的数据传输与通信（CUDA核函数工作项转发）。

Result: 实现了RaFI框架，并通过多个应用示例展示了其在处理多GPU工作项迁移场景中的有效性和潜力。

Conclusion: RaFI有效地简化了跨GPU的工作项（如光线）迁移开发，为构建高性能、跨设备的GPU并行软件提供了一个可行且易用的抽象框架。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Q-ANCHOR: Federated Quantum Learning with ZNE-guided Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30075)
*Hoang M. Ngo,Quan Nguyen,Wanli Xing,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对量子联邦学习中由非IID数据和硬件噪声导致的“双重漂移”问题，本文提出了Q-ANCHOR架构，通过集成零噪声外推和客户端修正机制来提升训练稳定性和模型准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习（QFL）受到“双重漂移”现象制约：一方面是由于数据非独立同分布（non-IID）引起的客户端漂移，另一方面是量子硬件产生的噪声梯度估计偏差。传统的FedAvg无法消除这些偏差，导致模型训练出现持续的误差下限。

Method: 提出了Q-ANCHOR架构，结合了零噪声外推（Zero-Noise Extrapolation, ZNE）来锚定服务器更新，并利用有状态的客户端修正（Stateful Client Correction）机制来抑制客户端漂移和量子硬件引入的偏差。

Result: 实验结果表明，Q-ANCHOR显著优于现有的联邦学习基准，在存在硬件噪声和数据异质性的情况下，实现了更稳健的训练收敛。理论推导也证实了其在缓解客户端漂移和降低硬件偏差下限方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Q-ANCHOR通过引入零噪声外推和有状态客户端修正机制，有效地解决了量子联邦学习中由非独立同分布（non-IID）数据导致的客户端漂移和量子硬件噪声导致的偏差问题，不仅降低了误差下限，还实现了比传统FedAvg更稳定的训练表现。

Abstract: Quantum Federated Learning (QFL) offers a promising framework to train quantum models across distributed clients while keeping data strictly local. Due to its simplicity and low communication overhead, Federated Averaging (FedAvg) is the standard aggregation choice in QFL literature. However, deploying QFL on practical hardware exposes a severe double-drift phenomenon: the global model is simultaneously derailed by client drift from non-IID data and hardware bias from noisy quantum gradient estimates. In this work, we first analyze the convergence of FedAvg under these realistic conditions, mathematically demonstrating that quantum hardware bias creates a persistent error floor that standard averaging cannot correct. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-ANCHOR, a quantum-aware federated aggregation architecture that anchors server updates with zero-noise extrapolation while applying stateful client correction to suppress both client drift and hardware-induced bias. Our convergence theory proves that Q-ANCHOR successfully mitigates classical client drift while actively reducing the hardware-bias floor. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-ANCHOR achieves significantly more stable training than conventional FL baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MBRL的“模型开发”问题，本文提出了一种鲁棒性驱动的对抗博弈学习方法，通过解决Error-MDP对偶性，实现了仿真到现实的高效策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于预测误差最小化的模型基强化学习（MBRL）容易受到优化器的利用，导致模拟模型存在微小误差，进而造成策略在仿真中表现优异但在现实世界中失败（现实差距）。

Method: 提出了一种基于零和极小极大博弈（minimax game）的框架，将模拟器学习建模为模型玩家与对抗策略玩家的博弈；利用Error-MDP对偶性开发了一种主动数据选择算法。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，将战略重要区域的预测误差降低了1.5-2.2倍，并使仅在仿真中训练的策略在真实世界中达到了近乎最优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型学习从专注于预测准确度转变为专注于策略鲁棒性，克服了模型基强化学习中的模拟器利用问题（Simulator Exploitation），实现了仿真策略在真实环境下的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [177] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了随机决策理论在线学习在纯差分隐私下的最优遗憾界计算问题，提出了一种无视界算法，实现了$O(\frac{\log K}{\Delta_{\min}} + \frac{\log K}{\epsilon})$的遗憾边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Hu和Mehta提出的一项关于随机决策理论在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私（event-level pure DP）约束下的最优遗憾率（gap-dependent regret rate）的公开问题，特别是改善现有界限并实现与$T$无关的遗憾界。

Method: 提出了一种无视界纯差分隐私算法，该算法将时间划分为呈指数增长的块，在每个块内只执行单一动作；通过在每个块的前缀应用指数机制选择下一个动作，利用随机前缀将块遗憾转化为Softmax选择误差的求和，并结合熵势（entropy-potential）分析来控制隐私相关的遗憾项。

Result: 证明了对于任意视界$T$，该算法的遗憾上界为$O(\frac{\log K}{\Delta_{\min}} + \frac{\log K}{\epsilon})$，达到了理论上的最优量级。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建一种基于指数增长块分区和指数机制的无视界（horizon-free）算法，成功解决了Hu和Mehta提出的关于纯差分隐私（pure-DP）下随机决策理论在线学习最优遗憾界限的公开问题。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [178] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol 引入“晚期交互”机制，通过残基级的细粒度比对代替传统的单一向量池化，有效提升了蛋白质远程同源性检索的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的序列比对方法在“暮光区”（同源性微弱的区域）灵敏度不足；现有的基于蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的检索通常会将整个蛋白质池化为单一向量，导致丢失关键的局部基序（motifs）或结构域信息。

Method: 提出 ProtoCol 模型：1. 将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入集合；2. 采用 ColBERT 风格的“晚期交互”（late interaction）机制进行比对；3. 使用 MaxSim 算子计算候选蛋白质残基之间的匹配得分；4. 支持预计算候选蛋白质嵌入，保证检索效率。

Result: 在 SCOPe 超家族和 Pfam 家族基准测试中，ProtoCol 在同源性查询性能上超越了序列组成比对、基于序列比对的方法、池化 PLM 方法以及训练过的单一向量基准方法。

Conclusion: “Late Interaction”机制（即基于残基级嵌入的比较）是提升远程同源性搜索精度的有效方案，优于传统的序列比对及现有的池化（pooled）嵌入方法。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [179] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将仅注意力Transformer视为两阶段经验贝叶斯模型的理论框架，证明了其深度动力学等同于一种无显式密度建模的样本后验推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的Transformer模型通常被视为复杂的黑盒，缺乏对自注意力机制和深度在统计推理层面上的清晰机理理解，特别是如何通过在位（in-context）计算实现有效的去噪和推理。

Method: 通过在全标记损坏（all-token corruption）下分析最小化仅注意力（attention-only）Transformer，构建了两阶段经验贝叶斯框架。利用粒子动力学模拟深度演化，并结合长期残差连接实现后验推理。通过统计分析在固定核带宽和有限积分区间下的去噪行为，并建立在特定先验下的后验均值恢复保证。

Result: 研究表明，深度（Depth）负责通过粒子动力学优化分布，而跳跃连接（Skip-connection）则作为后验推理的查询。证明了在特定条件下，模型能够实现无需显式噪声调度的有效去噪，并达到了贝叶斯最优预测器的渐近收敛保证。

Conclusion: 注意力机制被解释为一种基于样本的上下文后验估计，且无需显式建模密度。该研究揭示了注意力模型通过类扩散的粒子动力学实现自动去噪，为理解Transformer的深度与自注意力机制提供了统计学习视角。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [180] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究发现，神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α并非定值，而是受优化器选择显著影响。研究表明，采用预处理优化器（如自然梯度）可大幅提升缩放指数，从而获得更优的 Scaling 效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的缩放定律将指数α视为架构和数据的固有属性，缺乏对优化器如何影响缩放效率及缩放指数的系统性研究，作者旨在探究优化器在缩放行为中的作用。

Method: 在随机特征回归（random-feature regression）框架下，通过受控实验，对比了五种优化器变体在六种不同谱条件（spectral conditions）下的缩放表现，并测量了指数α的变化。

Result: 预处理优化器（如自然梯度）能显著增大缩放指数α。例如，在谱指数s≈1.0时，自然梯度的α≈0.31，远高于梯度下降的α≈0.12，这意味着模型规模每翻倍一次，性能差异会产生复合增长。这种优势在谱指数接近1.5时达到峰值。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α并非是架构和数据的固定常数，而是高度依赖于优化器的选择。研究引入了一种光谱诊断工具，能够预测高级优化器在特定数据特征下的提升效果，强调了在缩放预测中必须考虑优化器因素。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>


### [181] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 尽管马尔可夫边界理论上是特征选择的“圣杯”，但在实际 tabular 学习中，由于因果发现算法的瓶颈及结构优化与预测目标的不一致，简单的先发现边界后建模流水线效果不仅难以超越全特征集，且效率低下。需要转向以预测为导向的特征选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马尔可夫边界理论上是特征选择的最优目标（实现条件独立且信息充分），但现代机器学习回归任务中往往仍使用全特征集。研究旨在量化“马尔可夫边界”在实际 tabular 预测任务中的真实增益及其实施障碍。

Method: 在包含40到1000个特征、涵盖6种结构因果模型（SCM）系列的3,450个合成任务（SCM3K）上，对比了六种回归模型在全特征集与Oracle（真实）马尔可夫边界下的表现，并评估了现有因果发现算法在实际预测流水线中的效能。

Result: 1. 使用Oracle定义的边界显著提升了预测性能，特别是在高维稀疏数据中；2. 现有的因果发现算法难以达到该理论增益，原因在于计算开销大、结构恢复与预测目标不匹配，以及假阳性/假阴性对预测带来的非对称影响。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界理论上是有效的，但在实际应用中，由于因果发现算法的局限性（计算消耗大、预测对结构错误不敏感、结构优化与预测目标不一致）以及“特征冗余与协同”的复杂性，直接使用发现的边界并不总是最优策略。未来的研究应聚焦于“以预测为导向”的特征选择方法，而非盲目追求完美的因果结构恢复。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [182] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LoopFM 框架，通过将基础大模型的中间表征作为下游模型的输入特征，解决了传统蒸馏方法信息迁移受限的问题，实现了更高效的知识传递与业务性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识蒸馏（KD）通过标量预测进行迁移，在处理大模型（FM）向小模型（VM）迁移时存在“迁移率递减”问题，无法充分捕捉 FM 学习到的丰富中间特征。

Method: 提出了 LoopFM（Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM）框架，将 FM 的中间嵌入（embeddings）结构化为 VM 的输入特征（例如用户历史序列）；在理论上提供了增益分解和迁移率分析，且无需在线实时推理或架构耦合。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试上 AUC 提升显著（如 TaobaoAd 提升 6%+）；在工业级超大规模系统中，与 KD 配合使用时，知识迁移率翻倍，并在实际业务中实现了 0.5% 至 1.22% 的转化率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM 有效克服了传统知识蒸馏（KD）中单标量预测带来的信息瓶颈，通过将基础模型（FM）的历史中间表征作为垂直模型（VM）的输入特征，实现了更高效的知识迁移，在学术与工业规模场景中均表现出显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [183] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文引入 LoRA-Curve 方法，通过在 LoRA 参数空间构建分段贝塞尔曲线，实现了多个独立最优解的连续连接，有效捕捉了功能多样性并优化了不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 LoRA 微调难以进行原则性认知不确定性估计；现有的离散式集成方法在 LoRA 空间中效果有限，且尚未研究 LoRA 空间中是否存在类似标准深度学习中的“连接低损失谷”结构以实现功能多样性。

Method: 提出了 LoRA-Curve 方法，这是一种在 LoRA 参数空间中基于分段贝塞尔曲线（Bézier curves）的参数化技术。包含“自由配置（free configuration）”和“锚定配置（anchored configuration）”两种变体，并结合了平坦极小值扰动（flat-minima perturbations）和 Jensen-Shannon 散度正则化器。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 7B 模型上的实验表明，线性插值存在损失屏障（loss barriers），而 LoRA-Curve（特别是锚定配置）能成功连接独立的最优解，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升了预测分布的互信息（Mutual Information）。

Conclusion: 研究证实了通过 LoRA-Curve 构建的参数空间路径能够有效桥接独立的 LoRA 最优解（低损失谷），并能通过连续的参数遍历实现更高的预测功能多样性，从而克服了传统离散集成方法的局限性。
