Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [184] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $s$-稀疏设定下的上下文赌博机问题，设计了两种算法并实现了接近最优的样本复杂度 $\tilde{O}((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log|\Pi|/\delta)$，消除了先前研究中昂贵的动作空间依赖，并将其推广至组合半赌博机场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决 $s$-稀疏奖励设定（$L_1$-范数受限）下，上下文赌博机在有效识别近似最优策略方面的样本复杂度问题，并填补现有研究在算法效率上的巨大差距。

Method: 使用了两种互补的方法：1. 基于决策估计系数（DEC）的探索优化算法（信息论视角）；2. 基于低方差探索技术的特定算法（计算上可实现，并可扩展至组合半赌博机）。

Result: 获得了 $O((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log|\Pi|/\delta)$ 的样本复杂度界限，并给出了匹配的下界，证明了该结果的近乎最优性。此外，将方法扩展至上下文组合半赌博机，改进了多类列表分类的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了上下文赌博机（Contextual Bandits）在 $s$-稀疏奖励设定下的样本复杂度问题，通过理论推导与算法设计，消除了先前研究中依赖 $|A|^9$ 的限制，并提供了近乎最优的样本复杂度界限。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [185] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CB-SLICE，一种基于概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的错误切片发现方法。它通过分析导致模型决策失败的概念预测错误，实现了对模型错误来源的精确识别和解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的错误切片发现方法（SDM）通常无法解释模型推理的深层原因，导致生成的错误解释不够准确；作者旨在利用 CBM 中人类可理解的概念表示来精确定位错误来源。

Method: 引入了 CB-SLICE 方法，该方法基于概念瓶颈模型（CBM），通过聚类具有相似概念预测失败的样本来定义错误切片，并识别出导致这些错误的关键概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，CB-SLICE 在发现已知偏差方面优于现有最先进的方法，并且提供了更丰富、更忠实于模型内部逻辑的错误解释。

Conclusion: CB-SLICE 通过利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的可解释概念空间，克服了传统切片发现方法（SDM）与模型推理过程脱节的弊端，为识别模型系统性错误提供了更准确、更具可解释性的方案。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [186] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对民航复诵异常监控难题，论文提出了 SCOPE 框架，通过结合轻量化 LLM 与上下文学习机制，实现了高精度、低延迟且具备可解释性的飞行员指令复诵实时监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民航飞行员对空中交通管制（ATC）指令的复诵异常是导致航空事故的主要原因；传统规则或机器学习模型难以处理复杂多变的通话规范，而现有的大模型解决方案存在计算成本高、难以实际部署的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SCOPE 的轻量化 LLM 框架：1. 核心是利用冻结的 LLM 进行语义推理；2. 集成外挂式（Plug-in）开放集分类器；3. 采用精心设计的上下文学习（In-Context Learning）机制来处理复杂多变的航管指令。

Result: 在半合成通信数据集上，SCOPE 在少样本（few-shot）设置下实现了 91.05% 的开放集检测准确率，并能纠正 96.63% 的异常复诵，同时具备低延迟响应和决策解释能力，优于现有基准模型。

Conclusion: SCOPE 框架通过结合外挂式开放集分类器与上下文学习（ICL），在 ATC 复诵监控任务中实现了高准确度、低延迟与可解释性，为航空交通管理的实时辅助提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [187] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了深度学习在处理离散代数任务时的局限性，提出以“凯莱表补全”为基准，研究如何通过引入平坦性先验来构建系统性的算法归纳偏置，从而使深度学习能够自主发现离散算法公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代统计学习理论和深度学习主要基于连续性容量控制（如正则化、边缘最大化），但在外推精确算法或离散代数规则方面表现不佳，反映了模型缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出以凯莱表补全（Cayley-table completion）作为研究深度学习离散代数归纳偏置的基准测试；利用算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验（flatness prior）来诱导对离散结合律的归纳偏置。

Result: 初步研究表明，算子值张量分解与平坦性先验的结合，能够为深度学习模型赋予实现精确离散结合律的隐式算法偏置。

Conclusion: 当前的深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置，无法有效处理离散代数规则。研究者呼吁建立凯莱表补全的精确恢复界，并提出开发能自动发现离散算法公理的通用平坦性先验。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [188] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机重置动力学与岭回归（Ridge Regression）之间的理论桥梁，证明了泊松重置产生标准 ridge 解，并揭示了更一般的重置过程如何产生新型的谱滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非平衡统计物理中的随机重置机制与统计学习中岭正则化（Ridge Regularization）之间的深层数学联系，试图通过动力学视角解释正则化行为，并推广至更一般的重置律。

Method: 1. 将统计物理中的随机重置（Stochastic Resetting）框架应用于线性梯度流；2. 利用拉普拉斯变换对比 ridge 回归的解析解；3. 通过 renewal 理论推广重置分布；4. 引入 OU 过程对 SGD 进行算子平滑逼近分析。

Result: 1. 证明了重置率为 r 的泊松重置过程产生的稳态均值等价于惩罚参数为 λ=r 的岭估计；2. 确定了指数分布是唯一能精确再现常规岭回归谱滤波器的更新律；3. 指出了在包含 Langevin 噪声的 SGD 场景中，虽然均值行为一致，但由于噪声积累，协方差结构与确定性的 ridge 估计存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 研究证明了随机重置过程与岭回归（Ridge Regression）在数学上的等价性：在线性梯度流中，泊松重置产生岭估计，而非指数重置则会生成不同的谱滤波器；此外，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程分析了随机梯度下降（SGD）带来的噪声影响。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [189] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RTE通过学习任务与任务之间的变换关系（Relational Transformation），使模型能够将已知任务的知识迁移至未见过的任务，实现了在分布外场景下的系统化外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统擅长内插（Interpolation），但在处理未见过的任务（即分布外外推）时表现较差，即便使用预训练大模型也难以解决参数超出训练范围、组合深度增加等外推难题。

Method: 提出关系任务外推器（Relational Task Extrapolator, RTE）。核心机制是将目标任务解构为“锚定任务（Anchor Task）”与“变换（Transformation）”，学习一个关系操作符，通过将已学到的任务间转换逻辑应用到锚定任务上，从而推导未见过的目标任务。

Result: 在函数预测（参数、长度、组合外推）和序列预测（基于基础模型的微调）的多项实验中，RTE显著超越了现有的外推方法。

Conclusion: RTE提供了一种通过学习任务间变换关系来实现系统化外推的有效方法，弥补了现有模型在分布外（OOD）任务上的泛化缺陷，在函数和序列预测中均表现优异。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [190] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模的机器学习事后校准标准化基准与评估框架，通过近2000个实验量化分析了现有方法的性能，并引入PHI指标以更全面地评估校准质量及预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模型普遍存在校准不佳的问题；现有校准方法过多且评估缺乏统一标准，导致难以确定最佳实践。

Method: 构建了一个包含表格和计算机视觉任务、跨越近2000个实验的大规模标准化基准；集成了多种经典模型、深度学习架构及基础模型；提出了使用“事后改进指标（PHI）”来替代传统的校准误差估计器，用于评估模型校准质量及预测性能。

Result: 完成了迄今为止最全面的事后校准实证研究，揭示了跨领域的校准规律，并开源了相关代码、数据和评估工具。

Conclusion: 平滑校准函数优于基于分箱的方法；在超大规模设置下，专门的多分类校准方法至关重要；通用机器学习模型若无针对性的校准设计，表现不佳。提出PHI作为评估校准方法的原则性指标。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [191] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过结构化数据描述符和正则化典型相关分析（CCA）来实现异构数值数据集跨数据集对齐与相似性度量的新方法，无需共享变量名即可在保持统计上下文的同时整合异构数据，并支持差分隐私，适用于 RAG 管道及数据驱动的算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型缺乏有效表征异构特征空间下数值型表格数据集的原生机制，而现有方法要么局限于单一数据集的预测建模，要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐手段。

Method: 通过结构化探索性数据分析（EDA）描述符表征数据集，利用预训练句子转换器将描述符嵌入共享向量空间，并使用正则化典型相关分析（CCA）量化跨数据集相似性，以发现稀疏、可解释的可变级对应关系。此外，支持在嵌入前应用差分隐私以保护敏感数据。

Result: 在涵盖通用基准、材料信息学和核级石墨表征的15个数据集上进行了评估，总 P@1 得分达到 0.9，且在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下，最近邻检索和聚类结构保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该框架为将异构数值数据集有效整合进检索增强生成（RAG）流程提供了一个具有原则性的路径，同时保留了统计上下文，在算法选择和模拟模型初始化方面具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [192] [Momentum Based Reward Design for Low Emission Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29693)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于深度强化学习的动量式交通信号控制奖励函数（MBRF），在不改变基础设施的前提下，能有效改善城市交通状况，提升系统稳定性并降低车辆排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决城市交通拥堵及由此产生的环境问题，并克服现有基于延迟和队列长度的DRL交通控制奖励函数导致策略短视、不稳定等局限性。

Method: 提出了一种基于动量的奖励函数（Momentum-Based Reward Function, MBRF），该函数通过鼓励车辆持续移动而非仅仅惩罚拥堵来优化交通；并在SUMO仿真平台中进行了对比验证。

Result: MBRF模型在平均等待时间、队列长度、吞吐量和CO2排放等指标上均优于基准方法，展现出更优的吞吐量-排放权衡及更稳定的学习收敛表现。

Conclusion: 基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF）在城市交通信号控制中表现优于传统的延迟/队列奖励函数，且优于Max Pressure和LQF等经典控制器，实现了更好的吞吐量与排放平衡，并提升了DRL的训练稳定性。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion is a growing global issue contributing significantly to long commute times and environmental pollution. Traditional traffic signal control systems often fail to adapt to dynamic traffic conditions. Adaptive traffic signal control can improve urban traffic without changing road infrastructure. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong performance for this task, but existing delay and queue-based rewards often produce short-sighted or unstable policies. This paper proposes a Momentum-Based Reward Function (MBRF) that encourages vehicles to keep moving rather than penalizing congestion alone. The method is evaluated in SUMO (Simulation of Urban MObility) using standard traffic metrics such as waiting time, queue length, throughput, and CO2 emissions. Results show that the proposed reward produces better throughput-emission trade-offs and more stable learning behavior than delay or queue-based rewards, as well as classical controllers such as Max Pressure and LQF.

</details>


### [193] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示ROME与MEMIT等知识编辑方法并非真正“覆盖”知识，而是通过抑制后续层的过度注意力来发挥作用，这种机制解释了为何编辑难以泛化，并为模型安全防御提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要从模型输出行为层面进行评估，缺乏对其修改底层参数（MLP权重）内在机制的深刻理解，且不清楚不同事实修改是否依赖于共同的机制。

Method: 研究人员针对ROME和MEMIT修改的MLP权重训练了一个紧凑的二进制掩码（binary mask），通过观察掩码对编辑效果的逆转作用来定位关键权重参数，并分析该机制的行为特征。

Result: 1. 发现不同事实的编辑共享一个共同的功能子空间（通过掩码可移除80%的编辑）。2. 编辑的核心机制是抑制后续层的过度注意力。3. 在编辑过程中引入该掩码会导致编辑成功率从98%大幅下降至38%，证明了该机制的必要性。

Conclusion: 知识编辑方法（ROME/MEMIT）并非通过覆盖原有知识，而是通过抑制后续层中的“过度注意力”（overattention）来实现编辑。这一发现揭示了这些方法无法传播相关事实知识的原因，并为检测和防御恶意模型编辑提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [194] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用TradeArena平台分析了LLM金融交易智能体在市场压力下的行为表现，发现了一套能在预测模型决策失败前的“预警特征”，并探讨了结构化风险反馈对模型对齐的作用及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在金融决策环境下的行为对齐与表示动力学，特别是如何在市场压力下检测其逻辑推理的偏移与潜在失效。

Method: 利用TradeArena测试平台，结合内存、回放机制及风险报告功能，通过对80个滚动失败锚点进行分析；采用多种嵌入探测技术（Hash, LSA, Transformer, 白盒隐藏状态）；通过CoT消融、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声注入及伪审计报告进行压力测试；并引入滚动Markowitz基准进行相关性分析。

Result: 研究识别出金融失败前的可测量特征：规划嵌入从正常状态中心漂移、计划与风险表示的分离以及失效前的有效秩（Effective-rank）收缩。实验发现结构化风险反馈可作为无微调的对齐信号，但也存在模型校准差异；同时指出LLM在处理资产组合相关性时存在逻辑盲区。

Conclusion: 审计风险反馈和表示轨迹能够有效揭示LLM金融推理的对齐、偏移或失效状态，而非直接作为盈利工具。研究发现，虽然风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号，但其绩效影响因模型和场景而异，且LLM在处理资产相关性时存在明显的“盲点”。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [195] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入“差异化电路脆弱性”指标，从机制层面证明了RL相比SFT能更有效地保留大语言模型的内部计算电路，从而在缓解灾难性遗忘方面表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨为何强化学习（RL）在微调大语言模型时比监督微调（SFT）更能保留原有能力，并试图从模型内部“计算电路”的机制层面验证这一行为现象。

Method: 提出了“差异化电路脆弱性”（differential circuit vulnerability）指标，用于量化头级（head-level）电路在微调过程中的退化程度；对比了在科学问答任务上，使用强化学习（RL）与监督微调（SFT）对 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 模型的影响。

Result: SFT虽能更快适应目标任务，但会导致严重的电路破坏和能力遗忘；RL虽然任务适应较慢，但能保留更高比例的基座模型电路。

Conclusion: RL在微调过程中展现出更优的电路保存能力，这是其相比SFT能更有效缓解灾难性遗忘的机制解释。该研究揭示了任务适应速度与模型内部计算电路稳定性之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [196] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 LLM 的智能体 TRACE，通过引入序列决策思维来优化药物先导化合物，有效解决了传统单步优化方法在长期设计决策上的局限性，在 ADMET 属性优化任务中取得了更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的药物发现先导优化（lead optimization）多依赖于单步分子优化方法，忽略了决策链的长期影响，难以在维持结合亲和力的同时有效实现 ADMET 属性的微调与改善。

Method: 提出 TRACE（Trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning Agent），其核心在于将分子优化过程建模为序列决策任务。利用大语言模型（LLM）作为推理内核，根据当前分子状态和优化目标，在复杂的工具空间内进行“前瞻性”的工具选择和轨迹规划，从而在满足结构约束的同时优化 ADMET 属性。

Result: 在多次 ADMET 优化任务中，TRACE 表现出比基线模型更高的优化成功率、更显著的属性提升以及更好的分子有效性，同时在保持分子骨架相似性方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: TRACE 框架证实了引入轨迹感知（trajectory-aware）和多步决策机制能够显著提升分子先导化合物的优化效果，证明了将分子设计视为顺序决策过程优于传统的单步优化方法。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [197] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过开发大老二游戏AI框架，证明了PPO在处理非完全信息多人博弈中的优越性，并指出适度的熵正则化及当前策略自我博弈是提升训练效果的关键要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完全信息的多人博弈游戏（如大老二）具有隐藏信息、稀疏奖励及非静态对手等挑战，是评估人工智能决策能力的极佳环境。现有的深度强化学习方法在处理这些复杂约束时的有效性和对比研究尚不充分。

Method: 构建了一个在大老二（Big 2）多人纸牌游戏中的自我博弈（Self-play）强化学习框架，对比了PPO（策略梯度）与Monte Carlo Q近似、SARSA、Q-learning（价值近似）等算法，同时探索了熵正则化和不同自我博弈课程学习策略（current-policy vs. checkpoint/fixed-opponent）的影响。

Result: 在相同的环境参数、输入表示及训练预算下，PPO算法表现显著优于Monte Carlo Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning。实验还证明了：(1) 适度的熵正则化有助于避免策略过早收敛至确定性；(2) “当前策略自我博弈”比“检查点自博弈”或“固定对手训练”更适合有限预算下的课程学习。

Conclusion: 大老二（Big 2）是研究深度强化学习在非完全信息、多人博弈、稀疏奖励及动态行动空间下表现的理想基准测试平台。PPO算法在自我博弈框架下，结合适度的熵正则化和当前策略自我博弈（current-policy self-play），在处理此类复杂任务时展现出比传统价值近似方法更优的性能。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [198] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究表明，无需语言监督，世界模型仅通过物理探索即可习得与物理几何一致的潜在语义结构，且预测能力的提升与语义结构的形成互为因果，共同驱动模型的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界模型在没有任何语言监督的情况下，仅通过物理探索学习到了什么。作者认为，世界模型的学习过程是由物理世界的几何结构这一单一原则所组织的。

Method: 1. 模型架构：使用基于变分自编码器（VAE）的世界模型。2. 数据获取：通过随机具身探索（random embodied exploration）收集数据。3. 分析评估：使用方向准确度评估和表征相似性分析（RSA）衡量潜在空间的结构；通过Spearman相关性分析研究预测性能与语义对齐的演变；利用“双敲除”（double knockout）实验，通过调整KL正则化系数（beta）来验证预测性能与几何结构组织之间的因果关联。

Result: 1. 训练后的模型潜在空间呈现出与物理几何镜像对应的空间语义结构（方向准确度0.677，位置RSA 0.192，显著优于初始编码器）。2. 预测误差与语义对齐程度呈强相关（r=-0.61），支持共享驱动假设。3. 实验证明KL正则化过强会破坏几何结构，导致预测与语义能力同时崩溃；降低正则化则可恢复上述能力。

Conclusion: 物理世界的几何结构是世界模型表征的核心组织原则。预测能力的提升与语义表征的对齐具有相关性（共享驱动机制），这一发现为设计具有语义基础的具身智能体提供了理论依据。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [199] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对TS-LLMs忽略时序本质特征的问题，本文提出了COM策略，通过在嵌入层引入几何约束来保留时序数据的连续性与序数性，显著增强了模型的分析与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Token的时间序列大模型在处理时序数据时忽视了时序数据本质的“连续性”和“序数性”，这导致了模型表示能力的局限，限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出了一种名为COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）的策略。该方法通过在模型初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束，将时间序列的连续性和序数性属性纳入Token嵌入表示中。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准测试中，使用COM策略的TS-LLMs在各项指标上均表现出一致的性能提升，且具有更强的通用性和竞争力。

Conclusion: 在时间序列大模型（TS-LLMs）中，显式地建模和保护时间序列数据的连续性与序数性对于提升模型性能和泛化能力至关重要。COM策略为实现这一目标提供了一种有效且通用的方法。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [200] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow 引入 Koopman 启发下的动态专家混合机制和 WTA 学习策略，克服了流匹配在处理复杂时间序列时的过度平滑问题，显著提升了生成数据的质量及高频细节保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配（FM）方法在处理多模态、多尺度时间序列时，受限于单一全局向量场估计器，导致不同动态模式间产生“过度平滑”效应，进而引起谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出 PrismFlow 框架，结合 Koopman 算子理论构建动态专家系统（学习潜在空间线性残差修正），并设计自信度感知的 Winner-Take-All (WTA) 目标函数，实现专家间的模式特异性分配，采样时通过全局场加残差项方式保持稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现卓越，Context-FID 提升 15.6%，判决得分（Discriminative Score）提升 38.6%，且在低数据场景下具有鲁棒性，适用于预测和填充任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow 通过引入专家混合机制和自信度感知的 WTA 优化目标，有效解决了传统流匹配模型在生成复杂时间序列时因过度平滑导致的谱失真问题，实现了时间序列生成性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [201] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中的模态失衡问题，研究提出一种通过重塑标签空间（BMLR）来平衡不同模态学习难度的新方法，有效提升了多模态模型的整体性能与协同效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中存在模态失衡问题（即收敛速度差异），现有方法侧重于调整优化梯度而非解决本质的映射难度差异，且往往会牺牲强模态的优化表现。作者认为失衡源于模态特征空间到标签空间的映射难度差异。

Method: 提出“平衡多模态标签重塑”（Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping, BMLR），通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡不同模态的映射难度，从而在训练中引入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的广泛实验表明，BMLR 能持续提升多模态模型的性能，并与现有的多种模型设计表现出良好的兼容性。

Conclusion: BMLR 通过从标签空间视角解决模态学习进度不平衡问题，提供了一种高效且通用性强的多模态学习优化方案，证明了从标签侧设计出发能够有效促进模态间的平衡与协作。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [202] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过“信号-偏差-噪声”三元框架验证柏拉图表征假设，证明了线性特征提取的有效性、几何预处理对降低偏差的重要性，以及数据分布稀疏性对表征对齐噪声的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究“柏拉图表征假设”（PRH），即不同模型学习到的表征是否趋向于某种通用的真实世界表示。作者试图通过分解表征的构成要素（信号、偏差、噪声）来解析模型对齐的潜在机制。

Method: 1. 信号：利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取线性目标-属性特征；
2. 偏差：采用中心化（Centering）和归一化（Normalization）技术消除模型间架构与训练差异；
3. 噪声：分析词频与表征对齐度的统计相关性，量化数据稀疏性带来的影响。

Result: 1. 线性特征（LRH）是推动柏拉图对齐的核心要素，稀疏化后的表示比稠密向量更具跨模态对齐能力；
2. 几何变换（中心化和归一化）能有效降低模型偏差带来的对齐干扰；
3. 表征噪声与训练数据稀疏性高度相关，词频越高的概念，模型对其表征的对齐程度越高。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个融合信号、偏差与噪声的统计模型，该模型完善了线性表征假设（LRH），并为解释现代AI架构中表征对齐现象提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [203] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种量化基准测试的样本量规划工具，通过推导MDE边界，说明了大部分量化评估差异源于抽样噪声，并揭示了提示模板不确定性对基准测试结果的影响远大于量化本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型（如NF4）与原始模型（FP16）的基准测试结果差异经常被误解为模型性能的显著下降，导致评估结果不可靠。缺乏量化评估的预设计划导致评估成本与预算难以把控，且难以区分随机噪声、提示模板噪声与量化噪声。

Method: 基于Miettinen (1968) 的经典配对二元样本量计算法，推导出一套适用于量化基准测试的最小可检测效应（MDE）边界公式；并通过四种模型、四种基准测试的实验以及MMLU提示模板的对比研究，验证了该理论框架。

Result: 该MDE边界公式能有效转化为基准测试者的预算规划工具；实验显示大多数 observed NF4-FP16 差异落入二项式抽样噪声范围内；MMLU实验表明提示模板带来的方差（2-10 pp）远超量化产生的噪声（<3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: 基于样本量计算公式，量化审计中的大部分表现差异（如FP16与NF4之间的偏差）可以由二项随机抽样误差解释，而非模型本质的可靠性问题。此外，提示模板引起的不确定性往往超过了量化引起的偏差，提示模板的标准化对确保评估的有效性至关重要。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [204] [Towards Continuous-time Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28880)
*Dennis Thumm,Ruben Wiedemann,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间因果模型在观测时间依赖上的缺陷，提出了轨迹定律不变性，并验证了采用精细网格积分方法可有效提升模型在不规则采样及干预场景下的鲁棒性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散时间因果Prior-data Fitted Networks扩展到连续时间（SDE形式）时，若仅按观测间隔积分，其轨迹定律仍依赖于采样频率，导致其本质上仍为离散时间Markov模型，缺乏真正的连续时间特性。

Method: 提出“轨迹定律不变性”（trajectory-law invariance）作为连续时间因果建模的标准；构建了一个三层分类法，并实现了一种基于随机DAG（有向无环图）和OU过程或小规模MLP非线性漂移的架构，结合精细网格积分（fine-grid integration）与解耦观测机制。

Result: 通过消融实验发现，精细网格积分在所有8种测试场景下均优于朴素观测网格积分（显著性p < 1/256），且随着评估网格的细化，性能差距进一步扩大。此外，引入精细积分后，编码器的选择对性能影响减小，而朴素积分则高度依赖于编码器的时序感知能力。

Conclusion: 文章提出了轨迹定律不变性作为评估连续时间因果模型优劣的关键标准，并证明了精细网格积分方法在处理非均匀观测和干预时的表现显著优于传统的朴素积分方法。

Abstract: Extending discrete-time causal Prior-data Fitted Networks for time series to continuous time invites writing the mechanism as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) -- but if the SDE is integrated \emph{once per observation gap}, the trajectory law depends on when it is observed, and the prior remains a discrete-time Markov model in SDE clothing.
  We propose a precise continuity criterion -- trajectory-law invariance to the observation schedule -- together with a three-tier taxonomy (discrete; naive observation-grid integration; fine-grid integration with decoupled observation) and a construction realising the top tier on a random DAG with OU or small-MLP nonlinear drifts, irregular observation schedules, and hard / soft / time-varying interventions.
  A $2 \times 2$ encoder $\times$ integrator ablation, run independently on a linear and a nonlinear prior, finds fine-grid integration beats naive on 8/8 cells (sign-consistency $p < 1/256$) with the gap growing as the eval grid refines; the encoder axis is null with fine integration but time-aware-leading with naive.
  We release the prior and a preliminary zero-shot protocol on pharmacokinetic and physical-system data.

</details>


### [205] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究解决了个性化上下文蒸馏记忆的管理难题，提出了一种基于 модуляр LoRA 和自门控机制的高效知识检索与激活框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏出的潜在记忆如何在大规模应用场景中进行高效存储、精准检索以及安全激活的问题。

Method: 提出将上下文蒸馏视为潜在记忆管理问题，利用独立的LoRA适配器构建记忆库，引入“检索-路由-自门控（Self-Gating）”机制进行记忆选择与激活，并采用缓存共享（Cache Sharing）技术降低推理开销。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在检索任务中显著优于现有基线模型；自门控机制有效提升了模型的稳健性，通过抑制不必要的潜在记忆激活，保证了推理效率。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种有效的上下文蒸馏管理框架，通过模块化的LoRA适配器和自门控机制，实现了在大规模上下文场景下选择性、安全且高效的知识检索与激活。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [206] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）揭示了LoRA微调会在模型剩余流中引入新的、预训练特征字典无法捕捉的表示结构，提示在进行微调模型的可解释性研究时需建立专门的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LoRA微调如何改变大模型内部表示的几何结构尚不明确，制约了对微调模型的可解释性分析。

Method: 提出了“Delta激活框架”来分离LoRA对剩余流的贡献；利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在Gemma-2-9B模型上针对不同的LoRA秩（4, 8, 16, 32）训练专用SAE，并通过余弦相似度、主角分析及中心核对齐（CKA）进行对比分析。

Result: LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间的几何对齐度较弱；专用SAE比预训练SAE能更有效地重构Delta激活；特征密度随秩（Rank）和深度的增加而增加，而几何差异在不同秩下保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调在剩余流中形成了与预训练模型不同的特征结构，现有的可解释性字典（SAE）无法完全捕捉这些结构，这意味着在对微调模型进行安全审计和机制可解释性分析时，不能仅依赖预训练模型的可解释性工具。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [207] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spectral Guidance 提出了一种利用扩散模型内在几何特性进行引导的框架，通过自监督提取关键特征基底，实现了高效、高保真且无需额外反向传播的条件生成控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型控制方法通常依赖于训练、复杂的反向传播或高计算成本的采样过程，难以在保持生成高质量图像的同时实现灵活、快速的控制。由于去噪过程中只有少数特征对引导有效，作者寻求一种能够挖掘并利用这些特征的几何方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于条件期望算子奇异函数（singular functions）的框架。通过自监督学习提取出在去噪过程中具有代表性的特征基底，将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP 嵌入、掩膜）投影至去噪轨迹上，实现无需反向传播的引导。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上，条件准确率对比最强的无训练基线提升了 37 个百分点；采样速度提升了 4 倍；支持标签、CLIP 和空间掩膜等多模态控制。

Conclusion: Spectral Guidance 是一种高效且具备通用性的扩散模型控制框架。它通过识别并投影关键特征，在无需微调或反向传播的情况下，实现了比传统方法更高质量、更快速且具备空间感知能力的条件生成。此外，该框架揭示了扩散过程中的相位变换现象，为确定引导的最佳时机提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [208] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CosmicFish-HRM模型，通过分层推理模块实现推理深度自适应，使小型模型能够根据任务复杂度动态分配计算资源，在保持推理能力的同时优化计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然推理能力强，但参数规模巨大且推理成本高昂。现有研究侧重于扩大规模，本文旨在探索小型模型通过“自适应推理深度”来实现高效推理的潜力。

Method: 提出CosmicFish-HRM模型，核心为分层推理模块（HRM）。该机制通过高低层循环动态分配推理计算量，并根据输入复杂度确定终止条件，同时整合了分组查询注意力（GQA）、RoPE位置编码及SwiGLU激活函数。

Result: 实验表明模型能够学习非均匀的推理行为，即针对不同任务和输入自动分配不同数量的推理步骤。验证了通过计算动态分配提升推理效率的可行性。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度为提升小型语言模型的推理能力提供了一条有效的替代路径，无需过度依赖扩大参数规模。随着模型规模的增长，分层推理机制的成本效率将更具优势。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [209] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种事后解释方法在EEG抑郁症检测模型中的表现，发现模型关注的脑区与既往文献具有一定一致性，但不同解释方法之间的差异表明模型可解释性极大地依赖于方法论的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在EEG抑郁症分类中表现出色，但高容量模型缺乏可解释性（“黑盒”问题），限制了其在临床精神病学中的应用和验证。

Method: 基于InceptionTime架构构建EEG抑郁症（MDD）检测模型，并应用了五种事后（post-hoc）可解释性方法：DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion和Permutation Feature Importance，并在受试者层面的5折交叉验证框架下进行全局归因分析。

Result: 不同方法揭示了部分收敛的归因模式，即模型倾向于关注额叶、颞叶和后部区域（特别是右半球）。梯度驱动和扰动驱动的方法表现出较高的一致性，而DeepSHAP产生的分布差异较大。方法学假设显著影响了解释结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不同的事后解释方法在解释EEG抑郁症检测模型时既有重合也有差异。虽然观察到的神经生理模式与既往研究一致，但目前的解释方法尚不能作为确凿的临床生物标志物，强调了事后解释技术在精神病学应用中的潜力和局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [210] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督 fMRI 表征学习框架，通过利用正样本对有效解决小样本与高维数据下的过拟合问题，在降低算力需求的同时实现了优异的跨任务泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 fMRI 领域因数据采集成本高、标注主观性强导致的样本匮乏，以及数据高维性带来的过拟合风险，同时解决现有大模型预训练/微调算力需求过高的问题。

Method: 提出 BrainSimSiam 框架，这是一种基于正样本对（positive-only pairs）的自监督表征学习方法，旨在通过轻量级架构减少对大规模算力的依赖并提高特征的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: BrainSimSiam 在多种下游分类及回归任务中表现优异，证明了其在数据受限环境下的泛化能力和鲁棒性，性能优于完全监督的基线模型，且能媲美大规模模型。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam 作为一种轻量级、数据高效的自监督学习框架，能够有效缓解 fMRI 数据样本量小和标注质量不稳定的问题，在下游任务中实现了优于全监督基线并在性能上逼近大规模模型的表现，为资源受限的神经影像应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [211] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前LLM基准测试对改写（paraphrase）质量极度敏感且存在语义一致性（SCR）严重不足的问题。作者提出了FormInv框架及一系列评估指标，证明了现有排名体系在高鲁棒性要求下存在隐形偏差，并为稳健的模型评估提供了解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准测试未考虑改写质量的影响，导致语义含义等价但表述不同的题目（paraphrase）会导致模型表现不一致，且基准设计者的偏差会隐性操纵竞赛结果（No-Free-Benchmark corollary）。需要一种能够量化评估语义鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出FormInv研究框架，包含：1. 基于交叉模型一致性（unanimity）的改写审计协议；2. 利用语义一致性率（SCR）和Cochran's Q检验作为核心不变性衡量指标；3. 开发FormInvSelector进行情境感知的模型选择。该框架在Lean4验证过的定理集上对9个主流模型进行了评估。

Result: 1. 仅仅通过移除小部分（3.1%）语义错误的改写，即可大幅改变GPT-4o等模型的排名；2. 发现模型在语义等价的改写下表现差异巨大，SCR（语义一致性率）指标甚至存在50%-82%的巨大差距，而传统准确率却仅有86%-96%的差异；3. 证明了可以通过调整改写集合的权重来影响任何模型排名，揭示了基准测试缺乏鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 现有的通用基准测试容易受到改写（paraphrase）质量的影响，导致模型排名不稳定且不具有鲁棒性。研究提出了FormInv框架作为标准化的模型评估与审计方案，以捕捉语义一致性（SCR）问题，避免基准设计者的潜在偏差，从而实现更透明和稳健的模型排名。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [212] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe 是一种为扩散式组合优化求解器设计的无需训练封装器，通过动态路由关键交互，显著提升了大规模问题的推断速度与显存利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散（Diffusion-based）的组合优化神经求解器在处理复杂图结构时，因需要反复计算稠密的边/因子交互，导致推断成本高昂、效率低下，且在大规模场景下极易受限于显存容量。

Method: 提出一种名为 LoRe 的推断时 drop-in 包装器，核心在于“基于预算的动态交互评估”：在扩散模型的每一步迭代中，利用物理学中的多体处理思想，根据冲突度或不确定性指标，动态路由仅计算部分交互关系，而非依赖静态稀疏化方案。

Result: 在最大独立集（MIS）问题上，LoRe 实现了约 8 倍加速和 12 倍内存削减，并将可推断规模扩展至基线模型显存上限的 3 倍以上；在旅行商问题（TSP）上，于 n=1000 规模下实现约 15 倍加速和 44 倍内存转换，且解质量保持竞争优势。

Conclusion: LoRe 证明了一种无需训练的推断时动态路由机制，能够高效处理组合优化问题，在大幅降低显存占用和提升推断速度的同时，保持了与全量计算相当的解质量，并具有极佳的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [213] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST 是一种基于因果智能的通用框架，旨在通过学习结构因果模型并结合约束优化，设计出兼顾效率、复杂度和稳定性的系统状态转换干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测模型缺乏机制洞察力，且难以提供系统性的干预决策框架。研究人员需要一种能够通过针对性干预，将复杂系统从一个状态驱动到另一个状态的方法。

Method: COAST（Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions）框架：1. 学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型（SCM）；2. 将分布偏移归因于机制水平的因果驱动因素；3. 引入一种约束感知的多目标优化算法，平衡转换效率、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性；4. 模块化设计，支持跨领域的特征选择、因果推断和干预评估。

Result: 在合成基准测试和真实生物学数据集上，COAST 成功识别了关键因果驱动因素，并能生成稳健的单/多目标干预策略，同时提供了用于实验验证的透明机制解释。

Conclusion: COAST 为复杂系统的状态转换建模提供了一个通用、可解释且符合因果逻辑的决策框架。它不仅能发现因果驱动因素，还能在考虑干预成本、复杂度和系统稳定性的前提下，提出可落地的干预策略。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [214] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为CME（交叉模型熵）的无标签强化学习奖励机制，利用独立验证器模型评估生成器的回复质量，有效解决了传统RLHF中奖励获取困难、成本高昂或易被破解的问题，显著提升了大模型在开放式指令任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的RLHF受到奖励信号的限制：基于真值的奖励仅适用于受限领域，基于人类反馈的奖励成本高且易被破解，而现有的无标签自反馈方法（如多数投票）容易导致模型自我强化错误。需要一种更鲁棒的、适用于开放式任务的奖励信号。

Method: 提出Cross-Model Entropy (CME)方法，即计算生成器响应在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然作为奖励信号。将其集成到GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）框架中，在无需人工标签或显式验证器的情况下进行强化学习对齐。

Result: 在UltraFeedback提示词集上，在Qwen、Llama、Gemma和OLMo四个模型家族及三种训练状态下，使用CME奖励训练的模型在AlpacaEval 2.0中均表现优异，胜率区间为52.5%至71.4%，证明了其在开放领域指令遵循中的有效性。

Conclusion: CME是一种有效的无标签RL训练奖励信号，通过利用独立验证器模型进行交叉熵评估，能够显著提升大语言模型在开放式指令遵循任务中的表现，同时避免了自反馈方法中常见的奖励黑客行为和自我强化错误。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [215] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoMa QL 引入最大均值差异（MMD）实现矩匹配，消除了生成式模型在强化学习中的迭代采样需求，在提升计算效率的同时增强了模型在复杂任务中的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于得分（Score-based）和流（Flow-based）的生成模型虽然表达能力强，但因其迭代采样过程导致的推理延迟过高，成为强化学习（RL）中计算效率的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL) 框架：利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术匹配原分布与目标分布的所有阶矩统计量，对条件得分函数（Conditional Score Function）施加强正则化，从而在无需迭代采样的情况下实现分布对齐。

Result: 在多个 D4RL 基准测试中表现出与现有先进方法相当甚至更优的性能；在离线转在线（Offline-to-online）的 RL 任务中，由于加速了动作采样，该方法展现出更快的收敛速度和更强的在线交互微调适应能力。

Conclusion: MoMa QL 通过引入 MMD 正则化，有效解决了生成式模型在 RL 中的采样延迟难题，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了离线深度强化学习（Offline RL）的计算效率及在线微调的适应性。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [216] [OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089)
*Xinyu Liu,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Yang Zhou,Jindong Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OISD是一种新型强化学习自蒸馏框架，通过将最终层的推理模式和注意力模式引导至中间层，在无需额外辅助信息的情况下显著增强了模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL后训练方法大多仅关注最终输出策略的稀疏奖励，忽略了中间层表征中隐含的预测信号，限制了模型推理能力的进一步挖掘。

Method: 提出了“同策略内部自蒸馏”（OISD）框架。主要包含两个核心机制：通过逻辑对齐（Logit Alignment）迁移高层推理行为，以及通过注意力对齐（Attention Alignment）强制实现跨层注意力模式的一致性；并结合GRPO，采用符号优势加权的Jensen-Shannon散度进行优化。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准任务上，相比强基准模型（如GRPO），OISD展现出了显著且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: OISD通过利用模型自身的最终层作为内部教师，有效提升了中间表征的预测能力和推理性能，为大模型强化学习提供了一种无需外部监督的优化范式。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD

</details>


### [217] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将模型合并建模为凸二次规划（QP）的统一框架，不仅为模型融合提供了数学最优性保证，还通过残差能量诊断机制实现了对下游合并质量的精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如Task Arithmetic、TIES等）多基于启发式设计，缺乏形式化的最优性保证且对合并质量难以预测。

Method: 将模型合并重构为一个关于残差更新的凸二次规划（QP）问题；提出了一种基于校准集和预训练模型输出的平方输出校准目标函数；引入了一个闭式诊断指标（残差能量的分数）来预测合并效果；并为多层模型开发了一种顺序逐层合并算法。

Result: 该方法在单层设置中匹配或超过了现有方法，并在多语言和视觉基准测试中实现了性能的持续提升；同时，该框架能有效量化最优基与简化对角QP之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 模型合并可以通过凸二次规划（QP）公式化，这不仅提供了一种寻找最优合并权重的数学框架，还统一了现有的启发式方法，并通过残差能量诊断工具有效预测了合并质量。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [218] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探测器经常产生“虚假关联”，它们能解码出信息，但这些信息并非模型用于下游推理的真实因果机制。研究表明探测器识别的特征与模型执行任务的真实电路在几何上是正交的，这证明了基于探测器的可解释性方法在安全性监控上存在根本性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示线性探测（Probe）在解码模型表征时存在的局限性，特别是当探测器发现的信息与模型内部实际执行下游计算的因果路径完全脱节（即“探测到但无关”）的现象。

Method: 1. 使用线性探测器（Linear Probes）与分布式对齐搜索（DAS）对比；2. 计算探测器方向与因果子空间之间的“读出-中介角”（readout-mediator angle）；3. 通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）分解特征进行验证；4. 在多个模型规模及领域（日期计算、空间移动、符号算术）中进行复现。

Result: 1. 探测器定位的日期信息方向与模型实际计算时的 causal subspace（因果子空间）正交；2. 删除探测出的方向对模型性能无影响，删除 DAS 定位的因果子空间则导致性能坍塌；3. 模型通过特定的注意力偏移机制进行日期计算，而非直接依赖探测器捕获的日期表征。

Conclusion: 线性探测（Linear Probes）往往学习到的是与模型实际因果计算正交、无关的方向，因此它们在模型可解释性和实时安全性监控方面是不可靠的。研究强调必须使用如 DAS 等因果干预方法来定位真实电路。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [219] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用蒸馏与量化技术，基于 Apertus 8B 模型构建了兼具高效率与高性能的 Apertus-v1.1 小尺寸模型系列，以满足不同硬件与成本约束下的部署需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在广泛应用中受限于预算和硬件条件，需要通过提供不同尺寸的模型来满足多样化的计算约束。

Method: 利用蒸馏（Distillation）和量化（Quantization）技术，基于 Apertus 8B 模型开发了参数量低至 4B 的模型系列（Apertus-v1.1）。在规模为 1.7T 的许可数据集（permissive license tokens）上进行训练。

Result: 成功构建了 Apertus-v1.1 模型系列，验证了该方案在不同硬件环境下的成本效益与高精度表现。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏和量化技术，能够以低成本高效地扩展模型系列，使其适应多样的硬件配置和约束条件，同时保持较强的性能。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>
