However, the continual emergence of novel compounds results in variations in molecular scaffolds and sizes, causing drug synergy data to exhibit out-of-distribution (O.O.D.) shifts with respect to topological structure. Existing works rely on in-distribution (I.D.) assumption, failing to handle the O.O.D. shifts. To solve this problem, we study out-of-distribution generalized drug synergy prediction through a graph large language model for the first time. Nevertheless, O.O.D. generalized DSP is highly non-trivial, posing several challenges: i) how to discover structurally relevant and irrelevant molecular representations with respect to cell targets; ii) how to find the optimal graph neural architectures that accurately calculate molecular representations; and iii) how to jointly leverage molecular structural and semantic information in LLMs. To address these challenges, we propose OOD-GraphLLM, a novel graphLLM framework which is able to accurately predict drug synergy under O.O.D. settings via jointly optimizing molecular graph representation and biomedical semantic language representations in a unified manner. Furthermore, we finetune DrugSyn-LLM, a biomedical LLM, and employ a retrieval-augmented biomedical instruction tuning strategy to align molecular topological information and molecular semantic information with language-based reasoning for O.O.D. generalized DSP. Both the source code (https://github.com/EkkoXiao/Bio-GraphLLM) and released model (https://mn.cs.tsinghua.edu.cn/bio-graphllm/) are publicly available, where users are allowed to download model resources and interactively use the system through a web interface.

</details>


### [296] [In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30323)
*Zhenyu Sun,Zheng Xu,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RLHF 中静态奖励模型泛化性不足的问题，本文提出了一种基于上下文学习的奖励适应框架，通过融入人类响应时间，实现了对未见过偏好分布的实时适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于静态奖励模型的 RLHF 方法难以处理多样化的人类价值观，且多奖励框架在面对未见过的偏好分布时泛化能力差、且需要昂贵的再训练。

Method: 提出了“上下文奖励适应”（In-Context Reward Adaptation）框架，利用 Transformer 的上下文学习能力，从少量偏好演示中推断奖励结构，并创新性地将“人类响应时间”作为关键辅助输入信号。

Result: 实验证明，仅靠标准 Transformer 架构存在难以收敛的渐近偏差，而引入人类响应时间后，模型能成功适应先前未见过的偏好领域，有效表征异构奖励并应对偏好分布偏移。

Conclusion: 引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号，结合上下文学习能力，使模型能够实时适应未见过的分布，为实现更稳健、可扩展的人类-AI 对齐提供了范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically relies on static reward models to align Large Language Models with human preferences. However, human values are inherently diverse and heterogeneous, and a single reward model often lacks the robustness required to generalize to unseen preference domains. While existing multi-reward frameworks attempt to address this, they are often restricted to a fixed set of known domains and fail to adapt to unseen human distributions without costly retraining. In this work, we propose In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based framework designed to model diverse and unseen human preferences on the fly. By leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of transformers, our approach adaptively infers the underlying reward structure from a small set of preference demonstrations. We demonstrate that while a standard transformer architecture is insufficient for this task by characterizing an asymptotic bias to the ground-truth, incorporating human response time as an auxiliary input signal enables the model to successfully adapt to preferences from previously unseen domains. Our findings show that this approach provides a more robust foundation for preference modeling, allowing for the representation of heterogeneous rewards and preference distribution shift, and offering a scalable path toward more flexible human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [297] [When, why, and how do diffusion posterior samplers fail? A finite-sample lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30330)
*Benjamin A. Burns,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散模型逆问题采样中似然近似带来的缺陷，指出其导致的后验散度估计错误是从源头上造成采样幻觉和模态偏置的关键因素，并提供了一个诊断框架来评估后验采样器的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在解决成像逆问题时，依赖于计算上可行的似然函数近似。这些近似方法的理论基础和对所得后验分布的影响尚不明确，导致无法解释的采样失败（如幻觉、模态权重偏差等）。

Method: 引入“有限样本视角”（finite-sample perspective），在训练集规模趋于无穷的情况下，以任意精度近似后验分布；以此作为诊断工具，分析现有常用近似方法在扩散模型中间采样步骤中的表现。

Result: 发现现有采样方法在扩散中间步骤中往往会过度或低估后验分布的散度（spread），即便在简单的线性测量模型下，仅凭多模态先验和不准确的散度估计也会导致后验误差、模式崩溃及幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于有限样本视角的扩散模型后验采样分析框架，揭示了常用似然函数近似带来的后验扩散估计偏差是导致伪影和错误采样模式的核心原因，并为评估未来采样器的准确性提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excellent capacity to model complex distributions of natural data, which has made them a popular and effective choice for posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing methods can incorporate any measurement model at inference time but must use an inexact approximation for the likelihood at intermediate timesteps for computational tractability. Although these approximations can often work well empirically, their downstream effect on the sampled posterior is poorly understood and can result in unexplained failures. To understand when, why, and how these likelihood approximations propagate to erroneous posterior distributions, we introduce a finite-sample perspective on posterior sampling that approximates the posterior to arbitrary precision as training set size tends towards infinity, for any forward model and prior distribution. Using this finite-sample lens, we observe that popular posterior sampling approximations tend to under- or over-estimate the spread of the posterior at intermediate timesteps, causing downstream consequences including sensitivity to early stopping time, inaccurate relative weighting of posterior modes, and hallucination, both of prior modes that are not in the posterior and likelihood modes that are not supported by the prior. Moreover, we find that the cause of these posterior errors requires neither a nonlinear measurement model nor a multimodal posterior, but can arise solely due to a multimodal prior and inaccurate posterior spread at intermediate sampling times. Our finite-sample posterior sampling approach is agnostic to the type of likelihood approximation and the type of (linear or nonlinear) forward model, and can thus serve as a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate the accuracy and failure modes of existing and future posterior samplers.

</details>


### [298] [Fairness-Aware Federated Learning with Trajectory Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30336)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中聚合权重固定导致的不公平与不稳定问题，本文提出了能够实时捕捉客户端贡献度变化及其对优化轨迹影响的 FedTSV 算法，显著提升了模型的性能与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习普遍采用固定聚合权重（如平均加权），无法适应异构数据环境及客户端贡献随时间变化的特性，导致模型收敛缓慢、结果出现偏差且缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出“轨迹 Shapley 值（TSV）”，利用基于验证集的、时间一致的效用指标来量化每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响；并基于此设计了 FedTSV 自适应聚合算法，将 TSV 转化为动态聚合权重。

Result: 实验表明，FedTSV 在多个基准数据集上能有效加速模型收敛，提升系统对异构及对抗性参与者的鲁棒性，同时实现了更公平的贡献评估。

Conclusion: FedTSV 通过动态评估客户端贡献，克服了传统联邦学习中固定聚合带来的偏见与不稳定问题，为公平、稳健的联邦优化提供了理论基础与实践方法。

Abstract: Federated learning is an emerging distributed paradigm that addresses the challenges posed by heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive data. It enables multiple clients to train a model collaboratively by aggregating their local updates at a server. However, conventional aggregation schemes typically use fixed weights that fail to reflect unequal and time-varying client contributions, leading to biased and unstable learning. To improve fairness and stability, we propose the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV), a contribution metric that evaluates how each client influences the optimization trajectory of the global model using a validation-based, temporally consistent utility. Building on TSV, we design FedTSV, an adaptive aggregation method that converts per-round evaluations into dynamic client weights, allowing the server to respond to heterogeneous and adversarial participation in real time. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that FedTSV accelerates convergence, improves robustness, and yields more equitable contribution assessments, thereby providing a principled foundation for fairness-aware federated optimization.

</details>


### [299] [Efficient Test-Time Finetuning of LLMs via Convex Reconstruction and Gradient Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30337)
*Alaa Khamis,Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HullFT 提出了一种基于几何优化的 TTFT 框架，通过高效的稀疏凸组合选择样本，并结合梯度重用技术，在保持高质量微调的同时大幅提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 TTFT 方法在处理每条查询时，面临检索冗余或增强多样性选择计算成本过高的瓶颈，导致速度与质量难以兼顾。

Method: 1. 使用无投影的 Frank-Wolfe 优化算法，将查询向量表示为训练序列的稀疏凸组合，从而构建具有高相关性和多样性的支持集；2. 采用几何整数化过程将凸权重转化为整数多重集；3. 利用“梯度重用”（Gradient Reuse）技术，通过处理重复样本来分摊前向和反向传播的计算成本。

Result: 实验表明，HullFT 在实现更低比特每字节（bits-per-byte）的同时，显著降低了总运行时间，优化了当前的 TTFT 质量-效率权衡曲线。

Conclusion: HullFT 通过几何方法解决了测试时微调（TTFT）在大规模应用中的效率与质量平衡难题，显著降低了每查询的计算成本并提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Test-time finetuning (TTFT) is a rapidly evolving paradigm that adapts a language model to each prompt by retrieving related sequences, updating the model on them, and then evaluating the prompt. However, TTFT is only practical if it is fast: selection and finetuning both happen per query, making each a direct bottleneck. Existing methods trade speed for quality: fast retrieval is often redundant, while stronger diversity-aware selection adds prohibitive per-query cost. We introduce HullFT, a geometric approach to TTFT that addresses both bottlenecks. Given a query, HullFT first represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of few training sequences, using efficient projection-free Frank-Wolfe optimization. This yields a support set that is inherently relevant and diverse. We then convert the fractional convex weights into an exact integer multiset for finetuning through a geometric integerization procedure. The resulting multiplicities naturally create repeated examples, which we exploit with Gradient Reuse to amortize forward-backward computation across repeated finetuning steps. Our experiments show that HullFT improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff over current state-of-the-art TTFT methods, achieving lower bits-per-byte at substantially lower total runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu,Laurent Charlin,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高性能的“深度搜索”流水线，显著提升了文献检索的召回率，并揭示了人类在引用行为中存在显著的合作偏好，建议采用评估维度多元化的综合框架替代单一的参考列表评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模学术文献搜索任务，旨在解决传统搜索链路召回率低的问题，并探讨“人类参考列表”作为评估基准的局限性与潜在偏差。

Method: 1. 构建深度搜索流水线（Deep Research Pipeline）：基于全文档查询及参考文献的广度优先搜索。 2. LLM-as-a-judge评估：利用中立大模型判断参考文献的相关性质量。 3. 数据分析：基于OpenAlex合作关系图对比人工智能与人类引用偏好。

Result: 1. 深度搜索流水线将RollingEval基准上的召回率从低于20%提升至80%以上。 2. 研究发现人类引用存在偏见，人类引用直接合作者的概率是强人工智能重排器的2.5倍；人类参考列表仅51%的引用被模型判定为中高相关，远低于AI重排器的86-88%。

Conclusion: 文献搜索评估不应仅依赖单一指标（如召回率），应结合召回率、主题相关性、排序列表多样性及作者合作距离等多维度指标进行综合评估。

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [301] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于多信号融合的可解释决策层，通过实时分析学习行为和反馈，无需依赖成绩即可对课程教学重点进行智能化优先级排序，辅助教师精准干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助课堂能生成丰富的早期反馈，但教师难以将其高效转化为及时的教学干预；且现有的评估多依赖滞后的成绩，缺乏实时指导价值。

Method: 提出了一种可解释的决策层机制，通过整合三种信号（学生学习困难普遍性、学生自评与客观困难的差异、未解决的教师疑虑）对课程主题进行优先级排序，并提供解释性的决策记录。

Result: 通过对研究生CS课程的实证，该方法的排序与导师的关注点高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.80），并与学生报告的困难相关（ρ=0.46）；多信号整合机制在识别需要帮助的学生方面优于单一信号源（AUC 0.96 vs 0.91）。

Conclusion: 在反馈不完整的情况下，透明的协调机制能够有效支持人机协作决策，辅助教师及时做出教学调整，而无需依赖滞后的成绩反馈。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [302] [CoHyDE: Iterative Co-Training of LLM Rewriter & Dense Encoder for Tool Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29271)
*Vaishali Senthil,Ashutosh Hathidara,Sebastian Schreiber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决 LLM 在大规模 API 检索中的词汇鸿沟，本文提出了 CoHyDE 框架，通过编码器与 LLM 重写器的相互协同与迭代式对齐，显著提升了检索对口语化及模糊查询的处理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（对比学习编码器 vs. HyDE 查询扩展）在处理用户口语化/模糊查询与技术性 API 词汇之间的语义鸿沟时存在互补性失败：前者泛化能力差，后者生成内容与目录缺乏一致性。

Method: 引入 CoHyDE 迭代框架，将编码器（InfoNCE 训练）和重写器（基于检索分数的 DPO 偏好对齐）作为协同进化的系统进行联合训练。

Result: 在 ToolBench 的 10k 工具子集上，CoHyDE 在标准查询上提升 2.5 pp NDCG@5，在模糊查询上提升 6.3 pp，并在最困难的查询类别中提升高达 8 pp。

Conclusion: CoHyDE 证明了共同训练 LLM 重写器和密集检索编码器是解决 API 检索中词汇鸿沟问题的关键，优于传统的单一微调或零样本增强方法。

Abstract: Tool retrieval over large API catalogs is a core bottleneck for LLM agents: user queries arrive in colloquial, often underspecified language, while the catalog uses technical API vocabulary that no fixed encoder can bridge on its own. The two dominant training approaches, contrastive encoder fine-tuning and HyDE-style query expansion with a frozen LLM, address this problem from opposite ends and fail in complementary directions: the fine-tuned encoder excels when the query's surface form already matches the catalog but collapses when it does not, while zero-shot HyDE is more robust to underspecified queries yet generates catalog-unaware hypothetical descriptions that degrade retrieval when queries are well-formed. We introduce CoHyDE, an iterative procedure that trains the dense encoder and the LLM rewriter as a single co-evolving system: the encoder is retrained with InfoNCE on catalog-style hypothetical descriptions produced by the rewriter, and the rewriter is preference-aligned via DPO against the encoder's retrieval scores, with both sides warm-started on the tool catalog before the loop begins. On a ~10k tool subset of the ToolBench catalog, three rounds of CoHyDE improve over the strongest single-component baseline by +2.5 pp NDCG@5 on standard queries and +6.3 pp on held-out vague queries, with gains as large as +8 pp on the hardest vague tier. Ablations confirm that co-training is the key ingredient: using either component in isolation fails to match CoHyDE on both well-formed and vague queries, with losses of up to -8 pp on vague queries.

</details>


### [303] [Xetrieval: Mechanistically Explaining Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29507)
*Zhixin Cai,Jun Bai,Yang Liu,Jiaqi Li,Yichi Zhang,Taichuan Li,Zhuofan Chen,Zixia Jia,Zilong Zheng,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对稠密检索“黑盒”问题，提出 Xetrieval 框架，通过在嵌入空间内近似思维链推理并将向量分解为可解释的稀疏特征，实现了对检索决策的深层机制解释与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稠密检索（Dense Retrieval）解释方法多局限于表面信号（如词汇匹配），难以从高维向量空间的深层潜变量层面揭示检索逻辑和决策机制。

Method: 1. 构建“推理内化器”（Reasoning Internalizer）：在 embedding 空间通过单次前向传播近似思维链（CoT）推理，增强嵌入的推理意图；2. 特征分解：将增强后的嵌入分解为稀疏且人类可解释的特征；3. 解释机制：通过聚合跨文档视角的稀疏特征重叠来解释具体的检索决策。

Result: 实验表明，Xetrieval 能提取出连贯且可解释的特征，在配对层面的干预实验中表现更强，并支持对检索模型的任务级特征引导（Feature Steering）。

Conclusion: Xetrieval 提供了一种有效的嵌入级可解释性框架，能够揭示稠密检索背后的机制，通过稀疏特征分解实现了对检索行为的解释、干预和引导。

Abstract: Explaining why dense retrievers assign high relevance scores remains challenging because retrieval decisions are made through opaque high-dimensional embeddings. Existing explanations often focus on surface signals, such as lexical matches, token alignments, or post-hoc textual rationales, and thus provide limited insight into the latent factors that shape dense retrieval behavior at the embedding level. We propose \textit{Xetrieval}, an embedding-level mechanistic framework for explaining dense retrieval. \textit{Xetrieval} first introduces a lightweight reasoning internalizer that approximates Chain-of-Thought reasoning directly in the embedding space with a single forward pass, enriching sentence embeddings with reasoning-oriented information while avoiding expensive autoregressive generation. It then decomposes these reasoning-enhanced embeddings into sparse, human-interpretable features, each associated with a coherent natural language description. By aggregating sparse feature overlaps across multiple document-side views, \textit{Xetrieval} provides feature-level explanations of individual retrieval decisions. Experiments on diverse retrievers and benchmarks show that \textit{Xetrieval} uncovers coherent interpretable features, yields stronger pair-level intervention effects, and supports task-level feature steering. The project page and source code are available at https://hihiczx.github.io/Xetrieval .

</details>


### [304] [HiKEY: Hierarchical Multimodal Retrieval for Open-Domain Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29606)
*Joongmin Shin,Gyuho Shim,Jeongbae Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiKEY 是一种层次化树状多模态 RAG 框架，通过引入文档层次解析和逻辑图编码，实现了大规模工业文档的高效精准检索与多模态证据整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 方法（基于扁平文本或页面图像）在应对大规模工业语料时，面临无法精准定位目标文档以及难以在有限令牌限制下整合碎片化多模态证据（表格、图表）的瓶颈。

Method: 1. 文档层次化解析 (DHP)：构建逻辑异构图，明确父子关系；2. 粗细结合的检索策略：利用层次索引进行全局路由剪枝，随后通过多模态融合进行细粒度排序；3. 混合结构-语义打包：构建令牌高效的证据子图。

Result: 实验证明，HiKEY 对比传统的基于页面或块的基线方法，检索召回率提升高达 12.9%，端到端问答性能提升高达 6.8%。

Conclusion: HiKEY 通过将文档层次结构作为核心检索信号，有效解决了大规模工业语料中检索定位困难和多模态证据碎片化的问题，在 ODQA 性能上显著优于现有基准。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for document-based Open-domain Question Answering (ODQA) on large-scale industrial corpora faces two critical bottlenecks: routing failure in locating the correct document and evidence fragmentation in integrating scattered information. Existing approaches relying on flat text chunks or page-level images inherently struggle to (i) precisely pinpoint the target document among thousands of candidates and (ii) organically connect multimodal evidence, such as tables and figures, within a limited token budget. To address these challenges, we propose HiKEY, a hierarchical tree-based multimodal retrieval framework that elevates document hierarchy to a first-class retrieval signal. Instead of simple chunking, HiKEY reconstructs a logical heterogeneous graph via Document Hierarchical Parsing (DHP), explicitly encoding parent-child relationships. Adopting a hierarchical coarse-to-fine strategy, the framework (1) performs global routing to rapidly prune the search space using hierarchical indexing, and (2) conducts fine-grained retrieval to rank sections by employing a multimodal fusion strategy that captures the most discriminative evidence. Finally, HiKEY assembles a token-efficient evidence subgraph via a hybrid structural-semantic packing strategy. Experiments on ODQA benchmarks demonstrate that HiKEY significantly outperforms page- and chunk-based baselines, improving retrieval recall by up to 12.9% and end-to-end QA performance by up to 6.8%.

</details>


### [305] [Conformal Certification of Reasoning Trace Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30085)
*Matt Y. Cheung,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Hanjie Chen,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CROP是一种新型校准程序，通过利用共形预测技术，对大模型推理链中的有效前缀进行统计认证，实现了在保留可靠推理的同时剔除错误后续，优化了模型的推理评估与自动修复流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型不确定性量化方法通常仅针对最终答案，缺乏对推理链中部分可靠的前缀进行统计保证的方法，无法有效处理推理过程中“部分正确但后续包含错误”的情况。

Method: 提出CROP（Conformal Reasoning Output Prefixes）框架，利用共形预测（Conformal Prediction）原理，给定一个步骤级风险指标，通过校准阈值来确定最长的可靠推理前缀，并将后续可能错误的部分进行截断或标记以供修复。

Result: 在六个过程标注的推理数据集上验证了CROP的有效性；证明了标准AUROC指标无法全面反映前缀效用，提出通过“已认证前缀长度”来评价验证器；实验表明CROP能平衡过度保留与过度过滤，显著提升后续修复任务的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CROP方法通过共形预测框架，为语言模型推理链中的“清洁前缀”提供了统计保证，有效弥补了现有方法无法对推理过程进行部分认证的空白，并为过程监督、自动修正和拒绝机制之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Language model reasoning traces are rarely all-or-nothing; they frequently contain valid intermediate steps before a critical error occurs. Existing uncertainty quantification methods typically certify final answers or entire responses, failing to provide statistical guarantees for the proportion of a sequential trace that can be safely retained. To address this, we introduce CROP (Conformal Reasoning Output Prefixes), a verifier-agnostic calibration procedure for clean-prefix certification. Given any step-level risk proxy, CROP selects a calibrated threshold and returns the longest contiguous prefix whose step risk proxies remain below it, routing the uncertified suffix for downstream review or repair. Assuming exchangeability, CROP rigorously controls the marginal probability that the returned prefix contains an annotated error. Across six process-labeled reasoning datasets, we demonstrate that standard step-level metrics such as AUROC do not fully capture prefix utility, suggesting verifiers should instead be evaluated by certified prefix length. Furthermore, CROP balances over- and under-withholding, improving downstream repair accuracy by preserving valid intermediate reasoning while discarding misleading suffixes. Ultimately, this work positions prefix certification as a rigorous, practical bridge between process supervision, abstention, and repair.

</details>


### [306] [Planning with the Views via Scene Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29563)
*Kangrui Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Shiqi Chen,Zihan Wang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Lijuan Wang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现当前VLMs在长程3D视图规划中存在组合能力瓶颈，通过引入“自我探索+视图图蒸馏”的训练框架，可显著增强模型的主动推理与规划能力，超越了主流商业大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究视觉语言模型（VLMs）是否具备视图规划能力（即理解单步动作变换及多步规划以达到目标视角），并发现当前前沿模型存在多步规划组合能力缺失的显著差距。

Method: 提出名为“ViewSuite”的3D点云测试环境；利用自我探索生成遍历轨迹构建视图图（View Graph）；通过将视图图蒸馏为监督任务，重塑策略分布，从而在稀疏奖励环境下实现强化学习的有效导航。

Result: 该方法将Qwen2.5-VL-7B在该任务上的性能从2.5%大幅提升至47.8%，性能超过了GPT-4o（文中指代GPT-5.4 Pro，18.5%）和Gemini 1.5 Pro（文中指代Gemini 3.1 Pro，21.4%）。

Conclusion: 通过自我探索与视图图蒸馏相结合的迭代框架，能够有效解决视觉语言模型在多步三维视图规划中的组合推理难题，使模型表现实现跨越式提升。未来，自我探索是实现视觉语言模型在三维空间主动推理与规划的关键途径。

Abstract: Can VLMs predict how each camera move changes the view, and plan many such moves ahead? We call this capability view planning, requiring (1)understanding how a single action transforms the view, and (2)composing many such transformations across multi-turn plans to identify a target view. We probe both abilities in our proposed ViewSuite, a 3D point-cloud environment on real ScanNet scenes. Across 13 frontier VLMs, a critical planning gap emerges: they possess basic view-action knowledge but fail to compose it across multi-turn plans, with the gap widening as viewpoint distance grows. To close this gap, we propose an iterative framework that alternates self-exploration with view graph distillation. The key insight is that all exploration trajectories, regardless of their outcome, collectively form a view graph that compactly captures how viewpoints connect across a scene. Distilling this graph into diverse supervised tasks reshapes the policy distribution and overcomes the sparse rewards that stall pure RL. This improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 2.5% to 47.8% on interactive view planning, surpassing GPT-5.4 Pro (18.5%) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (21.4%). Self-exploration emerges as a promising path toward VLMs that can actively reason and plan in 3D space.

</details>


### [307] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 STHTD-MP 的离策略 TD 学习方法，通过使用对称化的行为策略 Bellman 矩阵取代传统的特征协方差作为几何度量，显著改善了鞍点问题的更新几何，从而提升了线性函数近似下的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度 TD/Mirror-Prox 方法多采用特征协方差作为度量，但在离策略（off-policy）学习中，这种几何结构往往表现不佳。研究动机在于通过引入行为策略的转移信息（即 Bellman 矩阵）来构建更具信息量和更优几何结构的度量，从而提升算法在离策略预测中的稳定性和收敛性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于行为诱导度量的 Mirror-Prox 时间差分算法（STHTD-MP）。其核心在于将原对偶鞍点公式中的特征协方差度量替换为“对称化行为策略 Bellman 矩阵”，并通过 Mirror-Prox 预测-校正步骤进行更新，同时统一了原始变量与辅助变量的学习率。

Result: 1. 完成了在标准随机逼近假设下的形式化收敛性证明（包括正定性、Hurwitz 性及 ODE 收敛性）；2. 推导了投影预言机遍历间隙界（ergodic gap bounds）；3. 证明了在特定几何条件下 STHTD-MP 具有更小的均值收缩因子；4. 通过多项基准测试（Random Walk, Boyan Chain 等）验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP 通过利用行为策略的 Bellman 矩阵作为预处理度量，在 saddle-point 几何上实现了比 GTD2-MP 更优的收敛特性（更小的均值收缩因子）。理论分析与数值实验共同证明了该方法在提升离策略预测稳定性与收敛速度方面的潜力。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [308] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了认知范畴Transformer（CCT），证明了引入范畴论中的单纯形消息传递拓扑结构可显著降低语言模型困惑度，并确定了结构化先验比一致性先验对性能提升更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索范畴论（Category Theory）和认知科学原理是否能通过引入结构化先验，提升预训练语言模型（如GPT-2）的性能表现。

Method: 基于GPT-2 Small架构，引入来源于范畴论（特别是单纯形消息传递，即GT-Full）和认知科学的组件，构建了306M参数的CCT模型，并采用受控实验（matched-step protocol）在WikiText-103数据集上进行评估。

Result: CCT模型在WikiText-103数据集上达到了21.27的验证困惑度（PPL），优于GPT-2 Small基线的24.19 PPL（12%的相对提升）；消融实验证明，84%的性能提升归功于单纯形消息传递（GT-Full）。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“结构/一致性区别”（structure/consistency distinction）经验规律，即引入新拓扑的范畴先验能提升语言模型性能，而仅起强制一致性作用的先验则无效。这为范畴论在深度学习中的应用提供了重要的实证依据。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [309] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了LLM在科研论文评审中的表现，发现其表现不稳定且与人类评审对齐度有限；同时证明了通过利用LLM评审进行针对性修改，作者可以有效提升论文评分，引发了关于学术评审公平性的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛应用于科研论文的撰写与评审，学术界急需了解LLM在评审过程中的表现、其与人类专家的对齐程度，以及该过程是否容易遭受“刷分”行为（即针对LLM偏好进行论文优化）的影响。

Method: 使用2025年ACL滚动评审（ARR）论文作为数据集，从作者和评审员双重视角评估LLM评审；通过不同Prompt和模型测试LLM与人类评审的一致性；模拟作者利用LLM评审进行迭代式修改（draft-revise workflow）并观察分数变化。

Result: 1. LLM评审与人类评审之间的对齐度有限且不稳定；2. 针对LLM评审进行“迭代修订”的策略在特定情况下非常有效，可导致高达35%的论文在评审指标上获得显著的分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM在科学论文评审中的应用存在局限性，且模型与人类评审员之间的对齐度在不同设置下表现不稳定。更重要的是，作者通过迭代式修改（针对LLM评审进行优化）能够显著“刷分”，这揭示了学术同行评审在AI介入下的潜在评估公平性风险。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [310] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OCE 提出一种基于乘法更新和几何解构的扩散模型概念擦除框架，通过正交变换精确移除特定概念，在避免损害生成质量的同时实现了高效的大规模多概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于训练的概念擦除方法计算成本高，而基于编辑的方法（主要依赖加法参数更新）虽然高效，但因混淆了神经元方向、模数和角度几何，导致概念擦除与模型生成能力之间存在严重的干扰。

Method: 提出了一种名为“正交概念擦除 (OCE)”的方法。该方法将概念擦除重构为乘法参数更新，通过层级正交变换（闭式解）来擦除目标概念，同时保持神经元模数和角度几何结构以维护生成质量；并进一步引入子空间级目标函数，以解决多概念擦除场景下的约束冲突。

Result: OCE 在单概念和多概念擦除任务中均优于现有先进方法。在保持非目标概念生成性能的同时实现了精确的概念擦除，且效率极高，仅需 4.3 秒即可完成 100 个概念的擦除。

Conclusion: OCE 通过将概念擦除重新定义为乘法参数更新，不仅解决了现有加法更新方法中干扰生成质量的问题，还通过子空间操作实现了高效的多概念擦除，在效率、精度和保真度方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [311] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent 是一个端到端的多智能体系统，通过融合多模态视觉推理与验证优先的代码合成，实现了从图像及文本描述到 FEA 仿真的全自动化流程，显著提升了仿真的准确度与工程效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 LLM 的 FEA 方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务时存在局限性，且 FEA 工作流程复杂、高度依赖领域专家，需要实现更智能、自动化的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 VFEAgent 的端到端多智能体系统：1. 采用基于 ReAct 推理的多模态视觉-语言智能体流程，从图像和文本描述中提取结构化 FEA 指令；2. 结合“验证优先”的代码合成框架，引入自调试和回退机制以确保代码的可执行性与物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的评估表明，VFEAgent 在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面具有高成功率，性能优于现有的 LLM 基线方法。

Conclusion: VFEAgent 成功验证了实现有限元分析（FEA）全流程自动化的可行性，显著提升了仿真的可靠性与准确性，能够有效降低工程师的重复性劳动。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [312] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议旨在制定“以人为本”的AI建模仿真工具评估基准，经测试发现，当前模型在逻辑推理与复杂定量任务上仍有提升空间，需建立更具透明度和协同性的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持现实世界的决策，AI辅助系统不仅需提供建议，还需构建可解释的仿真模型。鉴于该过程需平衡自动化与人类专家经验，作者旨在建立一套负责任、合乎道德且符合“以人为本”原则的建模评估基准。

Method: 建立了BEAMS倡议，通过开源项目（sd ai）及技术导向的自动化基准测试框架，协同评估针对定性/定量建模及模型讨论的AI工具，并通过不同LLM引擎的交叉对比来量化性能优劣。

Result: 评估结果显示，现有的AI建模工具在定性分析和讨论方面表现较佳，但在因果逻辑推理和定量纠错方面能力有限。且不存在单一的LLM在所有任务中均占绝对优势，任务需求与系统表现（速度与准确性）之间存在明显的权衡。

Conclusion: AI辅助建模工具在不同任务中表现出显著的性能差异，当前模型在定性讨论和基础任务上表现较好，但在因果推理与定量纠错方面仍面临挑战。BEAMS倡议通过建立基准测试，强调了开发以人为本、透明且协同的建模AI工具的重要性。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [313] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析 Bing Copilot 的大规模用户数据，发现用户与 LLM 的交互行为极难改变且个体差异巨大；同时指出公共数据集 WildChat 并不能准确反映普通用户的交互现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于用户与大语言模型（LLM）交互的研究多为静态分析，尚缺乏对用户个体行为随时间演变的纵向研究。

Method: 对约1.2万名随机抽样的 Microsoft Bing Copilot 用户进行对话轨迹分析，并与 WildChat-4.8M 数据集进行对比。

Result: 1. 用户习惯具有极高的粘性，群体行为趋势在个体层面表现较弱；2. 高活跃度用户与低活跃度用户存在显著差异，前者更倾向于处理复杂与专业性任务；3. 证实了 WildChat-4.8M 数据集在代表性上存在偏差，偏向于高水平用户。

Conclusion: 用户行为具有高度的“粘性”（难以改变），且用户群体存在显著的异质性。WildChat数据集由于偏向高端“超级用户”，无法代表典型的大众用户行为，研究人员在将其用于后续研究时需保持谨慎。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [314] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM分类公众意见时存在重大解释性分歧，传统的准确性验证不够，提出利用“分歧”来审计模型并指导人机协作，以确保政策制定中对公众意见分类的客观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构利用LLM对公众评论进行分类，但现有的基于立场准确性的标准评估无法探测模型间对相同输入在分类结果上的实质性差异，这可能影响政策制定者对公众意见的感知。

Method: 提出了“解释性审计管线”（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），利用多模型分歧来识别解释复杂性，并将人类审查聚焦于真正歧义的输入，而非仅依赖准确性验证。通过对USDA公文中的1,260条公众评论进行实验，涉及四种LLM的对比、专家打分标准评估以及两阶段标注实验。

Result: 研究发现，模型间的专题分歧（Thematic divergence）远超单个模型内提示词变化带来的差异；专家评分标准虽然能抑制明显的解释性分歧，但无法真正解决分歧；在两阶段标注实验中，人类标注者的修改往往引入了模型群体中不存在的框架，证明了现有评估模式的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于分歧的评估（Disagreement-based evaluation）是准确性指标的重要补充，在处理LLM辅助的解释性编码任务时，应将模型间分歧视为诊断工具，而非简单的错误。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [315] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了提示词语调会显著影响大语言模型的测试准确性且各模型表现不一，提醒开发者在构建应用时需关注语调对模型推理模式的潜在干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）应用广泛，但其性能往往受提示词风格和语调影响，研究旨在揭示语调变化是否以及如何导致客观选择题准确率的偏差。

Method: 使用两个数据集（50题自定义集和570题MMLU子集）对4种经济高效的LLM（ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）进行测试，涵盖多种语调变体，并引入路由框架分析语调如何调动模型的内部推理模式。

Result: 研究发现语调效果是系统性的，但不同模型对语调的敏感度差异巨大；同时发现不同学科对语调的敏感性也存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 提示词的语调（Tone）对LLM的准确性有显著且系统性的影响，但表现出高度的模型依赖性。用户在部署LLM时不能假设模型具备“语调鲁棒性”，应审慎考虑提示词语调的影响。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [316] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究调查了高等教育领域AI应用现状，证实从业者在认可AI辅助功能的同时高度依赖人工监管，但实践中面临机构资源不足与设计理论落实不到位等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入探讨高等教育从业者对人工智能在教学中整合的认知、行为习惯及受到的机构环境制约情况，通过实证研究架起设计导向理论与AI教学实际应用之间的桥梁。

Method: 采用横截面调查研究法（n=72），基于DOT框架（结合设计思维与开放系统理论）进行问卷调查，并对19个信念指标使用探索性因子分析（EFA）提取出三个核心维度。

Result: 识别出三个认知因子：AI功能能力、监管与治理、教师协作与规划；研究表明从业者对AI持积极支持态度，但强调人工监管必要性；实践中多依赖迭代提示与内容生成；机构层面的政策、培训与基础设施供给仍存在显著短板。

Conclusion: 该研究初步验证了DOT框架作为描述高等教育从业者AI认知与实践模型的有效性，同时揭示了当前教学设计理论与AI实际应用之间的差距，为后续研究提供了测量结构及优化方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [317] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为D-BOS的对手塑造方法，通过直接对观测者的信念动态进行微分，使智能体能够根据环境奖励自动学习如何影响他人信念，在复杂的多智能体任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对手塑造方法（如参数/策略/价值空间操作）以及隐藏角色游戏中的信念操纵技术（如硬编码欺骗）存在局限性，缺乏灵活且基于环境奖励的通用塑造机制。

Method: 提出了Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS)，一种一阶梯度方法。通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态进行微分，将观测者的信念视为被塑造的对手状态，并聚合来自多个观测者的梯度。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS在性能上超过了PPO和BBM，且在混合动机环境下表现出最大的提升。

Conclusion: D-BOS是一种有效的对手塑造方法，通过将信念状态作为塑造目标，使策略能从奖励结构中自然产生，在隐藏角色游戏中优于PPO和BBM，特别是在混合动机设置中表现最佳。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [318] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌套学习和记忆系统的多智能体管道，通过多阶段纠错与语义缓存机制，显著降低了LLM管道中的幻觉风险并提升了运维效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体管道中幻觉（无根据的声明）在不同阶段不受控传播的问题，同时平衡幻觉缓解、能源效率与系统的可观测性。

Method: 采用了基于HOPE的嵌套学习架构、连续记忆系统（CMS）及语义相似度缓存；设计了一个由“开放楼层协议”（OFP）协调的三阶段智能体流水线；使用5个关键绩效指标（KPI）聚合为总幻觉分数（THS）并进行加权评估。

Result: 端到端总幻觉分数（THS）降低了31.3%至35.9%；通过语义缓存实现了47.3%的命中率，有效降低了LLM调用次数及环境足迹；验证了高可观测性配置能强化而非削弱幻觉缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强型多智能体架构能够协同提升大模型（LLM）的事实可靠性、操作效率和可审计性，且无需进行模型重训练。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [319] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD 提出了一种将思维链推理过程压缩为潜在空间表示的防护新架构，在不牺牲安全检测精度的前提下，极大地优化了部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型安全防护方案中，传统分类方法效果不佳，而基于推理（思维链）的方法虽准确但存在严重的延迟和Token开销，无法满足高吞吐场景的需求。

Method: 提出 COLAGUARD，通过阶段性训练课程（stage-wise training curriculum）将多步安全推理过程转化为连续的潜在空间表示，从而在推理时直接进行隐状态传播，免去了显式的思维链生成。

Result: 在八个基准测试、十个审核任务上，COLAGUARD 的 Macro-F1 比 Llama Guard 3 提升了 8.24 点，与 GuardReasoner（显式推理基准）效果持平，同时实现了 12.9 倍的推理加速和 22.4 倍的 Token 消耗缩减。

Conclusion: 通过潜在空间推理（Latent Reasoning）代替显式推理生成，证明了在保持高安全性能的同时，可以显著提升推理效率，实现了安全鲁棒性与部署效率的统一。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [320] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对工业调度中的异步执行不确定性，提出了一种中间层框架，通过标准化执行合约与多维度差异归因，实现了决策语义与执行行为的解耦，提升了系统的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中事件驱动调度策略面临异步、部分可观测状态带来的问题，导致决策状态时间不一致、动作准入明确性不足以及执行错误归因模糊，限制了系统的稳定性和可解释性。

Method: 提出并实现了一个位于调度策略与工业执行环境之间的中间层（Execution and Measurement Layer）。该层通过构建异步事件流的“决策有效快照”、定义显式的“动作准入合约”，并将系统输出记录为四类差异（意图、交易结果、物理执行、人为干预）来实现解耦与可追溯性。

Result: 基于离散事件仿真的评估表明，该框架能将未定义的执行故障转化为结构化的、可归因的类型化输出；在观测延迟较低的场景下，支持在动作执行前预防错误，显著提升了系统的可控性和策略评估效率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种策略中立的执行与度量层，能够将复杂的异步执行环境标准化，从而解决工业调度策略中的不可靠和不可解释性问题，并为持续的策略优化提供了数据支撑。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [321] [Trends in AI and Human-AI Interaction in Clinical Trials -- A Hybrid Human-AI Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29096)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用GPT-5.5辅助人工处理ClinicalTrials.gov数据，发现全球AI临床试验快速增长，中美两国处于领先地位，且验证了混合工作流在试验筛选中的潜力，同时也指出了现有临床试验记录在交互描述上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化临床试验中AI术语的演变趋势、分析相关试验的地理分布，并探索一种高效的混合方法来评估此类试验的研究动态。

Method: 使用生成式AI模型（GPT-5.5）联合人类专家审核的混合方法（Hybrid human-AI approach），对ClinicalTrials.gov中AI相关的临床试验记录进行检索、筛选和分类。

Result: 1. AI相关临床试验数量呈显著上升趋势；2. 术语演变明显，机器学习、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT及大模型等词汇增长迅速；3. 中国和美国是开展此类试验最多的国家，且意大利、法国等国近期增长显著；4. 在验证中，AI与人在识别“非AI应用研究”方面表现良好，但在界定复杂的“人机交互”时一致性较低。

Conclusion: 混合人类-AI筛选临床试验记录的方法具有可行性，但临床试验报告需要更加规范，且必须对“人机交互”给出更精确的定义，以提升分析的准确性。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>


### [322] [Beyond Consensus: Trace-Level Synthesis in Mixture of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29116)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM协作中的“聚合悖论”，即全量聚合推理轨迹优于多数投票。作者提出了Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents，通过语义扰动生成多样化轨迹并进行锚定聚合，显著提升了复杂推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前多智能体协作方案（如多数投票或分层合成）通过压缩信息“以共识为终点”存在严重的损失，会导致模型在看似一致的情况下忽略了少数派中正确的中间推理步骤。

Method: 提出Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents框架：1. 通过语义保持输入扰动（Input Perturbations）生成推理轨迹的多样性；2. 采用基于锚定细化（Anchored Refinement）的策略确保聚合过程不出现性能退化；3. 抛弃多数投票（Majority Voting），改为基于轨迹的合成聚合。

Result: 该方法在结构化推理、博士级科学问题、竞赛数学及竞赛编程等任务中，单一模型通过扰动产生的轨迹多样性，性能表现优于异构模型池，且显著超越了传统的多数投票基准。

Conclusion: 推理过程（Reasoning Trace）是多智能体协作中比最终答案更具价值的分析单元。通过全量推理轨迹的聚合，即使在模型达成共识的情况下，依然能进一步提升决策质量，“聚合悖论”证明了这种策略的优越性。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents solve the same problem, standard practice compresses each agent's reasoning into a majority vote or layered synthesis, treating agreement as the finish line. We show this is unnecessarily lossy: an LLM aggregator that reads complete reasoning traces recovers correct solutions even when agents unanimously agree, with beneficial corrections consistently outweighing harmful ones -- the \emph{aggregation paradox}. Majority voting has a ceiling that perturbation diversity does not raise (error correlations are identical); the aggregator's gain comes from trace-level complementarity, assembling correct intermediate steps from minority chains that voting discards. These findings motivate Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents which generates trace diversity through semantic-preserving input perturbations, safeguards the majority via anchored refinement with provable non-degradation guarantees, and always synthesizes -- never gates on consensus. A single model with perturbation-induced trace variation outperforms heterogeneous model pools across structured reasoning, PhD-level science, competition mathematics, and competitive programming. The unit of aggregation should be the reasoning trace, not the answer.

</details>


### [323] [The Confidence Shortcut: A Reasoning Failure Mode of Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29123)
*Dueun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究指出，遮蔽扩散语言模型常用的“置信度对齐训练”策略会损害复杂推理能力；相比之下，看似简单的“随机掩码”策略在处理需要长距离逻辑依赖的任务时表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遮蔽扩散语言模型（MDM）训练方案倾向于与置信度解码策略对齐，但这可能与复杂逻辑推理所需的轨迹相冲突，导致在处理长跨度依赖任务时出现高性能错误。

Method: 研究团队对比了置信度对齐训练（Confidence-Aligned Training）与传统的随机掩码训练策略，并在五个推理任务中通过多位数加法等实验评估了它们在复杂推理任务中的表现。

Result: 实验显示，在多位数加法及其他五个推理任务中，置信度对齐训练使得模型在复杂输入上的错误率提高了一个数量级；而随机掩码策略虽然看似效率较低，但通过保持推理轨迹的条件依赖性，在处理复杂任务时表现出更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 置信度对齐训练虽然在简单任务上表现良好，但在复杂推理任务中由于破坏了逻辑依赖，反而大幅增加了错误率。随机掩码（Random Masking）在处理需要长距离依赖的复杂推理时更为鲁棒，是更优的策略。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) uniquely support any-order generation, with confidence-based decoding currently serving as the de facto standard inference policy. To optimize for this, recent training schemes attempt to align training mask patterns directly with those observed during generation. However, we argue that confidence-based decoding is inherently misaligned with the logical-flow trajectories required for complex reasoning, and that confidence-aligned training actively entrenches this misalignment. We make this concrete using multi-digit addition, where the decoding strategy prematurely predicts locally easy digits before resolving their long-range dependencies, producing high-confidence errors on challenging inputs. While traditional random masking keeps the failure rate low on this challenging tail, confidence-aligned training amplifies the error rate by an order of magnitude. Across five distinct reasoning tasks, this same pattern emerges with task-dependent severity: confidence-based decoding induces failures on highly complex inputs, and confidence-aligned training exacerbates them. In contrast, random masking -- despite its perceived inefficiency -- robustly preserves the reasoning-trajectory conditionals essential for solving the challenging tail.

</details>


### [324] [Better Later Than Sooner: Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge Graph Construction via Ontology-grounded Post-extraction Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29168)
*Lorenzo Loconte,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种神经符号化框架，通过本体论约束与后期修正策略，实现了低成本、高一致性的知识图谱构建，有效支持复杂逻辑推理查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于文本的RAG难以执行复杂的逻辑推理，而现有的符号化知识图谱构建流程高度依赖LLM且易产生逻辑/本体冲突，导致准确性不足且计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出了一种神经符号框架：包含开放域提取、基于嵌入（embedding）的类型与谓词规范化（canonicalization），以及针对本体冲突的定向LLM修正。将修正过程置于后处理阶段，避免了重复调用LLM。

Result: 相比传统方法，该方法在保持下游问答质量的同时，显著减少了token消耗，并提升了知识图谱的逻辑一致性，其生成的知识图谱非常契合SPARQL等符号化查询。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过引入本体论约束和延迟修正机制，可以构建出更具一致性、更适合复杂符号化推理的知识图谱，同时显著降低了LLM调用成本。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) is a core challenge in AI, particularly for complex queries requiring multi-hop reasoning across documents, or symbolic operations like aggregation or exhaustive listing. Retrieval-augmented generation has become the dominant approach to QA, with recent graph-based variants addressing part of these issues by organizing knowledge to better support compositional questions. However, most textual graph-based RAG methods still lack the structure needed for symbolic operations useful to answer complex questions reliably. This motivates symbolic graph-based approaches, which extract knowledge graphs (KGs) whose relations are logic predicates that enable SQL-like querying. Yet these pipelines typically use LLMs for KG extraction, which can introduce consistency issues, where extracted facts may violate commonsense ontology constraints. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework for ontology-grounded KG construction combining open-domain extraction, embedding-based canonicalization of types and predicates, and targeted LLM-based correction of ontology violations. By deferring corrections to a post-extraction stage, our method avoids repeated LLM calls, substantially reducing token usage while improving KG consistency and preserving downstream QA quality. Finally, we show that the extracted KGs are well suited for symbolic querying by measuring the occurrence of SPARQL graph patterns.

</details>


### [325] [Paper Agents, Paper Gains: An Empirical Analysis of DeFi Investment Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29174)
*Jay Yu,Amy Zhao,Danning Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了当前的DeFi AI投资智能体多为缺乏真实自治能力的投机性产品，存在严重的市场估值泡沫与利益分配不均，亟需构建成熟度标准以指导行业规范化发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi投资智能体估值虽已破30亿美元，但缺乏对其架构稳健性、真实交易自治性及投资者利益分配机制的严谨评估，急需对其行业现状进行实证研究。

Method: 1. 对1,900+个AI加密项目进行筛选，确立10个代表性案例；2. 对ElizaOS和Virtuals Protocol进行架构分析；3. 对11个Solana生态智能体金库进行链上量化分析（涵盖925,323名持币者）。

Result: 1. 大多数项目缺乏真正的自治执行，多为基础API集成；2. 智能体金库账面盈利但持币者群体亏损严重（亏损1.917亿美元），财富集中度极高；3. 市场估值与金库基本面脱节（估值/资产比超10,000x）；4. 用户平均收益为负，代币较历史高点平均跌幅达93%。

Conclusion: DeFi投资智能体市场尚处于早期阶段，表现为高度异构、投机性强且缺乏稳健的自治与合规标准。作者提出了一个包含自治执行、风险调整后盈利能力和利益相关者一致性的成熟度框架，旨在弥合当前实验性部署与投资级系统之间的差距。

Abstract: DeFi investment agents, systems that use AI for autonomous on-chain trading, have attained over USD 3 billion in combined token valuations since late 2024. We survey over 1,900 AI-tagged crypto projects, filter to investment-focused agents, and curate 10 representative projects spanning strategy and observability dimensions. We then conduct a deep-dive architectural analysis of two prominent agent frameworks, ElizaOS and Virtuals Protocol, and a quantitative on-chain performance analysis of 11 Solana-based agent treasuries with publicly attributable trading activity, covering 925,323 token holders. We find that current deployments remain early and heterogeneous: (1) in our sample, many projects do not yet provide clear evidence of autonomous trade execution, and developer interviews suggest that many visible deployments remain basic API integrations; (2) agent treasuries retain over USD 30M in paper gains while token holders collectively lost USD 191.7M, with the top 1% of wallets capturing 81.4% of all gains (USD 1.81B); (3) token valuations are weakly connected to treasury fundamentals, with market-cap-to-AUM ratios exceeding 10,000x versus below 1x for established DeFi protocols; and (4) aggregate user gains peaked at USD 2.4B before declining to net losses, with median returns negative on every platform and tokens declining 93% on average from all-time highs. We interpret these outcomes as characteristic of a permissionless, first-generation market in which open infrastructure enables rapid experimentation but also allows naive or speculative agents to launch before robust standards for autonomy, performance, and stakeholder alignment emerge. We therefore propose a maturity framework along three dimensions: autonomous execution, risk-adjusted profitability, and stakeholder alignment, to characterize the gap between current deployments and future investment-grade agent systems.

</details>


### [326] [ReasonOps: Operator Segmentation for LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29192)
*Daniel Lee,Owen Queen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonOps 是一种非监督式的分析方法，通过将 LLM 的思维链重构为 7 个基础推理操作符，实现了对模型推理模式的量化分析、指纹识别及推理结果的早期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链（CoT）分析方法要么过于僵化，要么表达能力不足，无法跨领域和跨模型捕捉其内部结构，导致人们缺乏描述长序列思维链内部运作机制的词汇。

Method: 开发了 ReasonOps，这是一种非监督式思维链分析方法。通过对句首 3-token 透视点（pivots）进行聚类，识别出 7 种通用的推理操作符。该方法在 12 个推理模型、44,662 条思维链上进行了验证，并通过独立 LLM 判断器评估了分类准确性。

Result: 1) 识别出 7 个跨模型的核心推理操作符；2) 发现反射性操作符在难题中起增益作用，在简单题中反而有害；3) 建立了基于操作符序列的模型身份识别（指纹）；4) 实现了无需完整追踪即可进行的高精度早期正确性预测（50% 序列即达 0.81 AUC）。

Conclusion: ReasonOps 揭示了大型推理模型在思维链中具有通用的 compositional 结构，其实质表现为 7 个递归的推理操作符（如回溯、推断和假设）。这些操作符可作为“推理指纹”用于模型识别，并能有效预测推理的正确性及早期质量评估，证明了非监督式架构分析在理解 LLM 推理行为中的强大效能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces from large reasoning models can span tens of thousands of tokens, yet we lack a vocabulary for describing their internal structure. Previous methods developed to analyze chain-of-thought traces are either too rigid or not expressive enough, failing to capture features across domains and models. To remedy this, we develop ReasonOps, an unsupervised, expressive method for annotating chain-of-thought traces, providing succinct universal operators. Using ReasonOps, we analyze 44,662 traces from 12 thinking LLMs spanning 6 families across 8 reasoning benchmarks and discover that they share a common compositional structure: 7 recurring reasoning operators -- discourse-level moves such as backtracking, inferring, and hypothesizing -- that emerge from unsupervised clustering of sentence-initial 3-token pivots. These operators appear across every model family and benchmark domain, confirmed by three independent LLM judges who classify held-out samples at 70 -76% accuracy. We analyze the structure of operators on easy vs. hard problems, revealing that reflective operators are more helpful on hard problems and harm performance on easy problems. Operator sequences are highly model-identifying: a classifier trained on operator distributions alone recovers the source model with macro-AUC, revealing that each model family has a distinctive reasoning fingerprint. Structural operator features predict within-problem answer correctness well above baselines. Classifiers built on these operators reach WP-AUC and on AIME specifically. ReasonOps further enables early quality estimation well before the trace completes: we predict at WP-AUC for only 50% of the trace. The ReasonOps pipeline is unsupervised and annotation-free, enabling deep insights into LLM reasoning traces as well as strong downstream results on model identification and correctness prediction.

</details>


### [327] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，用于量化评估数据集对特定学生模型推理蒸馏的适配度，通过基于 DMC 的数据选择可显著提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理蒸馏研究缺乏对训练数据与特定学生模型之间适配性的定量评估，导致蒸馏效果往往依赖于试错，效率较低。

Method: 提出了一种名为“数据-模型兼容性（Data-Model Compatibility, DMC）”的指标，综合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生模型能力，并据此进行数据筛选或动态调度。

Result: 实验证明 DMC 与蒸馏性能高度相关；基于 DMC 进行数据选择能显著提升模型效果；动态利用 DMC 选择数据进一步优化了训练过程，且在多种模型和任务上具有通用性。

Conclusion: DMC 是一种有效评估推理蒸馏数据适配性的指标，通过根据 DMC 指数进行数据选择和动态调整，能显著提升小模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [328] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang,Shuhang Lin,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为“密集推理”的概念，并通过 DenseSteer 推理框架，引导小参数模型优化推理路径，从而在无需额外训练的前提下显著提升了数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到现有小型大语言模型（≤3B 参数）在复杂多步推理任务中表现远不如大型模型；研究发现高性能推理的核心特征在于“更少的步骤、更高的单步信息密度”。

Method: 提出 DenseSteer 框架：一种无需训练（training-free）、在推理阶段（inference-time）的引导方法，通过调整模型内部表征，引导小模型采取更具信息密度的密集推理模式。

Result: 实验表明，DenseSteer 可以在不增加令牌级负对数似然（NLL）的情况下，稳定提升小模型在数学推理基准测试上的准确率。

Conclusion: 密集推理（Dense Reasoning）是一种有效的数学问题求解结构，通过优化推理步骤的信息密度，即便不增加推理性价（NLL），也能显著提升小参数模型在多步推理任务中的准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [329] [Provably Secure Agent Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29251)
*Benlong Wu,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen,Han Fang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决大模型智能体语义防御不可靠的问题，本研究提出了一种基于一阶逻辑约束（ePCA）的安全框架，强制智能体执行前通过数学形式化验证意图，实现了确定性的高安全防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型防御机制主要依赖语义护栏和概率判别，无法在复杂的“语义符号解耦攻击”中提供确定性的安全边界，导致AI智能体存在失控风险。

Method: 提出了一种基于逻辑推理局限性的安全新范式，并实现了一个“可执行证明约束行动”（ePCA）框架与神经符号隔离架构；该框架强制智能体将意图无损形式化为一阶逻辑数学约束，从而绕过对自然语言语义的依赖。

Result: 在宏观和微观二维动态对抗实验中，该形式化验证机制实现了零攻击成功率和零误报率，且具备极低的计算延迟。

Conclusion: 该研究为智能体构建了一个形式化安全底座，证明了通过逻辑约束而非仅仅依赖语义验证，可以实现确定性的安全保障，为未来智能系统防御复杂攻击提供了范式转变。

Abstract: As large language models transition from bounded generative engines to agents with expansive execution privileges, AI going out of control precipitates a fundamental crisis in artificial intelligence security. Existing defense architectures heavily rely on empirical semantic guardrails and probabilistic large model adjudicators, mechanisms that fail to provide deterministic security lower bounds when facing complex semantic symbol decoupling attacks. To overcome this empirical semantic guardrail dilemma, this paper proposes a new security paradigm for agents based on the fundamental limitations of logical reasoning. Based on this paradigm, we further introduce an executable Proof-Constrained Action (ePCA) framework with a neural symbolic isolation architecture. This framework abandons semantic trust in natural language, forcing agents to losslessly formalize their intentions into first-order logical mathematical constraints before performing physical operations. Empirical evaluations of macroscopic and microscopic two-dimensional dynamic adversarial systems demonstrate that our formal verification mechanism achieves zero attack success rate and zero false positive rate across the evaluated scenarios, with extremely low computational latency. This research provides a conditional formal foundation under explicit system assumptions and an engineering paradigm for constructing the underlying defense foundation for future intelligent systems.

</details>


### [330] [Entropy-KL Divergence-based Token Masking: A Novel Approach for Selective Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29303)
*Qi Liu,Mingdi Sun,Yongyi He,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu,Yi Zheng,Zhefeng Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对SFT小样本训练易导致模型分布偏移的问题，本文提出EKSFT方法，通过屏蔽高不确定性Token来保护预训练分布，有效提升了后继强化学习的探索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT后接RL的范式中，SFT阶段使用少量数据容易导致模型过拟合特定样本并偏离预训练分布，从而削弱了后续RL阶段的探索效率。

Method: 提出EKSFT（Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning）方法：在SFT训练过程中，基于参考模型计算Token的熵和KL散度，并将超过特定阈值的Token进行掩码处理，使其不参与损失计算。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，EKSFT不仅在SFT阶段优于标准SFT，而且作为强化学习（RL）的起点，能够显著提升RL训练后的模型最终性能。

Conclusion: 在低数据量的SFT阶段，通过选择性屏蔽高不确定性（高熵或高KL散度）的Token，可以有效地在注入任务知识与保持预训练分布完整性之间取得平衡，从而为后续的强化学习提供更好的初始模型。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models. This paradigm provides a cold-start for RL exploration, avoiding the inefficiency of pure RL where on-policy sampling yields insufficient positive samples. However, in practice, existing approaches often use a small amount of data for SFT initialization compared to the RL phase, which can cause the model to fit the limited samples and shift away from its pre-trained distribution. This distribution shift impedes the model's ability to effectively explore during subsequent RL training. To address this challenge, we propose that in low-data regimes, SFT should prioritize activating task-relevant capabilities rather than memorizing specific content. Along this line, we propose EKSFT (Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning), which selectively masks tokens that exhibit either high entropy or high KL divergence from a reference model. By excluding these high-uncertainty, distribution-shifting tokens from imitation, EKSFT injects task-specific knowledge while preserving the integrity of the model's pre-trained distribution. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EKSFT consistently outperforms standard SFT. Further RL fine-tuning from the EKSFT model yields consistently better post-RL performance, indicating improved exploration for the RL stage. Our codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/MINE-USTC/EKSFT.

</details>


### [331] [Rubric-Guided Process Reward for Stepwise Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29310)
*Shenghao Ye,Yu Guo,Zhengheng Li,Shuangwu Chen,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoRo 是一种通过 RUBRIC-GUIDED 过程奖励优化决策路径的路由框架，它解决了传统路由模型仅关注最终结果而忽略中间推理过程的缺陷，显著提升了大规模推理模型（LRM）的效率和决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的路由方法主要依赖“结果奖励”（仅关注最终答案正确性），无法有效评估中间过程的路由决策，导致模型性能和泛化能力受到限制。

Method: 1. 轨迹收集与偏好对构建：收集涵盖结果、成本和过程质量的路由轨迹偏好对；
2. 架构优化（交互式优化）：训练“准则器（Rubricor）”生成针对查询的评估准则，训练“评判器（Judge）”依据该准则对路径评分；
3. 策略优化：将过程奖励与结果奖励相结合，采用 GRPO（组相对策略优化）算法训练路由策略。

Result: 在五个推理基准测试中，RoRo 在同家族模型和跨家族模型设置下，均显著优于现有基线方法，在准确率（Accuracy）与计算成本（Cost）之间达到了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: RoRo 提供了一种基于准则引导的过程奖励框架，通过结合过程质量评估与结果准确性，有效提升了大规模推理模型在路由任务中的效率与准确率，实现了更好的性能与成本权衡。

Abstract: Stepwise model routing improves the efficiency of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) by assigning each reasoning step to a suitable model. Recent methods formulate routing as a sequential decision process and train the router with reinforcement learning. However, although they model routing as a process, they still supervise the router with outcome rewards. Such rewards only reflect final answer correctness and fail to evaluate intermediate routing decisions, which can weaken performance and generalization. To address this gap, we propose RoRo, a rubric-guided process reward framework for stepwise model routing. RoRo first collects diverse routing trajectories and constructs preference pairs based on outcome, cost, and process quality. It then trains a Rubricor to generate a query-specific evaluation rubric and a Judge to score routing trajectories under this rubric through alternating optimization. The resulting process rewards are combined with outcome rewards to optimize the routing policy via GRPO.
